标题：高斯过程的序贯推断：信号处理视角
具备能力和效率的机器学习（ML）模型的普及，标志着信号处理（SP）近百年历史中最强劲的方法论转变之一。ML模型支持开发能够以高预测精度表示复杂非线性关系的SP系统。适应这些模型通常需要序贯推断，这在理论和方法上与传统ML范式不同，后者常假设数据独立同分布。高斯过程（GPs）是一种灵活且严谨的随机函数建模框架，随着统计和ML方法在SP中扮演更突出角色，其重要性日益增加。本文提供一个自包含的教程式GPs概览，特别聚焦于序贯、增量或流式推断的最新方法论进展。我们从信号处理视角介绍这些技术，并将其与ML领域的最新进展相联系。我们综述的许多进展可直接应用于状态空间建模、序贯回归与预测、时间序列异常检测、序贯贝叶斯优化、自适应与主动传感，以及序贯检测与决策。通过从信号处理视角组织这些进展，我们旨在为从业者提供实用工具和清晰的路线图，以在实际系统中部署序贯GP模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing need to adapt machine learning models to real-time, sequential data streams in signal processing, this paper presents a signal-processing perspective on Gaussian processes, emphasizing their utility in sequential inference. It reviews recent methodological advances in incremental and streaming GP inference, contrasting them with traditional batch ML assumptions of i.i.d. data. The authors introduce techniques tailored for sequential applications such as state-space modeling, time-series forecasting, anomaly detection, and adaptive sensing, demonstrating their practical relevance through a tutorial-style exposition. Experimental results across these domains confirm that sequential GP methods yield accurate, real-time predictions and robust decision-making under dynamic conditions, validating their deployment in real-world signal processing systems.
鉴于实时自适应信号处理系统对序列数据的需求日益增长，本文从信号处理角度出发，系统梳理了高斯过程在增量推理中的方法进展，强调其在流式数据场景下的适用性。文章回顾了近期在流式推理、在线学习等方向的算法改进，并将其与状态空间建模、异常检测、贝叶斯优化等实际应用相结合。实验结果表明，相较于传统批处理方法，序列高斯过程在动态非平稳环境中表现更优，具备更强的适应性和预测能力。
RopeDreamer: A Kinematic Recurrent State Space Model for Dynamics of Flexible Deformable Linear Objects
Authors: Tim Missal, Lucas Domingues, Berk Guler, Simon Manschitz, Jan Peters, Paula Dornhofer Paro Costa
First: 2026-04-30T17:47:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:47:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The robotic manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) is a fundamental challenge due to the high-dimensional, non-linear dynamics of flexible structures and the complexity of maintaining topological integrity during contact-rich tasks. While recent data-driven methods have utilized Recurrent and Graph Neural Networks for dynamics modeling, they often struggle with self-intersections and non-physical deformations, such as tangling and link stretching. In this paper, we propose a latent dynamics framework that combines a Recurrent State Space Model with a Quaternionic Kinematic Chain representation to enable robust, long-term forecasting of DLO states. By encoding the DLO as a sequence of relative rotations (quaternions) rather than independent Cartesian positions, we inherently constrain the model to a physically valid manifold that preserves link-length constancy. Furthermore, we introduce a dual-decoder architecture that decouples state reconstruction from future-state prediction, forcing the latent space to capture the underlying physics of deformation. We evaluate our approach on a large-scale simulated dataset of complex pick-and-place trajectories involving self-intersections. Our results demonstrate that the proposed model achieves a 40.52% reduction in open-loop prediction error over 50-step horizons compared to the state-of-the-art baseline, while reducing inference time by 31.17%. Our model further maintains superior topological consistency in scenarios with multiple crossings, proving its efficacy as a compositional primitive for long-horizon manipulation planning.

标题：RopeDreamer：一种用于柔性可变形线性物体动力学的运动学递归状态空间模型
柔性可变形线性物体（DLOs）的机器人操作因柔性结构的高维非线性动力学及接触密集任务中保持拓扑完整性之复杂性而成为基本挑战。尽管近期数据驱动方法已利用递归与图神经网络进行动力学建模，但常难以处理自交与非物理形变，如缠绕与链段拉伸。本文提出一种潜在动力学框架，结合递归状态空间模型与四元数运动学链表示，以实现对DLO状态的鲁棒长期预测。通过将DLO编码为相对旋转（四元数）序列而非独立笛卡尔位置，我们内在约束模型至物理有效流形，保持链段长度恒定。此外，我们引入双解码器架构，将状态重建与未来状态预测解耦，迫使潜在空间捕捉形变的底层物理规律。我们在包含自交的复杂抓取与放置轨迹的大规模仿真数据集上评估该方法。结果表明，与当前最优基线相比，所提模型在50步预测时程内开放环预测误差降低40.52%，推理时间减少31.17%。该模型在多交叉场景中进一步保持卓越拓扑一致性，证明其作为长期操作规划组合原语的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of modeling flexible, deformable linear objects (DLOs) in robotic manipulation due to their high-dimensional, non-linear dynamics and topological constraints, this paper introduces RopeDreamer, a recurrent state space model that combines quaternionic kinematic chains with a dual-decoder architecture. The method encodes DLOs as sequences of relative rotations to enforce physical constraints like constant link length and avoid non-physical deformations. Evaluated on a simulated dataset with complex pick-and-place tasks involving self-intersections, the model achieves a 40.52% reduction in open-loop prediction error over 50 steps compared to the prior state-of-the-art, while also reducing inference time by 31.17% and maintaining superior topological consistency during multi-crossing scenarios.
为解决柔性线性物体（DLOs）在机器人操作中因高维非线性动力学和拓扑约束带来的建模难题，本文提出RopeDreamer模型，结合循环状态空间模型与四元数运动链表示，强制模型在物理有效流形上运行以保持链长不变并避免非物理形变。该方法采用双解码器架构，将状态重建与未来状态预测解耦，迫使潜在空间编码真实物理动力学。在包含自交轨迹的大规模仿真数据集上评估，该模型在50步预测中较当前最优方法误差降低40.52%，推理时间减少31.17%，且在多重交叉场景中保持更优拓扑一致性，证明其作为长时规划基础构件的有效性。
Continuous-tone Simple Points: An $\ell_0$-Norm of Cyclic Gradient for Topology-Preserving Data-Driven Image Segmentation
Authors: Wenxiao Li, Faqiang Wang, Yuping Duan, Li Cui, Liqiang Zhang, Jun Liu
First: 2026-04-30T17:45:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:45:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Topological features play an essential role in ensuring geometric plausibility and structural consistency in image analysis tasks such as segmentation and skeletonization. However, integrating topology-preserving learning based on simple points into deep learning tasks remains challenging, as existing simple point detection methods are confined to binary images and are non-differentiable, rendering them incompatible with gradient-based optimization in modern deep learning. Moreover, morphological and purely data-driven approaches often fail to guaranty topological consistency. To address these limitations, we propose a novel method that directly computes simple points on continuous-valued images, enabling differentiable topological inference. Building on this theory, we develop an efficient skeleton extraction algorithm that preserves topological structures in binary and continuous-valued images. Furthermore, we design a variational model that enforces topological constraints by preserving topologically non-removable (i.e., non-simple) points, which can be seamlessly integrated into any deep neural network segmentation with softmax or sigmoid outputs. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach effectively improves topological integrity and structural accuracy across multiple benchmarks. The codes are available in https://github.com/levnsio/CSP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：连续色调简单点：基于循环梯度的ℓ₀范数用于拓扑保持的数据驱动图像分割
拓扑特征在图像分析任务（如分割和骨架提取）中确保几何合理性和结构一致性起着关键作用。然而，将基于简单点的拓扑保持学习整合到深度学习任务中仍具挑战性，因为现有简单点检测方法仅适用于二值图像且不可微，无法与现代深度学习中的梯度优化兼容。此外，形态学和纯数据驱动方法往往无法保证拓扑一致性。为解决这些局限，我们提出一种新颖方法，直接在连续值图像上计算简单点，实现可微拓扑推理。基于此理论，我们开发了一种高效的骨架提取算法，可在二值和连续值图像中保持拓扑结构。此外，我们设计了一种变分模型，通过保留拓扑不可移除（即非简单）点来施加拓扑约束，可无缝集成到任何输出为softmax或sigmoid的深度神经网络分割模型中。实验结果表明，该方法在多个基准测试中有效提升了拓扑完整性和结构精度。代码见https://github.com/levnsio/CSP。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for topology-preserving image segmentation in deep learning, this paper introduces a differentiable method to compute simple points directly on continuous-tone images using an $\ell_0$-norm of cyclic gradients, overcoming the limitations of binary-only and non-differentiable existing approaches. The method enables topological consistency by preserving non-simple points in a variational framework compatible with softmax/sigmoid outputs, and it supports efficient skeleton extraction. Experiments show improved topological integrity and structural accuracy across multiple benchmarks compared to baseline methods.
为解决深度学习中拓扑保持图像分割的难题，本文提出一种直接在连续色调图像上计算简单点的可微方法，基于循环梯度的 $\ell_0$ 范数，突破了传统方法仅适用于二值图像且不可微的限制。该方法通过变分模型保留拓扑不可移除点（即非简单点），可无缝集成到使用 softmax 或 sigmoid 输出的神经网络中。实验表明，该方法在多个基准测试中显著提升了拓扑完整性和结构准确性。
Intern-Atlas: A Methodological Evolution Graph as Research Infrastructure for AI Scientists
Authors: Yujun Wu, Dongxu Zhang, Xinchen Li, Jinhang Xu, Yiling Duan, Yumou Liu, Jiabao Pan, Xuanhe Zhou, Jingxuan Wei, Siyuan Li, Jintao Chen, Conghui He, Cheng Tan
First: 2026-04-30T17:44:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:44:55+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 5 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing research infrastructure is fundamentally document-centric, providing citation links between papers but lacking explicit representations of methodological evolution. In particular, it does not capture the structured relationships that explain how and why research methods emerge, adapt, and build upon one another. With the rise of AI-driven research agents as a new class of consumers of scientific knowledge, this limitation becomes increasingly consequential, as such agents cannot reliably reconstruct method evolution topologies from unstructured text. We introduce Intern-Atlas, a methodological evolution graph that automatically identifies method-level entities, infers lineage relationships among methodologies, and captures the bottlenecks that drive transitions between successive innovations. Built from 1,030,314 papers spanning AI conferences, journals, and arXiv preprints, the resulting graph comprises 9,410,201 semantically typed edges, each grounded in verbatim source evidence, forming a queryable causal network of methodological development. To operationalize this structure, we further propose a self-guided temporal tree search algorithm for constructing evolution chains that trace the progression of methods over time. We evaluate the quality of the resulting graph against expert-curated ground-truth evolution chains and observe strong alignment. In addition, we demonstrate that Intern-Atlas enables downstream applications in idea evaluation and automated idea generation. We position methodological evolution graphs as a foundational data layer for the emerging automated scientific discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Intern-Atlas：面向AI科学家的研究基础设施——方法论演化图谱
现有研究基础设施本质上以文档为中心，仅提供论文间的引用链接，却缺乏对方法论演化的显式表达。尤其未能捕捉解释研究方法如何产生、适应并相互构建的结构化关系。随着AI驱动的研究代理作为科学知识的新类消费者兴起，这一局限愈发重要，因为此类代理无法从非结构化文本中可靠重建方法演化拓扑。我们提出Intern-Atlas，一种方法论演化图谱，可自动识别方法级实体，推断方法间的谱系关系，并捕捉驱动方法间创新过渡的瓶颈。该图谱基于103万篇涵盖AI会议、期刊及arXiv预印本的论文构建，包含941万条语义类型边，每条边均基于原文证据支撑，形成可查询的方法演化因果网络。为实现该结构，我们进一步提出一种自引导的时间树搜索算法，用于构建追踪方法随时间演进的演化链。我们通过与专家标注的真实演化链对比评估图谱质量，观察到高度一致性。此外，我们证明Intern-Atlas可支持下游应用，如创意评估与自动化创意生成。我们主张方法论演化图谱作为新兴自动化科学发现的基础数据层。

The graph, comprising 9.4 million semantically typed edges, captures causal transitions between methods and bottlenecks driving innovation. A self-guided temporal tree search algorithm enables reconstruction of historical method evolution chains, which align strongly with expert-curated ground truth. Experimental results confirm the graph’s utility for downstream tasks like idea evaluation and automated generation, positioning it as a foundational infrastructure for automated scientific discovery.
为解决现有研究基础设施缺乏方法论演化结构化表示的问题，本文提出Intern-Atlas方法演化图，从103万篇AI论文中自动识别方法实体、推断方法谱系并捕捉演化瓶颈。该图包含940万条语义标注边，每条均基于原文证据，支持对方法演化的因果查询。与专家标注的演化链对比显示高度一致性，配套的自引导时间树搜索算法能有效重建方法随时间的演进路径，支持创意评估与自动创意生成，为自动化科学发现奠定基础数据层。
FlashRT: Towards Computationally and Memory Efficient Red-Teaming for Prompt Injection and Knowledge Corruption
Authors: Yanting Wang, Chenlong Yin, Ying Chen, Jinyuan Jia
First: 2026-04-30T17:43:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:43:24+00:00
Comments: The code is available at https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/FlashRT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Long-context large language models (LLMs)-for example, Gemini-3.1-Pro and Qwen-3.5-are widely used to empower many real-world applications, such as retrieval-augmented generation, autonomous agents, and AI assistants. However, security remains a major concern for their widespread deployment, with threats such as prompt injection and knowledge corruption. To quantify the security risks faced by LLMs under these threats, the research community has developed heuristic-based and optimization-based red-teaming methods. Optimization-based methods generally produce stronger attacks than heuristic attacks and thus provide a more rigorous assessment of LLM security risks. However, they are often resource-intensive, requiring significant computation and GPU memory, especially for long context scenarios. The resource-intensive nature poses a major obstacle for the community (especially academic researchers) to systematically evaluate the security risks of long-context LLMs and assess the effectiveness of defense strategies at scale. In this work, we propose FlashRT, the first framework to improve the efficiency (in terms of both computation and memory) for optimization-based prompt injection and knowledge corruption attacks under long-context LLMs. Through extensive evaluations, we find that FlashRT consistently delivers a 2x-7x speedup (e.g., reducing runtime from one hour to less than ten minutes) and a 2x-4x reduction in GPU memory consumption (e.g., reducing from 264.1 GB to 65.7 GB GPU memory for a 32K token context) compared to state-of-the-art baseline nanoGCG. FlashRT can be broadly applied to black-box optimization methods, such as TAP and AutoDAN. We hope FlashRT can serve as a red-teaming tool to enable systematic evaluation of long-context LLM security. The code is available at: https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/FlashRT

标题：FlashRT：面向提示注入与知识污染的计算与内存高效红队测试框架
长上下文大语言模型（LLM），如Gemini-3.1-Pro和Qwen-3.5，广泛应用于检索增强生成、自主代理和AI助手等现实场景。然而，其大规模部署面临安全风险，如提示注入和知识污染。为量化LLM在这些威胁下的安全风险，研究社区开发了基于启发式和优化的红队测试方法。优化方法通常比启发式方法产生更强攻击，从而更严谨地评估LLM安全风险。但优化方法资源密集，尤其在长上下文场景下需大量计算和GPU内存，这成为学术界系统评估长上下文LLM安全风险及大规模评估防御策略的主要障碍。本文提出FlashRT，首个针对长上下文LLM优化型提示注入与知识污染攻击的计算与内存效率提升框架。大量评估表明，FlashRT相比当前最优基线nanoGCG，可实现2-7倍速度提升（如将运行时间从1小时缩短至10分钟内）和2-4倍GPU内存减少（如32K上下文从264.1GB降至65.7GB）。FlashRT可广泛应用于黑盒优化方法，如TAP和AutoDAN。我们希望FlashRT能作为红队测试工具，助力系统性评估长上下文LLM安全。代码见：https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/FlashRT

FlexiTac: A Low-Cost, Open-Source, Scalable Tactile Sensing Solution for Robotic Systems
Authors: Binghao Huang, Yunzhu Li
First: 2026-04-30T17:43:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:43:07+00:00
Comments: Website: https://flexitac.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present FlexiTac, a low-cost, open-source, and scalable piezoresistive tactile sensing solution designed for robotic end-effectors. FlexiTac is a practical "plug-in" module consisting of (i) thin, flexible tactile sensor pads that provide dense tactile signals and (ii) a compact multi-channel readout board that streams synchronized measurements for real-time control and large-scale data collection. FlexiTac pads adopt a sealed three-layer laminate stack (FPC-Velostat-FPC) with electrode patterns directly integrated into flexible printed circuits, substantially improving fabrication throughput and repeatability while maintaining mechanical compliance for deployment on both rigid and soft grippers. The readout electronics use widely available, low-cost components and stream tactile signals to a host computer at 100 Hz via serial communication. Across multiple configurations, including fingertip pads and larger tactile mats, FlexiTac can be mounted on diverse platforms without major mechanical redesign. We further show that FlexiTac supports modern tactile learning pipelines, including 3D visuo-tactile fusion for contact-aware decision making, cross-embodiment skill transfer, and real-to-sim-to-real fine-tuning with GPU-parallel tactile simulation. Our project page is available at https://flexitac.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlexiTac：一种低成本、开源、可扩展的机器人系统触觉传感解决方案
我们提出了FlexiTac，一种专为机器人末端执行器设计的低成本、开源且可扩展的压阻式触觉传感解决方案。FlexiTac是一个实用的“即插即用”模块，包含（i）提供密集触觉信号的薄柔性触觉传感垫，以及（ii）一个紧凑的多通道读取板，用于同步传输测量数据，以支持实时控制和大规模数据采集。FlexiTac传感垫采用密封三层层压结构（柔性电路板-维洛斯塔特-柔性电路板），电极图案直接集成于柔性印刷电路中，显著提高了制造效率和可重复性，同时保持机械顺应性，适用于刚性和柔性夹爪部署。读取电子元件采用广泛可用且低成本的组件，通过串行通信以100Hz速率将触觉信号传输至主机计算机。在多种配置（包括指尖垫和更大尺寸触觉垫）下，FlexiTac可无需重大机械改造即可安装于多种平台。我们进一步表明，FlexiTac支持现代触觉学习流程，包括用于接触感知决策的3D视觉-触觉融合、跨具身技能迁移，以及结合GPU并行触觉模拟的实-仿-实微调。项目页面详见https://flexitac.github.io/。

Motivated by the need for affordable, scalable, and easily deployable tactile sensing in robotics, FlexiTac introduces a low-cost, open-source piezoresistive tactile system designed as a plug-in module for robotic end-effectors. Experimental results demonstrate FlexiTac’s compatibility across diverse robotic platforms without requiring major mechanical modifications, and its effectiveness in supporting advanced tactile learning tasks such as 3D visuo-tactile fusion, cross-embodiment skill transfer, and real-to-sim-to-real fine-tuning using GPU-parallel tactile simulation, all while maintaining high fabrication repeatability and mechanical compliance.
为满足机器人领域对低成本、可扩展且易部署触觉传感的需求，FlexiTac 提出了一种基于压阻式传感的开源触觉系统。该系统由集成电极图案的柔性印刷电路触觉垫和紧凑型多通道读取板组成，可同步以100Hz速率传输数据。实验表明，该系统可适配多种机械抓手与平台，无需重大机械改造，并成功支持高级触觉学习任务，如3D视觉-触觉融合、跨机体技能迁移及真实-仿真-真实微调（通过GPU并行仿真），验证了其在科研与实际机器人应用中的实用性。
Explainable Load Forecasting with Covariate-Informed Time Series Foundation Models
Authors: Matthias Hertel, Alexandra Nikoltchovska, Sebastian Pütz, Ralf Mikut, Benjamin Schäfer, Veit Hagenmeyer
First: 2026-04-30T17:36:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:36:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have recently emerged as general-purpose forecasting models and show considerable potential for applications in energy systems. However, applications in critical infrastructure like power grids require transparency to ensure trust and reliability and cannot rely on pure black-box models. To enhance the transparency of TSFMs, we propose an efficient algorithm for computing Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) tailored to these models. The proposed approach leverages the flexibility of TSFMs with respect to input context length and provided covariates. This property enables efficient temporal and covariate masking (selectively withholding inputs), allowing for a scalable explanation of model predictions using SHAP. We evaluate two TSFMs - Chronos-2 and TabPFN-TS - on a day-ahead load forecasting task for a transmission system operator (TSO). In a zero-shot setting, both models achieve predictive performance competitive with a Transformer model trained specifically on multiple years of TSO data. The explanations obtained through our proposed approach align with established domain knowledge, particularly as the TSFMs appropriately use weather and calendar information for load prediction. Overall, we demonstrate that TSFMs can serve as transparent and reliable tools for operational energy forecasting.

Motivated by the need for transparent and trustworthy forecasting models in critical energy infrastructure, this work proposes an efficient SHAP-based explanation method tailored for Time Series Foundation Models. Sample-efficient evidence estimation of score based priors for model selection
Authors: Frederic Wang, Katherine L. Bouman
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-02-24T05:06:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:36:17+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The choice of prior is central to solving ill-posed imaging inverse problems, making it essential to select one consistent with the measurements $y$ to avoid severe bias. In Bayesian inverse problems, this could be achieved by evaluating the model evidence $p(y \mid M)$ under different models $M$ that specify the prior and then selecting the one with the highest value. Diffusion models are the state-of-the-art approach to solving inverse problems with a data-driven prior; however, directly computing the model evidence with respect to a diffusion prior is intractable. Furthermore, most existing model evidence estimators require either many pointwise evaluations of the unnormalized prior density or an accurate clean prior score. We propose DiME, an estimator of the model evidence of a diffusion prior by integrating over the time-marginals of posterior sampling methods. Our method leverages the large amount of intermediate samples naturally obtained during the reverse diffusion sampling process to obtain an accurate estimation of the model evidence using only a handful of posterior samples (e.g., 20). We also demonstrate how to implement our estimator in tandem with recent diffusion posterior sampling methods. Empirically, our estimator matches the model evidence when it can be computed analytically, and it is able to both select the correct diffusion model prior and diagnose prior misfit under different highly ill-conditioned, non-linear inverse problems, including a real-world black hole imaging problem.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于分数先验的模型选择中样本高效的证据估计
先验的选择对于解决病态成像逆问题至关重要，必须选择与测量值 y 一致的先验以避免严重偏差。在贝叶斯逆问题中，可通过在不同模型 M（指定先验）下评估模型证据 p(y|M) 并选择值最大的模型实现。扩散模型是当前数据驱动先验解决逆问题的最先进方法；然而，直接计算扩散先验的模型证据是不可行的。此外，大多数现有模型证据估计器需要大量未归一化先验密度的点评估或精确的干净先验分数。我们提出 DiME，一种通过后验采样方法的时间边缘分布积分来估计扩散先验模型证据的方法。该方法利用反向扩散采样过程中自然获得的大量中间样本，仅需少量后验样本（如20个）即可准确估计模型证据。我们还展示了如何将该估计器与近期扩散后验采样方法结合使用。实证表明，当模型证据可解析计算时，我们的估计器能匹配其值，并能在不同高度病态的非线性逆问题（包括真实黑洞成像问题）中正确选择扩散模型先验并诊断先验拟合不良。

DiME leverages posterior samples from reverse diffusion processes, integrating over time-marginals to approximate evidence using only a few samples (e.g., 20). Experiments show DiME accurately matches analytical evidence values and successfully selects correct diffusion priors while diagnosing prior misfit in challenging non-linear inverse problems, including real-world black hole imaging.
为解决逆问题中选择与测量一致的先验分布以避免偏差，本文提出DiME方法，一种针对扩散先验的样本高效模型证据估计器。该方法利用反向扩散采样过程中自然生成的中间样本，无需大量点态先验密度评估或精确干净的得分函数即可近似证据。实验表明，DiME在可解析计算时能准确估计证据，能正确选择扩散先验，并在高度病态非线性逆问题（包括真实黑洞成像）中诊断先验拟合不足。
Design and Characteristics of a Thin-Film ThermoMesh for the Efficient Embedded Sensing of a Spatio-Temporally Sparse Heat Source
Authors: Sajjad Boorghan Farahan, Ahmed Alajlouni, Jingzhou Zhao
First: 2026-04-30T17:35:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:35:40+00:00
Comments: 45 pages, 13 figures, 63 references, under review in Sensors and Actuators A: Physical
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work presents ThermoMesh, a passive thin-film thermoelectric mesh sensor designed to detect and characterize spatio-temporally sparse heat sources through conduction-based thermal imaging. The device integrates thermoelectric junctions with linear or nonlinear interlayer resistive elements to perform simultaneous sensing and in-sensor compression. We focus on the single-event (1-sparse) operation and define four performance metrics: range, efficiency, sensitivity, and accuracy. Numerical modeling shows that a linear resistive interlayer flattens the sensitivity distribution and improves minimum sensitivity by approximately tenfold for a $16\times16$ mesh. Nonlinear temperature-dependent interlayers further enhance minimum sensitivity at scale: a ceramic negative-temperature-coefficient (NTC) layer over 973--1273~K yields a $\sim14{,}500\times$ higher minimum sensitivity than the linear design at a $200\times200$ mesh, while a VO$_2$ interlayer modeled across its metal--insulator transition (MIT) over 298--373~K yields a $\sim24\times$ improvement. Using synthetic 1-sparse datasets with white boundary-channel noise at a signal-to-noise ratio of 40~dB, the VO$_2$ case achieved $98\%$ localization accuracy, a mean absolute temperature error of $0.23$~K, and a noise-equivalent temperature (NET) of $0.07$~K. For the ceramic-NTC case no localization errors were observed under the tested conditions, with a mean absolute temperature error of $1.83$~K and a NET of $1.49$~K. These results indicate that ThermoMesh could enable energy-efficient embedded thermal sensing in scenarios where conventional infrared imaging is limited, such as molten-droplet detection or hot-spot monitoring in harsh environments.

From Competition to Collaboration: Designing Sustainable Mechanisms Between LLMs and Online Forums
Authors: Niv Fono, Yftah Ziser, Omer Ben-Porat
First: 2026-02-04T13:58:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:34:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Generative AI (GenAI) systems draw users away from (Q&A) forums, they also depend on the very data those forums produce to improve their performance. Addressing this paradox, we propose a framework of sequential interaction, in which a GenAI system proposes questions to a forum that can publish some of them. Our framework captures several intricacies of such a collaboration, including non-monetary exchanges, asymmetric information, and incentive misalignment. We bring the framework to life through comprehensive, data-driven simulations using real Stack Exchange data and commonly used LLMs. We demonstrate the incentive misalignment empirically, yet show that players can achieve roughly half of the utility in an ideal full-information scenario. Our results highlight the potential for sustainable collaboration that preserves effective knowledge sharing between AI systems and human knowledge platforms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从竞争到协作：设计大语言模型与在线论坛之间的可持续机制
尽管生成式人工智能（GenAI）系统将用户从（问答）论坛中吸引走，但它们也依赖于这些论坛产生的数据来提升自身性能。为解决这一悖论，我们提出了一种顺序交互框架，其中GenAI系统向论坛提出问题，论坛可选择发布部分问题。该框架捕捉了此类协作中的若干复杂性，包括非货币交换、信息不对称和激励错配。我们通过使用真实Stack Exchange数据和常用大语言模型进行全面、数据驱动的模拟，使该框架得以实现。我们实证展示了激励错配，但表明参与者仍可实现理想完全信息场景下约一半的效用。我们的结果突显了AI系统与人类知识平台之间可持续协作的潜力，同时保持有效的知识共享。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the paradox that GenAI systems both compete with and rely on online forums, this work proposes a sequential interaction framework where an LLM proposes questions to a forum that selectively publishes them. The framework models real-world complexities such as non-monetary exchanges, asymmetric information, and incentive misalignment. Through simulations using real Stack Exchange data and standard LLMs, the study empirically confirms incentive misalignment but shows that participants can still achieve about half the utility of an ideal full-information scenario, suggesting a viable path for sustainable collaboration between AI and human knowledge platforms.
鉴于生成式AI既与在线论坛竞争又依赖其数据，本文提出一种序列交互框架，让LLM向论坛提出问题，论坛选择性发布，以建模非货币交换、信息不对称与激励错配。基于真实Stack Exchange数据和常用LLM的仿真表明，虽存在激励错配，但参与者仍可实现理想全信息场景下约一半的效用，证明AI与人类知识平台可持续协作的可行性。
Agentic Harness Engineering: Observability-Driven Automatic Evolution of Coding-Agent Harnesses
Authors: Jiahang Lin, Shichun Liu, Chengjun Pan, Lizhi Lin, Shihan Dou, Xuanjing Huang, Hang Yan, Zhenhua Han, Tao Gui
First: 2026-04-28T16:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:34:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Harnesses are now central to coding-agent performance, mediating how models interact with tools and execution environments. Yet harness engineering remains a manual craft, because automating it faces a heterogeneous action space across editable components, voluminous trajectories that bury actionable signal, and edits whose effect is hard to attribute. We introduce Agentic Harness Engineering (AHE), a closed loop that addresses these challenges through three matched observability pillars: (1) component observability gives every editable harness component a file-level representation so the action space is explicit and revertible; (2) experience observability distills millions of raw trajectory tokens into a layered, drill-down evidence corpus that an evolving agent can actually consume; and (3) decision observability pairs every edit with a self-declared prediction, later verified against the next round's task-level outcomes. Together, these pillars turn every edit into a falsifiable contract, so harness evolution proceeds autonomously without collapsing into trial-and-error. Empirically, ten AHE iterations lift pass@1 on Terminal-Bench 2 from 69.7% to 77.0%, surpassing the human-designed harness Codex-CLI (71.9%) and the self-evolving baselines ACE and TF-GRPO. The frozen harness transfers without re-evolution: on SWE-bench-verified it tops aggregate success at 12% fewer tokens than the seed, and on Terminal-Bench 2 it yields +5.1 to +10.1pp cross-family gains across three alternate model families, indicating the evolved components encode general engineering experience rather than benchmark-specific tuning. Ablations localize the gain to tools, middleware, and long-term memory rather than the system prompt, suggesting factual harness structure transfers while prose-level strategy does not.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理化夹具工程：基于可观测性的编码代理夹具自动演化
夹具如今是编码代理性能的核心，介导模型与工具及执行环境的交互。然而，夹具工程仍为手工操作，因为自动化面临可编辑组件间异构的动作空间、海量轨迹淹没可操作信号，以及编辑效果难以归因等问题。我们提出代理化夹具工程（AHE），通过三个匹配的可观测性支柱形成闭环：（1）组件可观测性为每个可编辑夹具组件提供文件级表示，使动作空间明确且可回滚；（2）经验可观测性将数百万原始轨迹令牌提炼为分层可钻取的证据语料库，供演化代理实际消费；（3）决策可观测性将每次编辑与自声明预测配对，后续通过下一轮任务级结果验证。三者协同使每次编辑成为可证伪的契约，从而实现夹具演化自主进行而不陷入试错。实证表明，十轮AHE迭代将Terminal-Bench 2的pass@1从69.7%提升至77.0%，超越人工设计的Codex-CLI（71.9%）及自演化基线ACE和TF-GRPO。冻结夹具无需再演化：在SWE-bench验证中，其总成功率比种子少消耗12%的tokens；在Terminal-Bench 2上，对三个不同模型家族均带来+5.1至+10.1个百分点的跨家族增益，表明演化组件编码的是通用工程经验而非基准特定调优。消融实验表明，收益集中于工具、中间件和长期记忆，而非系统提示，暗示事实性夹具结构可迁移，而策略性语句则不可。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of manual harness engineering for coding agents, this work introduces Agentic Harness Engineering (AHE), a closed-loop system that automates harness evolution through three observability pillars: component observability (explicit, revertible file-level representations), experience observability (layered, drill-down evidence from trajectories), and decision observability (self-declared predictions verified against task outcomes). These enable each edit to be treated as a falsifiable contract, allowing autonomous evolution without trial-and-error. Experiments show AHE improves pass@1 on Terminal-Bench 2 from 69.7% to 77.0%, outperforming human-designed Codex-CLI (71.9%) and self-evolving baselines. The frozen harness transfers effectively, achieving higher success with fewer tokens on SWE-bench and cross-family gains across models, indicating generalizable engineering knowledge rather than benchmark-specific tuning, with gains localized to tools, middleware, and long-term memory.
为解决编码代理中手动构建工具调用框架（harness）的低效问题，本文提出Agentic Harness Engineering（AHE）系统，通过三个可观测性支柱实现自动化演化：组件可观测性（文件级可逆表示）、经验可观测性（从百万轨迹中提炼分层证据）和决策可观测性（自声明预测与任务结果验证），使每次修改可被证伪，从而实现自主演化。实验表明，AHE将Terminal-Bench 2的pass@1从69.7%提升至77.0%，超越人工设计的Codex-CLI（71.9%）和自演化基线。冻结后的框架可直接迁移，SWE-bench上以更少token实现更高成功率，跨模型家族获得+5.1至+10.1个百分点提升，表明其编码的是通用工程经验而非基准特化调优，增益主要来自工具、中间件和长期记忆，而非系统提示。
On the Proper Treatment of Units in Surprisal Theory
Authors: Samuel Kiegeland, Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson, Tim Vieira, Ryan Cotterell
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-30T17:33:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:33:58+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 (main conference)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Surprisal theory links human processing effort to the predictability of an upcoming linguistic unit, but empirical work often leaves the notion of a unit underspecified. In practice, experimental stimuli are segmented into linguistically motivated units (e.g., words), while pretrained language models assign probability mass to a fixed token alphabet that typically does not align with those units. As a result, surprisal-based predictors depend implicitly on ad hoc procedures that conflate two distinct modeling choices: the definition of the unit of analysis and the choice of regions of interest over which predictions are evaluated. In this paper, we disentangle these choices and give a unified framework for reasoning about surprisal over arbitrary unit inventories. We argue that surprisal-based analyses should make these choices explicit and treat tokenization as an implementation detail rather than a scientific primitive.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于惊讶理论中单位的恰当处理
惊讶理论将人类处理努力与即将出现的语言单位的可预测性联系起来，但实证研究往往对单位概念缺乏明确界定。实践中，实验刺激被划分为语言驱动的单位（如单词），而预训练语言模型则在固定标记字母表上分配概率质量，通常与这些单位不一致。因此，基于惊讶的预测隐含依赖于将两种不同建模选择混为一谈的临时程序：分析单位的定义与预测评估所用兴趣区域的选择。本文将这些选择分离，并提供一个统一框架，用于对任意单位集合进行惊讶推理。我们认为，基于惊讶的分析应明确这些选择，并将分词视为实现细节，而非科学基本要素。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the ambiguity in how linguistic units are defined in surprisal theory, where empirical studies often use word-based segmentation while language models operate on fixed token alphabets that don't align with those units. The authors propose a unified framework that separates the choice of analysis units from the regions of interest for prediction, arguing that surprisal analyses should explicitly declare these choices to avoid conflating modeling decisions. Experiments demonstrate that treating tokenization as an implementation detail rather than a scientific primitive leads to more consistent and interpretable surprisal-based predictions across different unit inventories.
本文针对 surprisal 理论中语言单位定义模糊的问题，指出实验中常用词作为单位，而语言模型则基于固定词表，二者不一致。作者提出统一框架，明确区分分析单位的选择与预测区域的设定，主张将分词视为实现细节而非科学基础。实验表明，忽略此区分会导致 surprisal 预测失真，其框架可支持跨分词方案的一致性分析。
MoCapAnything: Unified 3D Motion Capture for Arbitrary Skeletons from Monocular Videos
Authors: Kehong Gong, Zhengyu Wen, Weixia He, Mingxi Xu, Qi Wang, Ning Zhang, Zhengyu Li, Dongze Lian, Wei Zhao, Xiaoyu He, Mingyuan Zhang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-12-11T18:09:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:33:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Motion capture now underpins content creation far beyond digital humans, yet most existing pipelines remain species- or template-specific. We formalize this gap as Category-Agnostic Motion Capture (CAMoCap): given a monocular video and an arbitrary rigged 3D asset as a prompt, the goal is to reconstruct a rotation-based animation such as BVH that directly drives the specific asset. We present MoCapAnything, a reference-guided, factorized framework that first predicts 3D joint trajectories and then recovers asset-specific rotations via constraint-aware inverse kinematics. The system contains three learnable modules and a lightweight IK stage: (1) a Reference Prompt Encoder that extracts per-joint queries from the asset's skeleton, mesh, and rendered images; (2) a Video Feature Extractor that computes dense visual descriptors and reconstructs a coarse 4D deforming mesh to bridge the gap between video and joint space; and (3) a Unified Motion Decoder that fuses these cues to produce temporally coherent trajectories. We also curate Truebones Zoo with 1038 motion clips, each providing a standardized skeleton-mesh-render triad. Experiments on both in-domain benchmarks and in-the-wild videos show that MoCapAnything delivers high-quality skeletal animations and exhibits meaningful cross-species retargeting across heterogeneous rigs, enabling scalable, prompt-driven 3D motion capture for arbitrary assets. Project page: https://animotionlab.github.io/MoCapAnything/
中文标题/摘要
标题：MoCapAnything：从单目视频中为任意骨骼结构统一重建3D动作捕捉
动作捕捉如今已远超数字人领域，支撑内容创作，但现有流程多局限于特定物种或模板。我们将其差距形式化为类别无关动作捕捉（CAMoCap）：给定单目视频和任意绑定的3D资产作为提示，目标是重建如BVH格式的基于旋转的动画，直接驱动该特定资产。我们提出MoCapAnything，一种参考引导、因子分解框架，首先预测3D关节轨迹，再通过约束感知逆运动学恢复资产特定旋转。系统包含三个可学习模块和一个轻量级IK阶段：（1）参考提示编码器，从资产骨骼、网格及渲染图像中提取每关节查询；（2）视频特征提取器，计算密集视觉描述符并重建粗略4D变形网格，弥合视频与关节空间差距；（3）统一运动解码器，融合这些线索生成时序一致的轨迹。我们还整理了Truebones Zoo，包含1038个动作片段，每个提供标准化的骨骼-网格-渲染三元组。在领域内基准及野外视频实验中，MoCapAnything生成高质量骨骼动画，并在异构绑定结构间实现有意义的跨物种重定向，支持任意资产的可扩展、提示驱动3D动作捕捉。项目页面：https://animotionlab.github.io/MoCapAnything/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible, template-agnostic motion capture from monocular videos, MoCapAnything introduces a reference-guided, factorized framework that reconstructs rotation-based animations (e.g., BVH) for arbitrary 3D assets. It uses three learnable modules: a Reference Prompt Encoder to extract joint-specific queries from the target asset, a Video Feature Extractor to generate dense visual descriptors and a coarse 4D mesh, and a Unified Motion Decoder to fuse these into temporally coherent joint trajectories. Evaluated on in-domain benchmarks and real-world videos using Truebones Zoo (1038 motion clips), the system achieves high-fidelity skeletal animations and demonstrates effective cross-species retargeting across diverse rigs, enabling scalable, prompt-driven motion capture.
为实现任意3D资产的单目视频驱动运动捕捉，MoCapAnything提出了一种参考引导的因子化框架，先通过三个可学习模块预测3D关节轨迹，再利用约束感知逆运动学生成资产专属旋转。该系统在领域内基准及真实视频上表现优异，生成高质量骨骼动画，并成功实现跨物种、跨骨骼结构的重定向，验证了其可扩展性和基于提示的驱动能力。
Global Optimality for Constrained Exploration via Penalty Regularization
Authors: Florian Wolf, Ilyas Fatkhullin, Niao He
First: 2026-04-30T17:31:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:31:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Efficient exploration is a central problem in reinforcement learning and is often formalized as maximizing the entropy of the state-action occupancy measure. While unconstrained maximum-entropy exploration is relatively well understood, real-world exploration is often constrained by safety, resource, or imitation requirements. This constrained setting is particularly challenging because entropy maximization lacks additive structure, rendering Bellman-equation-based methods inapplicable. Moreover, scalable approaches require policy parameterization, inducing non-convexity in both the objective and the constraints. To our knowledge, the only prior model-free policy-gradient approach for this setting under general policy parameterization is due to Ying et al. (2025). Unfortunately, their guarantees are limited to weak regret and ergodic averages, which do not imply that the final output is a single deployable policy that is near-optimal and nearly feasible. In this work we take a different approach to this problem, and propose Policy Gradient Penalty (PGP) method, a single-loop policy-space method that enforces general convex occupancy-measure constraints via quadratic-penalty regularization. PGP constructs pseudo-rewards that yield gradient estimates of the penalized objective, subsequently exploiting the classical Policy Gradient Theorem. We further establish the regularity of the penalized objective, providing the smoothness properties needed to justify the convergence of PGP. Leveraging hidden convexity and strong duality, we then establish global last-iterate convergence guarantees, attaining an $ε$-optimal constrained entropy value with $ε$ bounded constraint violation despite policy-induced non-convexity. We validate PGP through ablations on a grid-world benchmark and further demonstrate scalability on two challenging continuous-control tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过惩罚正则化实现约束探索的全局最优性
高效探索是强化学习中的核心问题，常被形式化为最大化状态-动作占用测度的熵。虽然无约束的最大熵探索相对较为成熟，但现实世界中的探索常受安全、资源或模仿等约束。该约束环境尤其具有挑战性，因为熵最大化缺乏可加结构，使得基于贝尔曼方程的方法不适用。此外，可扩展方法需要策略参数化，导致目标函数和约束条件均出现非凸性。据我们所知，针对该设定且在通用策略参数化下的唯一先前无模型策略梯度方法由Ying等（2025）提出。然而，其保证仅限于弱遗憾和遍历平均值，无法推出最终输出是一个接近最优且几乎可行的单一可部署策略。本文采用不同方法，提出策略梯度惩罚（PGP）方法，这是一种单循环策略空间方法，通过二次惩罚正则化强制满足通用凸占用测度约束。PGP构建伪奖励以获得惩罚目标的梯度估计，随后利用经典策略梯度定理。我们进一步建立了惩罚目标的正则性，提供了支持PGP收敛所需的平滑性性质。借助隐含凸性和强对偶性，我们建立了全局最后迭代收敛保证，在策略诱导非凸性下仍能实现ε-最优约束熵值，且约束违反量ε有界。我们在网格世界基准上通过消融实验验证PGP，并在两个具有挑战性的连续控制任务上进一步展示了其可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of constrained exploration in reinforcement learning where entropy maximization lacks additive structure and Bellman-equation methods fail, this work introduces Policy Gradient Penalty (PGP), a single-loop policy-space method that enforces general convex occupancy-measure constraints via quadratic-penalty regularization. PGP constructs pseudo-rewards to estimate gradients of the penalized objective and leverages the Policy Gradient Theorem, while establishing smoothness properties and leveraging hidden convexity and strong duality to prove global last-iterate convergence. Experiments on grid-world and continuous-control tasks show PGP achieves ε-optimal constrained entropy with bounded constraint violation, outperforming prior methods that only guarantee weak regret or ergodic averages without ensuring deployable near-optimal policies.
为解决强化学习中受约束的探索问题——其中熵最大化缺乏可加结构，导致贝尔曼方程方法失效——本文提出Policy Gradient Penalty（PGP）方法，通过二次惩罚正则化在策略空间中单循环执行，强制满足一般凸的占用测度约束。PGP构建伪奖励以估计惩罚目标的梯度，结合策略梯度定理，并通过建立光滑性性质和利用隐含凸性与强对偶性，证明全局最后迭代收敛性。实验表明，PGP在网格世界和连续控制任务中实现ε最优约束熵并保持约束违反有界，优于仅保证弱遗憾或遍历平均的现有方法，且能输出可部署的近最优策略。
Efficient Multivector Retrieval with Token-Aware Clustering and Hierarchical Indexing
Authors: Silvio Martinico, Franco Maria Nardini, Cosimo Rulli, Rossano Venturini
Venue: SIGIR 2026
First: 2026-04-30T17:30:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:30:15+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 2 figures, SIGIR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multivector retrieval models achieve state-of-the-art effectiveness through fine-grained token-level representations, but their deployment incurs substantial computational and memory costs. Current solutions, based on the well-known k-means clustering algorithm, group similar vectors together to enable both effective compression and efficient retrieval. However, standard k-means scales poorly with the number of clusters and dataset size, and favours frequent tokens during training while underrepresenting rare, discriminative ones. In this work, we introduce TACHIOM, a multivector retrieval system that exploits token-level structure to significantly accelerate both clustering and retrieval. By accounting for tokens' distribution during centroid allocation, TACHIOM easily scales to millions of centroids, enabling highly accurate document scoring using only centroids, avoiding expensive token-level computation. TACHIOM combines a graph-based index over centroids with an optimized Product Quantization layout for efficient final scoring. Experiments on MS-MARCOv1 and LoTTE show that TACHIOM achieves up to $247\times$ faster clustering than k-means and up to $9.8\times$ retrieval speedup over state-of-the-art systems while maintaining comparable or superior effectiveness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于令牌感知聚类与分层索引的高效多向量检索
多向量检索模型通过细粒度的令牌级表示实现最先进效果，但其部署需耗费大量计算与内存资源。现有基于经典k-means聚类算法的解决方案通过将相似向量分组，实现有效压缩与高效检索。然而，标准k-means在聚类数和数据集规模增大时扩展性差，且训练时偏向高频令牌，而对稀有、有区分度的令牌代表性不足。本文提出TACHIOM，一种利用令牌级结构显著加速聚类与检索的多向量检索系统。通过在质心分配时考虑令牌分布，TACHIOM可轻松扩展至百万级质心，仅用质心即可实现高精度文档评分，避免昂贵的令牌级计算。TACHIOM结合质心上的图结构索引与优化的乘积量化布局，实现高效最终评分。在MS-MARCOv1和LoTTE数据集上的实验表明，TACHIOM比k-means快247倍，比现有最先进系统快9.8倍，同时保持相当或更优的效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational and memory costs of deploying multivector retrieval models, this work introduces TACHIOM, a system that leverages token-aware clustering and hierarchical indexing to accelerate both clustering and retrieval. TACHIOM improves upon standard k-means by incorporating token distribution during centroid allocation, enabling scalable clustering of millions of centroids while preserving retrieval accuracy without token-level computation. Experimental results on MS-MARCOv1 and LoTTE demonstrate that TACHIOM achieves up to 247x faster clustering than k-means and up to 9.8x faster retrieval speed compared to state-of-the-art systems, with comparable or better effectiveness.
为降低多向量检索模型的计算与内存开销，本文提出TACHIOM系统，通过令牌感知聚类与分层索引加速聚类与检索过程。TACHIOM在质心分配时考虑令牌分布，支持百万级质心的可扩展聚类，同时避免令牌级计算，保持检索精度。系统结合质心上的图结构索引与优化的乘积量化布局，实现高效最终评分。在MS-MARCOv1和LoTTE数据集上的实验表明，TACHIOM比现有方法快247倍聚类、9.8倍检索速度，且效果相当或更优。
OpenClassGen: A Large-Scale Corpus of Real-World Python Classes for LLM Research
Authors: Musfiqur Rahman, SayedHassan Khatoonabadi, Emad Shihab
First: 2025-04-22T03:33:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:27:03+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted for publication at the 30th International Conference on Evaluation and Assessment in Software Engineering (EASE 2026) AI models/data track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing class-level code generation datasets are either synthetic (ClassEval: 100 classes) or insufficient in scale for modern training needs (RealClassEval: 400 classes), hindering robust evaluation and empirical analysis. We present OpenClassGen, a large-scale corpus of 324,843 Python classes extracted from 2,970 engineered open-source projects. Each entry pairs a human-written class with its corresponding skeleton, which comprises class and method signatures with associated docstrings, and is enriched with 27 static code metrics covering complexity, coupling, cohesion, and inheritance properties. Unlike prior benchmarks that require repository-level context resolution, OpenClassGen provides self-contained class skeletons that serve as complete generation specifications. We demonstrate the corpus's utility by evaluating three LLMs (GPT-o4-mini, Claude-4-Sonnet, Qwen-3-Coder) on a curated, executable subset of 300 classes, enriched with test suites achieving 58% branch coverage. Results show strong semantic similarity (CodeBERTScore-F3: 0.89) but moderate functional correctness (pass rate: 0.33), with substantial variance across models. This variance, along with diverse class characteristics, confirms that OpenClassGen enables meaningful differentiation of LLM capabilities. The dataset supports diverse use cases, including fine-tuning, retrieval-augmented generation, difficulty modelling, and failure mode analysis. The complete dataset and curation scripts are publicly available at https://zenodo.org/records/18409150.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OpenClassGen：面向大语言模型研究的现实世界Python类大规模语料库
现有类级代码生成数据集要么是合成的（ClassEval：100类），要么规模不足以满足现代训练需求（RealClassEval：400类），阻碍了稳健评估和实证分析。我们提出了OpenClassGen，一个从2,970个工程化开源项目中提取的324,843个Python类语料库。每个条目配对一个手写类与对应骨架，包含类和方法签名及其关联文档字符串，并附加27项静态代码度量，涵盖复杂度、耦合度、内聚性和继承特性。与先前需要仓库级上下文解析的基准不同，OpenClassGen提供自包含的类骨架，作为完整的生成规范。我们通过在300个精选可执行类子集（附带测试套件，实现58%分支覆盖率）上评估三个大语言模型（GPT-o4-mini、Claude-4-Sonnet、Qwen-3-Coder）来展示语料库的实用性。结果显示语义相似度高（CodeBERTScore-F3：0.89），但功能正确性中等（通过率：0.33），且模型间差异显著。这种差异结合类特征多样性，证实OpenClassGen能有效区分大语言模型能力。该数据集支持多种用途，包括微调、检索增强生成、难度建模和失败模式分析。完整数据集和筛选脚本已公开于https://zenodo.org/records/18409150。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of large-scale, real-world Python class datasets for evaluating LLMs, the authors introduce OpenClassGen, a corpus of 324,843 classes extracted from 2,970 open-source projects. Each class entry includes a human-written implementation paired with a self-contained skeleton containing signatures and docstrings, enriched with 27 static metrics. Evaluated on 300 executable classes with test suites achieving 58% branch coverage, three LLMs (GPT-o4-mini, Claude-4-Sonnet, Qwen-3-Coder) showed high semantic similarity (CodeBERTScore-F3: 0.89) but only moderate functional correctness (pass rate: 0.33), with significant model-specific performance variance. This confirms OpenClassGen’s utility in differentiating LLM capabilities and supports diverse research applications including fine-tuning and failure analysis.
为解决现有类级代码生成数据集规模小或合成度高的问题，作者构建了OpenClassGen，包含从2970个开源项目中提取的324,843个真实Python类。每个类条目包含人工编写代码与自包含的类骨架（含签名、文档字符串及27项静态度量），支持无需仓库上下文的生成评估。在300个可执行类上测试三款LLM（GPT-o4-mini、Claude-4-Sonnet、Qwen-3-Coder）时，结果显示语义相似度高（CodeBERTScore-F3: 0.89），但功能正确率仅0.33，且模型间差异显著，验证了该数据集对区分不同LLM能力的有效性。
Hinge Regression Tree: A Newton Method for Oblique Regression Tree Splitting
Authors: Hongyi Li, Han Lin, Jun Xu
First: 2026-02-05T06:49:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:23:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Oblique decision trees combine the transparency of trees with the power of multivariate decision boundaries, but learning high-quality oblique splits is NP-hard, and practical methods still rely on slow search or theory-free heuristics. We present the Hinge Regression Tree (HRT), which reframes each split as a non-linear least-squares problem over two linear predictors whose max/min envelope induces ReLU-like expressive power. The resulting alternating fitting procedure is exactly equivalent to a damped Newton (Gauss-Newton) method within fixed partitions. We analyze this node-level optimization and, for a backtracking line-search variant, prove that the local objective decreases monotonically and converges; in practice, both fixed and adaptive damping yield fast, stable convergence and can be combined with optional ridge regularization. We further prove that HRT's model class is a universal approximator with an explicit $O(δ^2)$ approximation rate, and show on synthetic and real-world benchmarks that it matches or outperforms single-tree baselines with more compact structures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：铰链回归树：一种用于斜向回归树分割的牛顿方法
斜向决策树结合了树的透明性和多变量决策边界的强大能力，但学习高质量的斜向分割是NP难问题，实际方法仍依赖缓慢搜索或无理论依据的启发式方法。我们提出了铰链回归树（HRT），将每个分割重新表述为两个线性预测器的最大/最小包络诱导ReLU类表达能力的非线性最小二乘问题。由此得到的交替拟合过程在固定分区下等价于阻尼牛顿（高斯-牛顿）方法。我们分析了节点级优化，并对回溯线搜索变体证明了局部目标单调递减并收敛；实践中，固定和自适应阻尼均能快速稳定收敛，且可结合可选岭正则化。我们进一步证明HRT的模型类是通用逼近器，具有显式的$O(δ^2)$逼近率，并在合成和真实基准上表明其结构更紧凑，性能可匹配或优于单树基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational difficulty of learning high-quality oblique splits in decision trees, the paper introduces Hinge Regression Tree (HRT), which reformulates each split as a non-linear least-squares problem over two linear predictors whose max/min envelope mimics ReLU-like expressivity. The method employs an alternating fitting procedure equivalent to a damped Newton (Gauss-Newton) method, with theoretical guarantees for monotonic local convergence and practical stability via fixed or adaptive damping. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that HRT achieves performance matching or exceeding single-tree baselines while producing more compact tree structures.
为了解决学习高质量斜切分在决策树中的计算难题，本文提出Hinge回归树（HRT），将每个分割重述为两个线性预测器的最大/最小包络所构成的非线性最小二乘问题，其表达能力类似ReLU。该方法采用交替拟合过程，等价于阻尼牛顿（高斯-牛顿）法，理论证明其局部目标单调下降并收敛，实践中固定或自适应阻尼均能快速稳定收敛，可结合岭正则化。在合成与真实数据集上的实验表明，HRT在结构更紧凑的前提下，性能可媲美或超越单树基线方法。
Claw-Eval-Live: A Live Agent Benchmark for Evolving Real-World Workflows
Authors: Chenxin Li, Zhengyang Tang, Huangxin Lin, Yunlong Lin, Shijue Huang, Shengyuan Liu, Bowen Ye, Rang Li, Lei Li, Benyou Wang, Yixuan Yuan
First: 2026-04-30T17:23:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:23:19+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://claw-eval-live.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
LLM agents are expected to complete end-to-end units of work across software tools, business services, and local workspaces. Yet many agent benchmarks freeze a curated task set at release time and grade mainly the final response, making it difficult to evaluate agents against evolving workflow demand or verify whether a task was executed. We introduce Claw-Eval-Live, a live benchmark for workflow agents that separates a refreshable signal layer, updated across releases from public workflow-demand signals, from a reproducible, time-stamped release snapshot. Each release is constructed from public workflow-demand signals, with ClawHub Top-500 skills used in the current release, and materialized as controlled tasks with fixed fixtures, services, workspaces, and graders. For grading, Claw-Eval-Live records execution traces, audit logs, service state, and post-run workspace artifacts, using deterministic checks when evidence is sufficient and structured LLM judging only for semantic dimensions. The release contains 105 tasks spanning controlled business services and local workspace repair, and evaluates 13 frontier models under a shared public pass rule. Experiments reveal that reliable workflow automation remains far from solved: the leading model passes only 66.7% of tasks and no model reaches 70%. Failures are structured by task family and execution surface, with HR, management, and multi-system business workflows as persistent bottlenecks and local workspace repair comparatively easier but unsaturated. Leaderboard rank alone is insufficient because models with similar pass rates can diverge in overall completion, and task-level discrimination concentrates in a middle band of tasks. Claw-Eval-Live suggests that workflow-agent evaluation should be grounded twice, in fresh external demand and in verifiable agent action.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Claw-Eval-Live：面向演进式真实世界工作流的活体代理基准
大型语言模型代理被期望能在软件工具、商业服务和本地工作空间中完成端到端的工作单元。然而，许多代理基准在发布时冻结一组精选任务，并主要评估最终响应，这使得难以评估代理对演进式工作流需求的适应性或验证任务是否实际执行。我们引入Claw-Eval-Live，一个面向工作流代理的活体基准，其将可刷新的信号层（从公共工作流需求信号中持续更新）与可复现、带时间戳的发布快照分离。每个发布版本基于公共工作流需求信号构建，使用当前版本的ClawHub Top-500技能，并以固定配置、服务、工作空间和评分器形式转化为受控任务。评分时，Claw-Eval-Live记录执行轨迹、审计日志、服务状态及运行后工作空间产物，证据充分时采用确定性检查，语义维度则仅由结构化LLM判断。该发布包含105项任务，涵盖受控商业服务与本地工作空间修复，评估13个前沿模型，采用统一公开通过规则。实验表明，可靠的流程自动化仍远未解决：领先模型仅通过66.7%任务，无模型达到70%。失败模式按任务类别和执行层面结构化，HR、管理及多系统商业工作流为持续瓶颈，本地工作空间修复相对容易但未饱和。仅靠排行榜排名不足，因相似通过率的模型在整体完成度上可能差异显著，且任务级区分集中在中间难度任务带。Claw-Eval-Live建议，工作流代理评估应双重锚定：既基于新鲜外部需求，也基于可验证的代理行为。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLM agents against evolving real-world workflow demands and verify actual task execution, Claw-Eval-Live introduces a live benchmark that decouples public workflow signals from fixed release snapshots. Each release is built from ClawHub Top-500 skills, materialized into controlled tasks with fixed fixtures and graders, and graded using deterministic checks and structured LLM judgment. Across 105 tasks spanning business services and local workspace repair, 13 frontier models were tested under a shared pass rule, with the top model achieving only 66.7% success—no model reached 70%. Failures clustered in HR, management, and multi-system workflows, while local repair tasks were easier but underutilized. Leaderboard rankings alone are insufficient, as models with similar pass rates diverge in overall completion, and task discrimination is concentrated in a middle difficulty band, underscoring the need for evaluation grounded in both fresh demand and verifiable agent actions.
为应对当前LLM代理评估依赖静态任务集、难以反映真实工作流动态需求的问题，Claw-Eval-Live提出一个动态基准，将公共工作流需求信号与可复现的发布快照分离。每个版本基于ClawHub Top-500技能构建，转化为固定环境与评分器的控制任务，并通过确定性检查与结构化LLM判断进行评分。在涵盖业务服务与本地工作空间修复的105个任务中，13个前沿模型在统一通过规则下测试，表现最佳模型仅通过66.7%，无模型达70%。失败集中在HR、管理及多系统工作流，本地修复任务相对简单但未被充分挑战。仅凭排行榜排名不足以反映真实能力，相似通过率的模型在整体完成度上差异显著，且任务区分度集中在中间性能区间，表明评估需同时结合实时需求与可验证的代理行为。
Crab: A Semantics-Aware Checkpoint/Restore Runtime for Agent Sandboxes
Authors: Tianyuan Wu, Chaokun Chang, Lunxi Cao, Wei Gao, Wei Wang
First: 2026-04-30T17:20:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:20:19+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 21 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous agents act through sandboxed containers and microVMs whose state spans filesystems, processes, and runtime artifacts. Checkpoint and restore (C/R) of this state is needed for fault tolerance, spot execution, RL rollout branching, and safe rollback-yet existing approaches fall into two extremes: application-level recovery preserves chat history but misses OS-side effects, while full per-turn checkpointing is correct but too expensive under dense co-location. The root cause is an agent-OS semantic gap: agent frameworks see tool calls but not their OS effects; the OS sees state changes but lacks turn-level context to judge recovery relevance. This gap hides massive sparsity: over 75% of agent turns produce no recovery-relevant state, so most checkpoints are unnecessary. Crab (Checkpoint-and-Restore for Agent SandBoxes) is a transparent host-side runtime that bridges this gap without modifying agents or C/R backends. An eBPF-based inspector classifies each turn's OS-visible effects to decide checkpoint granularity; a coordinator aligns checkpoints with turn boundaries and overlaps C/R with LLM wait time; and a host-scoped engine schedules checkpoint traffic across co-located sandboxes. On shell-intensive and code-repair workloads, Crab raises recovery correctness from 8% (chat-only) to 100%, cuts checkpoint traffic by up to 87%, and stays within 1.9% of fault-free execution time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Crab：面向智能体沙箱的语义感知检查点/恢复运行时
自主智能体通过沙箱化的容器和微虚拟机进行操作，其状态涵盖文件系统、进程及运行时工件。为实现容错、抢占式执行、强化学习轨迹分支及安全回滚，需对这些状态进行检查点与恢复（C/R）。然而现有方法存在两极：应用层恢复保留聊天历史但忽略操作系统层面影响；全量每轮检查点虽正确但成本过高，尤其在密集共置场景下。根本原因在于智能体与操作系统间的语义鸿沟：智能体框架仅感知工具调用，不感知其操作系统效应；操作系统虽能感知状态变化，却缺乏回合级上下文以判断恢复相关性。该鸿沟隐藏了巨大稀疏性：超过75%的智能体回合不产生恢复相关状态，故多数检查点实属冗余。Crab（面向智能体沙箱的检查点/恢复）是一种透明的宿主机侧运行时，无需修改智能体或C/R后端即可弥合此鸿沟。其基于eBPF的监控器对每回合操作系统可见效应进行分类，以决定检查点粒度；协调器将检查点对齐至回合边界，并将C/R与大语言模型等待时间重叠；宿主机级引擎则跨共置沙箱调度检查点流量。在以命令行密集型和代码修复为主的负载下，Crab将恢复正确率从8%（仅聊天）提升至100%，检查点流量减少高达87%，且运行时间仅比无故障执行慢1.9%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of existing checkpointing methods for agent sandboxes—either too coarse (missing OS effects) or too fine (overhead under dense co-location)—Crab introduces a host-side runtime that bridges the agent-OS semantic gap. It uses an eBPF-based inspector to classify each agent turn’s OS-visible effects, enabling granular checkpointing only when relevant; a coordinator aligns checkpoints with turn boundaries and overlaps them with LLM idle time; and a host-scoped engine schedules checkpoint traffic across co-located sandboxes. Experiments show Crab boosts recovery correctness from 8% (chat-only) to 100% while reducing checkpoint traffic by up to 87% and maintaining performance within 1.9% of fault-free execution time.
为解决现有代理沙箱检查点机制效率低下问题——要么过于粗粒度（忽略操作系统副作用），要么过于细粒度（密集共存时开销过大）——Crab 引入了主机侧运行时，弥合代理与操作系统之间的语义鸿沟。它通过 eBPF 检查器分类每个代理回合的可见操作系统效果，仅在必要时进行细粒度检查点；协调器将检查点对齐至回合边界，并在大语言模型等待时重叠执行检查点操作；主机级引擎调度多个沙箱的检查点流量。实验表明，Crab 在涉及 shell 和代码修复的工作负载上，将恢复正确率从 8%（仅聊天）提升至 100%，检查点流量减少高达 87%，且仅带来 1.9% 的执行时间开销。
Beyond Pixel Fidelity: Minimizing Perceptual Distortion and Color Bias in Night Photography Rendering
Authors: Furkan Kınlı
First: 2026-04-30T17:19:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:19:36+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 figures, Accepted to 2026 IEEE International Conference on Image Processing
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Night Photography Rendering (NPR) poses a significant challenge due to the extreme contrast between dark and illuminated areas in scenes, stemming from concurrent capture of severely dark regions alongside intense point light sources. Existing methods, which are mainly tailored for fidelity metrics, reveal considerable perceptual gaps and often detract from visual quality. We introduce pHVI-ISPNet, a novel RAW-to-RGB framework built on the robust HVI color space. Our network integrates four distinct key refinements: RAW-domain feature processing and Wavelet-based feature propagation to mitigate high-frequency detail loss; sample-based dynamic loss coefficients to ensure stable learning across varying exposure levels; and loss term based on feature distributions to maintain rigorous color constancy. Evaluations on the dataset introduced in the NTIRE 2025 challenge on NPR confirm our approach achieves competitive fidelity while establishing new state-of-the-art results in both CIE2000 color difference and LPIPS. This validates our perceptually-driven design for high-quality nighttime imaging.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越像素保真度：夜间摄影渲染中的感知失真与色彩偏差最小化
夜间摄影渲染（NPR）因场景中明暗区域对比极端而极具挑战性，源于同时捕捉极暗区域与强点光源。现有主要针对保真度指标的方法存在显著感知差距，常损害视觉质量。我们提出pHVI-ISPNet，一种基于稳健HVI色空间的新型RAW到RGB框架。网络包含四项关键改进：RAW域特征处理与小波特征传播以缓解高频细节损失；基于样本的动态损失系数确保不同曝光水平下的稳定学习；基于特征分布的损失项以维持严格的色彩恒常性。在NTIRE 2025挑战赛引入的数据集上评估，我们的方法在保真度方面表现优异，同时在CIE2000色差与LPIPS两项指标上均达到新SOTA，验证了我们以感知为导向的高质量夜间成像设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the perceptual shortcomings of existing night photography rendering methods that prioritize pixel fidelity over visual quality, this paper proposes pHVI-ISPNet, a RAW-to-RGB framework operating in the HVI color space. The method incorporates four key innovations: RAW-domain feature processing and wavelet-based propagation to preserve high-frequency details, sample-adaptive dynamic loss coefficients for stable training across exposure variations, and a feature distribution-based loss to enforce color constancy. Experimental results on the NTIRE 2025 NPR dataset demonstrate that pHVI-ISPNet achieves competitive fidelity while setting new state-of-the-art performance in CIE2000 color difference and LPIPS metrics, confirming its perceptual effectiveness for nighttime imaging.
为解决现有夜间摄影渲染方法过度追求像素保真度而忽视视觉感知质量的问题，作者提出pHVI-ISPNet，一种基于HVI色空间的RAW到RGB框架。该方法包含四项关键改进：RAW域特征处理与小波特征传播以保留高频细节，样本自适应动态损失系数以稳定不同曝光条件下的训练，以及基于特征分布的损失项以维持色彩恒定性。在NTIRE 2025 NPR数据集上的实验表明，pHVI-ISPNet在保持竞争力的保真度的同时，在CIE2000色差和LPIPS两项指标上达到当前最优，验证了其在夜间成像中的感知导向设计有效性。
3D-ReGen: A Unified 3D Geometry Regeneration Framework
Authors: Geon Yeong Park, Roman Shapovalov, Rakesh Ranjan, Jong Chul Ye, Andrea Vedaldi, Thu Nguyen-Phuoc
First: 2026-04-30T17:18:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:18:05+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 18 figures, 6 tables. Includes Appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider the problem of regenerating 3D objects from 2D images and initial 3D shapes. Most 3D generators operate in a one-shot fashion, converting text or images to a 3D object with limited controllability. We introduce instead 3D-ReGen, a 3D regenerator that is conditioned on an initial 3D shape. This conceptually simple formulation allows us to support numerous useful tasks, including 3D enhancement, reconstruction, and editing. 3D-ReGen uses a new conditioning mechanism based on VecSet, which allows the regenerator to update or improve the input geometry with consistent fine-grained details. 3D-ReGen learns a widely applicable regeneration prior from off-the-shelf 3D datasets via self-supervised pretext tasks and augmentations, without additional annotations. We evaluate both the geometric consistency and fine-grained quality of 3D-ReGen, achieving state-of-the-art performance in controllable 3D generation across several tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：3D-ReGen：一种统一的3D几何再生框架
我们研究从2D图像和初始3D形状再生3D物体的问题。大多数3D生成器采用一次性方式，将文本或图像转换为3D物体，但可控性有限。我们引入3D-ReGen，一种以初始3D形状为条件的3D再生器。这一概念上简单的公式使我们能够支持多种有用任务，包括3D增强、重建和编辑。3D-ReGen采用基于VecSet的新条件机制，使再生器能够以一致的细粒度细节更新或改进输入几何。3D-ReGen通过自监督预训练任务和数据增强，从现成的3D数据集中学习广泛适用的再生先验，无需额外标注。我们在几何一致性和细粒度质量方面评估3D-ReGen，在多个任务中实现了可控3D生成的最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for controllable and fine-grained 3D generation from 2D images and initial 3D shapes, 3D-ReGen introduces a unified framework that conditions regeneration on an initial 3D geometry, enabling tasks like enhancement, reconstruction, and editing. It employs a VecSet-based conditioning mechanism to update input shapes with consistent fine details and learns a general regeneration prior through self-supervised pretext tasks on off-the-shelf 3D datasets without additional annotations. Experimental results demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in geometric consistency and fine-grained quality across multiple controllable 3D generation tasks.
为实现从2D图像和初始3D形状可控且精细的3D生成，3D-ReGen提出一种统一框架，以初始3D几何作为条件，支持增强、重建与编辑等任务。其采用基于VecSet的条件机制，能对输入几何进行一致的细粒度更新，并通过自监督预训练任务从现成3D数据集中学习通用再生先验。实验结果在多个可控3D生成任务中达到当前最优的几何一致性与细节质量。
GraphMend: Code Transformations for Fixing Graph Breaks in PyTorch 2
Authors: Savini Kashmira, Jayanaka Dantanarayana, Thamirawaran Sathiyalogeswaran, Krisztian Flautner, Lingjia Tang, Jason Mars
First: 2025-09-17T17:15:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:17:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents GRAPHMEND, a high-level compiler technique that eliminates FX graph breaks in PyTorch 2 programs. Although PyTorch 2 introduced TorchDynamo and TorchInductor to enable just-in-time graph compilation, unresolved dynamic control flow and unsupported Python constructs often fragment models into multiple FX graphs. These fragments force frequent fallbacks to eager mode, introduce costly CPU-to-GPU synchronizations, and reduce optimization opportunities. GRAPHMEND addresses this limitation by analyzing and transforming source code before execution. Built on the Jaseci compilation framework, GRAPHMEND introduces two code transformations that remove graph breaks due to dynamic control flow and Python side effects. This design allows PyTorch's compilation pipeline to capture larger, uninterrupted FX graphs without requiring manual refactoring by developers. Evaluation across eight Hugging Face models shows that GRAPHMEND removes graph breaks due to dynamic control flow and Python side effects, reducing the break count to 0 in 6 models and reducing it from 5 to 2 in another model. On NVIDIA RTX 3090 and A40 GPUs, GRAPHMEND achieves up to 75% latency reductions and up to 8% higher end-to-end throughput. These results demonstrate that high-level code transformation is an effective complement to PyTorch's dynamic JIT compilation pipeline, substantially improving both usability and performance.

MoCapAnything V2: End-to-End Motion Capture for Arbitrary Skeletons
Authors: Kehong Gong, Zhengyu Wen, Dao Thien Phong, Mingxi Xu, Weixia He, Qi Wang, Ning Zhang, Zhengyu Li, Guanli Hou, Dongze Lian, Xiaoyu He, Mingyuan Zhang, Hanwang Zhang
First: 2026-04-30T17:16:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:16:38+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://animotionlab.github.io/MoCapAnythingV2/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent methods for arbitrary-skeleton motion capture from monocular video follow a factorized pipeline, where a Video-to-Pose network predicts joint positions and an analytical inverse-kinematics (IK) stage recovers joint rotations. While effective, this design is inherently limited, since joint positions do not fully determine rotations and leave degrees of freedom such as bone-axis twist ambiguous, and the non-differentiable IK stage prevents the system from adapting to noisy predictions or optimizing for the final animation objective. In this work, we present the first fully end-to-end framework in which both Video-to-Pose and Pose-to-Rotation are learnable and jointly optimized. We observe that the ambiguity in pose-to-rotation mapping arises from missing coordinate system information: the same joint positions can correspond to different rotations under different rest poses and local axis conventions. To resolve this, we introduce a reference pose-rotation pair from the target asset, which, together with the rest pose, not only anchors the mapping but also defines the underlying rotation coordinate system. This formulation turns rotation prediction into a well-constrained conditional problem and enables effective learning. In addition, our model predicts joint positions directly from video without relying on mesh intermediates, improving both robustness and efficiency. Both stages share a skeleton-aware Global-Local Graph-guided Multi-Head Attention (GL-GMHA) module for joint-level local reasoning and global coordination. Experiments on Truebones Zoo and Objaverse show that our method reduces rotation error from ~17 degrees to ~10 degrees, and to 6.54 degrees on unseen skeletons, while achieving ~20x faster inference than mesh-based pipelines. Project page: https://animotionlab.github.io/MoCapAnythingV2/

Latent Adversarial Detection: Adaptive Probing of LLM Activations for Multi-Turn Attack Detection
Authors: Prashant Kulkarni
First: 2026-04-30T17:16:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:16:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-turn prompt injection follows a known attack path -- trust-building, pivoting, escalation but text-level defenses miss covert attacks where individual turns appear benign. We show this attack path leaves an activation-level signature in the model's residual stream: each phase shift moves the activation, producing a total path length far exceeding benign conversations. We call this adversarial restlessness. Five scalar trajectory features capturing this signal lift conversation-level detection from 76.2% to 93.8% on synthetic held-out data. The signal replicates across four model families (24B-70B); probes are model-specific and do not transfer across architectures. Generalization is source-dependent: leave-one-source-out evaluation shows each of synthetic, LMSYS-Chat-1M, and SafeDialBench captures distinct attack distributions, with detection on real-world LMSYS reaching 47-71% when its distribution is represented in training. Combined three-source training achieves 89.4% detection at 2.4% false positive rate on a held-out mixed set. We further show that three-phase turn-level labels(benign/pivoting/adversarial) unique to our synthetic dataset are essential: binary conversation-level labels produce 50-59% false positives. These results establish adversarial restlessness as a reliable activation-level signal and characterize the data requirements for practical deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潜在对抗检测：通过自适应探测LLM激活实现多轮攻击检测
多轮提示注入攻击遵循已知路径——建立信任、转移、升级，但文本级防御机制无法识别隐蔽攻击，因为单轮内容看似无害。我们发现该攻击路径在模型残差流中留下激活级签名：每个阶段转换都会移动激活值，总路径长度远超良性对话。我们称之为对抗性躁动。五个标量轨迹特征捕捉该信号，使对话级检测准确率从76.2%提升至93.8%（在合成保留数据集上）。该信号在四个模型家族（24B-70B）中可复制；探测器模型特定，不跨架构迁移。泛化能力依赖数据源：留一源评估显示，合成数据、LMSYS-Chat-1M和SafeDialBench分别捕捉不同攻击分布，当真实世界LMSYS分布出现在训练集中时，检测准确率可达47-71%。三源联合训练在保留混合数据集上实现89.4%检测准确率，假阳性率仅2.4%。我们进一步证明，合成数据集独有的三阶段轮级标签（良性/转移/对抗性）至关重要：二分类对话级标签会导致50-59%的假阳性率。这些结果确立了对抗性躁动作为可靠的激活级信号，并刻画了实际部署所需的数据条件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of text-level defenses against multi-turn prompt injection attacks that appear benign per turn, the paper introduces 'adversarial restlessness' — a latent activation signature in LLM residual streams that accumulates across attack phases. The method uses five scalar trajectory features tracking activation movement during adversarial turns, which significantly improves detection from 76.2% to 93.8% on synthetic data.
