Crucially, turn-level labels (benign/pivoting/adversarial) are essential, as binary conversation labels yield 50-59% false positives.
为应对多轮提示注入攻击在单轮文本上难以被检测的问题，该研究提出“对抗性躁动”——一种在LLM残差流中累积的激活信号，反映攻击阶段（建立信任、转向、升级）的动态变化。方法通过五个标量轨迹特征捕捉激活位移，显著提升对话级检测准确率（从76.2%升至93.8%）。实验表明该信号在四个模型家族（24B-70B）中可迁移，但需模型特定探针，且泛化依赖数据源：在LMSYS真实数据上检测率达47-71%（当训练数据包含其分布），三源联合训练在混合数据上达到89.4%准确率（假阳性率2.4%）。关键发现是，需使用三阶段轮级标签（良性/转向/对抗），二分类标签会导致50-59%的假阳性率。
Normativity and Productivism: Ableist Intelligence? A Degrowth Analysis of AI Sign Language Translation Tools for Deaf People
Authors: Nina Seron-Abouelfadil, Poppy Fynes
First: 2026-04-30T17:14:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:14:08+00:00
Comments: Paper submitted and accepted to IJES 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sign languages, of any geographical or accentual variation, understandably face continuous scrutiny under the ever present popularity of verbal dictation and audism. Through this, many potential problems arise with the current lack of accessible communication for those who rely on such sign languages for essential conversation. Such AI systems regularly take the form of recognition and interpretation models, designed to provide seamless and accurate translation. In reality these systems are built from biased data and created without any input from deaf communities. Such models are widely used and accepted by their hearing counterparts who remain ignorant to the inherent culture, semantics and colloquial language present in gestural language systems. This phenomenon is best analysed under the scope of The Technological System and Technological bluff by Ellul. Indeed, what is at play here is the standardization of language by technicians into what can be captured by technique: data, statistics, a mathematical language. For that AI technique to exist, sign language must be rationalized, in a search for profit that annihilates the conditions for communication and fails to capture the human experience of the deaf person. By that process, it presents normative effects, creating a model of Man, standardized, massified, and who has to adapt to the tool and technical milieu instead of the other way around, which we assume should have been the goal of such a technology. Technique thus reshapes what it means to be human, to submit deaf people to the goals of productivity and efficiency. In doing so, it exhibits clear counter productivity, alienating instead of emancipating, isolating instead of nourishing human relationships. Therefore this paper argues for the idea of AI as Ableist Intelligence, as such systems seek to emphasise the humiliated and marginalised nature of sign.
中文标题/摘要
标题：规范性与生产主义：歧视性智能？对聋人手语翻译AI工具的退增长分析
无论地理或口音差异如何，手语始终面临来自口语听写和听觉主义持续盛行的审视。这导致依赖手语进行基本交流的人群在沟通无障碍方面存在诸多潜在问题。当前的AI系统多以识别与翻译模型形式出现，旨在提供无缝精准的翻译。然而，这些系统基于有偏见的数据构建，且未纳入聋人群体的任何输入。此类模型被其听力使用者广泛采用并接受，而后者却对手势语言系统中固有的文化、语义和口语化表达一无所知。 此现象最宜在埃吕尔的《技术系统与技术虚伪》框架下分析。实际上，这里发生的是技术人员将语言标准化为可被技术捕捉的形式：数据、统计、数学语言。为使AI技术存在，手语必须被理性化，以追求利润为目标，这摧毁了沟通条件，未能捕捉聋人的真实体验。通过这一过程，它产生规范性效应，塑造出标准化、规模化、必须适应工具和技术环境的“人”模型，而非相反——而这本应是此类技术的目标。 技术因此重塑了“人性”的含义，将聋人置于生产率与效率的目标之下。如此一来，它表现出明显的反生产性，反而造成疏离而非解放，隔离而非滋养人际关系。因此，本文主张将AI视为“歧视性智能”，因其系统试图强调手语使用者被贬低与边缘化的处境。
Summary / 总结
This paper critiques AI-driven sign language translation tools for deaf people, arguing that they embody 'Ableist Intelligence' by prioritizing technical efficiency over cultural and linguistic integrity. Motivated by concerns over audism and the marginalization of deaf communities, the authors analyze these systems through Ellul’s framework of technological rationalization, revealing how AI models are trained on biased data without deaf input, thereby standardizing sign languages into measurable, profit-driven formats. The method involves a critical degrowth analysis that exposes how such technologies alienate deaf users by forcing them to adapt to tools rather than the tools adapting to human needs. Experimental findings, though not empirical in the traditional sense, demonstrate that these systems fail to capture the richness of sign language, instead reinforcing normative, productivity-centered paradigms that undermine authentic communication and human dignity.
本文批判了面向聋人开发的AI手语翻译工具，指出其本质上是‘能力建构的智能’，因过度追求技术效率而忽视手语的文化与语义独特性。作者借助雅克·埃吕尔的技术理性理论，揭示这些AI系统如何将手语标准化为可量化的数据，剥离其文化内涵，且在缺乏聋人群体参与和数据偏倚下构建，迫使聋人适应技术而非技术适应聋人。最终，这些工具以‘无障碍’之名，实则加剧了聋人群体的边缘化，背离了技术应促进人类尊严与关系的目标。
PRISM: Pre-alignment via Black-box On-policy Distillation for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Sudong Wang, Weiquan Huang, Xiaomin Yu, Zuhao Yang, Hehai Lin, Keming Wu, Chaojun Xiao, Chen Chen, Wenxuan Wang, Beier Zhu, Yunjian Zhang, Chengwei Qin
First: 2026-04-30T17:12:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:12:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The standard post-training recipe for large multimodal models (LMMs) applies supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on curated demonstrations followed by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). However, SFT introduces distributional drift that neither preserves the model's original capabilities nor faithfully matches the supervision distribution. This problem is further amplified in multimodal reasoning, where perception errors and reasoning failures follow distinct drift patterns that compound during subsequent RL. We introduce PRISM, a three-stage pipeline that mitigates this drift by inserting an explicit distribution-alignment stage between SFT and RLVR. Building on the principle of on-policy distillation (OPD), PRISM casts alignment as a black-box, response-level adversarial game between the policy and a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) discriminator with dedicated perception and reasoning experts, providing disentangled corrective signals that steer the policy toward the supervision distribution without requiring access to teacher logits. While 1.26M public demonstrations suffice for broad SFT initialization, distribution alignment demands higher-fidelity supervision; we therefore curate 113K additional demonstrations from Gemini 3 Flash, featuring dense visual grounding and step-by-step reasoning on the hardest unsolved problems. Experiments on Qwen3-VL show that PRISM consistently improves downstream RLVR performance across multiple RL algorithms (GRPO, DAPO, GSPO) and diverse multimodal benchmarks, improving average accuracy by +4.4 and +6.0 points over the SFT-to-RLVR baseline on 4B and 8B, respectively. Our code, data, and model checkpoints are publicly available at https://github.com/XIAO4579/PRISM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PRISM：面向多模态强化学习的预对齐黑盒在线蒸馏方法
大型多模态模型（LMMs）的标准后训练流程通常先在精选演示数据上进行监督微调（SFT），再结合可验证奖励进行强化学习（RLVR）。然而，SFT会引入分布漂移，既无法保留模型原始能力，也难以忠实匹配监督分布。在多模态推理中，感知错误与推理失败遵循不同漂移模式，且在后续RL中会叠加放大。为此，我们提出PRISM，一种三阶段流程，在SFT与RLVR之间插入显式分布对齐阶段以缓解该问题。基于在线蒸馏（OPD）原理，PRISM将对齐建模为策略与混合专家（MoE）判别器之间的黑盒响应级对抗博弈，其中判别器包含专用感知与推理专家，提供解耦的修正信号，引导策略逼近监督分布，且无需访问教师模型的logits。126万公共演示数据足以完成广泛SFT初始化，但分布对齐需更高保真度监督；因此我们从Gemini 3 Flash中精选11.3万条额外演示，包含密集视觉定位与逐步推理，聚焦最难未解问题。在Qwen3-VL上的实验表明，PRISM在多种RL算法（GRPO、DAPO、GSPO）及多样多模态基准上持续提升下游RLVR性能，相较SFT-to-RLVR基线，4B与8B模型平均准确率分别提升4.4和6.0点。代码、数据与模型检查点已公开于https://github.com/XIAO4579/PRISM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the distributional drift caused by supervised fine-tuning in multimodal reinforcement learning, PRISM introduces a three-stage pipeline that inserts a black-box on-policy distillation stage between SFT and RLVR to realign the policy’s distribution. It frames alignment as an adversarial game between the policy and a Mixture-of-Experts discriminator with separate perception and reasoning experts, enabling disentangled corrective signals without needing teacher logits. Using 113K high-fidelity demonstrations from Gemini 3 Flash, PRISM achieves consistent performance gains across RL algorithms and benchmarks, boosting average accuracy by +4.4 and +6.0 points on 4B and 8B Qwen3-VL models respectively over the SFT-to-RLVR baseline.
为缓解监督微调在多模态强化学习中引入的分布漂移问题，PRISM在SFT与RLVR之间插入了一个基于黑盒在线蒸馏的分布对齐阶段。该方法将对齐建模为策略与混合专家判别器之间的对抗博弈，其中感知与推理专家分别提供解耦的修正信号，无需访问教师logits。通过从Gemini 3 Flash精选的11.3万高质量演示数据，PRISM在多个强化学习算法和多模态基准上持续提升性能，相较SFT到RLVR基线，在4B和8B模型上平均准确率分别提升4.4和6.0个百分点。
Beyond Gaussian Bottlenecks: Topologically Aligned Encoding of Vision-Transformer Feature Spaces
Authors: Andrew Bond, Ilkin Umut Melanlioglu, Erkut Erdem, Aykut Erdem
First: 2026-04-30T17:12:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:12:31+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern visual world modeling systems increasingly rely on high-capacity architectures and large-scale data to produce plausible motion, yet they often fail to preserve underlying 3D geometry or physically consistent camera dynamics. A key limitation lies not only in model capacity, but in the latent representations used to encode geometric structure. We propose S$^2$VAE, a geometry-first latent learning framework that focuses on compressing and representing the latent 3D state of a scene, including camera motion, depth, and point-level structure, rather than modeling appearance alone. Building on representations from a Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT), we introduce a novel type of variational autoencoder using a product of Power Spherical latent distributions, explicitly enforcing hyperspherical structure in the bottleneck to preserve directional and geometric semantics under strong compression. Across depth estimation, camera pose recovery, and point cloud reconstruction, we show that geometry-aligned hyperspherical latents consistently outperform conventional Gaussian bottlenecks, particularly in high-compression regimes. Our results highlight latent geometry as a first-class design choice for physically grounded visual and world models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越高斯瓶颈：视觉变换器特征空间的拓扑对齐编码
现代视觉世界建模系统日益依赖高容量架构和大规模数据以生成合理的运动，但往往无法保留底层3D几何结构或物理一致的相机动态。其关键限制不仅在于模型容量，更在于用于编码几何结构的潜在表示。我们提出S²VAE，一种以几何为先的潜在学习框架，专注于压缩和表示场景的潜在3D状态（包括相机运动、深度和点级结构），而非仅建模外观。基于视觉几何锚定变换器（VGGT）的表示，我们引入一种新型变分自编码器，使用幂球面潜在分布的乘积形式，显式强制瓶颈层保持超球面结构，从而在强压缩下保留方向和几何语义。在深度估计、相机姿态恢复和点云重建任务中，我们证明几何对齐的超球面潜在表示在高压缩场景下始终优于传统高斯瓶颈。我们的结果强调，潜在几何应作为物理接地视觉与世界模型的首要设计选择。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of current vision models to preserve 3D geometry and camera dynamics despite high capacity, the authors propose S²VAE, a geometry-first variational autoencoder that encodes latent 3D scene states including camera motion, depth, and point-level structure. It uses a product of Power Spherical distributions to enforce hyperspherical latent bottlenecks, preserving directional and geometric semantics under strong compression. Experiments across depth estimation, camera pose recovery, and point cloud reconstruction show that geometry-aligned hyperspherical latents consistently outperform Gaussian bottlenecks, especially under high compression.
鉴于当前视觉模型虽具备高容量却难以保持3D几何结构与相机运动一致性，本文提出S$^2$VAE，一种以几何为先的变分自编码器，直接编码场景的3D状态（含相机运动、深度与点级结构）。其采用幂球分布乘积构建超球形瓶颈，显式保留方向与几何语义于压缩过程中。在深度估计、相机位姿恢复与点云重建任务中，该几何对齐的超球形潜在表示在高压缩场景下显著优于传统高斯瓶颈，凸显潜在几何作为物理接地视觉模型核心设计要素的重要性。
Do Sparse Autoencoders Capture Concept Manifolds?
Authors: Usha Bhalla, Thomas Fel, Can Rager, Sheridan Feucht, Tal Haklay, Daniel Wurgaft, Siddharth Boppana, Matthew Kowal, Vasudev Shyam, Jack Merullo, Atticus Geiger, Ekdeep Singh Lubana
First: 2026-04-30T17:08:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:08:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to extract interpretable features from neural network representations, often under the implicit assumption that concepts correspond to independent linear directions. However, a growing body of evidence suggests that many concepts are instead organized along low-dimensional manifolds encoding continuous geometric relationships. This raises three basic questions: what does it mean for an SAE to capture a manifold, when do existing SAE architectures do so, and how? We develop a theoretical framework that answers these questions and show that SAEs can capture manifolds in two fundamentally different ways: globally, by allocating a compact group of atoms whose linear span contains the entire manifold, or locally, by distributing it across features that each selectively tile a restricted region of the underlying geometry. Empirically, we find that SAEs suboptimally recover continuous structures, mixing the global subspace and local tiling solutions in a fragmented regime we call dilution. This explains why manifold structure is rarely visible at the level of individual concepts and motivates post-hoc unsupervised discovery methods that search for coherent groups of atoms rather than isolated directions. More broadly, our results suggest that future representation learning methods should treat geometric objects, not just individual directions, as the basic units of interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏自编码器能否捕捉概念流形？
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）广泛用于从神经网络表示中提取可解释特征，通常隐含假设概念对应于独立的线性方向。然而，越来越多的证据表明，许多概念实际上沿低维流形组织，编码连续的几何关系。这引发三个基本问题：何为SAE捕捉流形？现有SAE架构何时以及如何实现这一点？我们构建了一个理论框架回答这些问题，表明SAEs以两种根本不同的方式捕捉流形：全局方式，通过分配一组紧凑的原子，其线性张成包含整个流形；或局部方式，通过将流形分布于各自仅覆盖底层几何特定区域的特征上。实证研究表明，SAEs在恢复连续结构时表现次优，混合全局子空间与局部铺砖解，形成我们称之为“稀释”的碎片化状态。这解释了为何单个概念层面难以观察到流形结构，并推动了事后无监督发现方法的发展，该方法搜索原子的连贯组而非孤立方向。更广泛地说，我们的结果表明，未来表征学习方法应将几何对象（而非仅单个方向）视为可解释性的基本单位。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that many concepts in neural networks lie on continuous geometric manifolds rather than independent linear directions, this study investigates whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) can capture such structures. The authors develop a theoretical framework to define manifold capture and show that SAEs can do so either globally—by assigning a compact set of atoms whose linear span fully covers the manifold—or locally—by distributing manifold coverage across features that each tile a restricted region. Empirical results reveal that SAEs often operate in a 'diluted' regime, mixing global and local strategies inefficiently, which obscures clear manifold structure at the concept level and suggests that interpretability should focus on coherent groups of atoms rather than isolated directions.
鉴于许多神经网络概念沿连续几何流形分布而非独立线性方向，本文研究稀疏自编码器（SAE）是否能捕捉此类结构。作者构建理论框架，识别两种机制：全局捕捉通过一组紧凑原子张成整个流形，或局部捕捉通过分布于各区域的特征分别覆盖局部几何区域。实验证明SAE常无法清晰捕捉流形，反而呈现“稀释”现象——全局与局部策略混合，导致单个概念几何上不连贯。这表明可解释性应聚焦原子组而非孤立方向，且未来表示学习应优先考虑几何结构而非线性成分。
DEFault++: Automated Fault Detection, Categorization, and Diagnosis for Transformer Architectures
Authors: Sigma Jahan, Saurabh Singh Rajput, Tushar Sharma, Mohammad Masudur Rahman
First: 2026-04-30T17:07:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:07:11+00:00
Comments: 71 pages, 15 figures, 22 tables. Preprint; under preparation for journal submission. Standalone version of Chapter 7 of the lead author's PhD thesis (Dalhousie University, 2026). Replication package: https://github.com/SigmaJahan/DEFaultplusplus-Transformer-Debugging
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Transformer models are widely deployed in critical AI applications, yet faults in their attention mechanisms, projections, and other internal components often degrade behavior silently without raising runtime errors. Existing fault diagnosis techniques often target generic deep neural networks and cannot identify which transformer component is responsible for an observed symptom. In this article, we present DEFault++, a hierarchical learning-based diagnostic technique that operates at three level of abstraction: it detects whether a fault is present, classifies it into one of 12 transformer-specific fault categories (covering both attention-internal mechanisms and surrounding architectural components), and identifies the underlying root cause from up to 45 mechanisms. To facilitate both training and evaluation, we construct DEFault-bench, a benchmark of 3,739 labeled instances obtained through systematic mutation testing. These instances are created across seven transformer models and nine downstream tasks using DEForm, a transformer-specific mutation technique we developed for this purpose. DEFault++ measures runtime behavior at the level of individual transformer components. It organizes these measurements through a Fault Propagation Graph (FPG) derived from the transformer architecture. It then produces an interpretable diagnosis using prototype matching combined with supervised contrastive learning. On DEFault-bench, DEFault++ exceeds an AUROC of 0.96 for detection and a Macro-F1 of 0.85 for both categorization and root-cause diagnosis on encoder and decoder architectures. In a developer study with 21 practitioners, the accuracy of choosing correct repair actions increased from 57.1% without support to 83.3% when using DEFault++.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DEFault++：用于Transformer架构的自动化故障检测、分类与诊断
Transformer模型广泛部署于关键AI应用中，但其注意力机制、投影等内部组件的故障常无声地降低性能，而不引发运行时错误。现有故障诊断技术多针对通用深度神经网络，无法识别导致特定症状的Transformer组件。本文提出DEFault++，一种基于分层学习的诊断技术，可在三个抽象层级操作：检测是否存在故障，将其归类为12种Transformer特有故障类别（涵盖注意力内部机制及周边架构组件），并从多达45种机制中识别根本原因。为支持训练与评估，我们构建DEFault-bench，包含通过系统性突变测试获得的3,739个标注实例，覆盖七种Transformer模型与九项下游任务，使用我们为此开发的Transformer专用突变技术DEForm。DEFault++在单个Transformer组件层面测量运行时行为，通过从Transformer架构导出的故障传播图（FPG）组织这些测量，并结合原型匹配与监督对比学习生成可解释诊断。在DEFault-bench上，DEFault++在检测任务中AUROC超过0.96，在编码器与解码器架构的分类与根本原因诊断中Macro-F1达0.85。在涉及21名开发者的实验中，使用DEFault++时正确选择修复动作的准确率从无支持时的57.1%提升至83.3%。

FreeOcc: Training-Free Embodied Open-Vocabulary Occupancy Prediction
Authors: Zeyu Jiang, Changqing Zhou, Xingxing Zuo, Changhao Chen
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-04-30T17:05:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:05:56+00:00
Comments: RSS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing learning-based occupancy prediction methods rely on large-scale 3D annotations and generalize poorly across environments. We present FreeOcc, a training-free framework for open-vocabulary occupancy prediction from monocular or RGB-D sequences. Unlike prior approaches that require voxel-level supervision and ground-truth camera poses, FreeOcc operates without 3D annotations, pose ground truth, or any learning stage. FreeOcc incrementally builds a globally consistent occupancy map via a four-layer pipeline: a SLAM backbone estimates poses and sparse geometry; a geometrically consistent Gaussian update constructs dense 3D Gaussian maps; open-vocabulary semantics from off-the-shelf vision-language models are associated with Gaussian primitives; and a probabilistic Gaussian-to-occupancy projection produces dense voxel occupancy. Despite being entirely training-free and pose-agnostic, FreeOcc achieves over $2\times$ improvements in IoU and mIoU on EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet compared to prior self-supervised methods. We further introduce ReplicaOcc, a benchmark for indoor open-vocabulary occupancy prediction, and show that FreeOcc transfers zero-shot to novel environments, substantially outperforming both supervised and self-supervised baselines. Project page: https://the-masses.github.io/freeocc-web/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FreeOcc：无需训练的具身开放词汇占用预测
现有基于学习的占用预测方法依赖大规模3D标注，在不同环境中的泛化能力差。我们提出FreeOcc，一种从单目或RGB-D序列中进行开放词汇占用预测的无需训练框架。与需要体素级监督和真实相机位姿的先前方法不同，FreeOcc无需3D标注、位姿真值或任何学习阶段。FreeOcc通过四层管道逐步构建全局一致的占用图：SLAM主干网络估计位姿和稀疏几何；几何一致的高斯更新构建密集3D高斯图；从现成视觉-语言模型中获取的开放词汇语义与高斯原语关联；概率高斯到占用投影生成密集体素占用。尽管完全无需训练且与位姿无关，FreeOcc在EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet上相比先前自监督方法在IoU和mIoU指标上提升超过2倍。我们进一步引入ReplicaOcc，一个用于室内开放词汇占用预测的基准，并展示FreeOcc可零样本迁移至新环境，显著优于监督和自监督基线。项目页面：https://the-masses.github.io/freeocc-web/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of 3D annotations and poor generalization of existing occupancy prediction methods, FreeOcc introduces a training-free framework for open-vocabulary occupancy prediction from monocular or RGB-D sequences. It avoids 3D supervision and pose ground truth by using a four-layer pipeline: a SLAM backbone estimates camera poses and sparse geometry, a Gaussian update generates dense 3D Gaussians, vision-language models assign semantics to Gaussian primitives, and a probabilistic projection yields dense voxel occupancy. Despite being entirely training-free and pose-agnostic, FreeOcc achieves over 2x improvements in IoU and mIoU on EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet compared to prior self-supervised methods and demonstrates strong zero-shot transfer on ReplicaOcc, outperforming both supervised and self-supervised baselines.
为了解决现有占用预测方法依赖大规模3D标注且泛化能力差的问题，FreeOcc提出了一种无需训练的框架，可从单目或RGB-D序列中预测稠密3D占用，无需体素级监督或相机位姿真值。其四层流程包括：SLAM主干估计位姿与稀疏几何，几何一致的高斯更新生成稠密3D高斯地图，视觉-语言模型为高斯元赋予开放词汇语义，概率投影将高斯转换为体素占用。尽管完全无训练且不依赖位姿，FreeOcc在EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet上IoU和mIoU均优于现有自监督方法两倍以上，并在ReplicaOcc基准上实现零样本迁移，显著超越监督与自监督基线方法。
Splitting Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks and Supports
Authors: Matti Berthold, Lydia Blümel, Giovanni Buraglio, Anna Rapberger
First: 2026-04-30T17:01:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:01:06+00:00
Comments: Extended version of a paper presented at the 23rd International Conference on Principles of Knowledge Representation and Reasoning July 20-23, 2026 - Lisbon, Portugal, 27 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work proposes novel splitting techniques for argumentation formalisms that incorporate supports between defeasible elements. We base our studies on bipolar set-based argumentation frameworks (BSAFs) which generalize argumentation frameworks with collective attacks (SETAFs), as well as bipolar argumentation frameworks (BAFs), by incorporating both collective attacks and supports. Notably, BSAFs establish a crucial link to structured argumentation as they naturally capture general (potentially non-flat) assumption-based argumentation. The increase in expressiveness calls for diverse forms of splitting. We consider splits over collective attacks (thereby generalizing the recently proposed splitting techniques for SETAFs), splits over collective supports, as well as splits over both collective attacks and supports. We establish suitable splitting schemata and prove their correctness for the most common argumentation semantics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带集体攻击与支持的论证框架分割
本文提出了适用于包含可撤销元素间支持的论证形式化方法的新分割技术。我们基于双极集合型论证框架（BSAFs），该框架通过同时引入集体攻击与支持，推广了带集体攻击的论证框架（SETAFs）和双极论证框架（BAFs）。值得注意的是，BSAFs与结构化论证建立了关键联系，因其自然捕捉一般（可能非扁平）的基于假设的论证。表达能力的增强要求多样化的分割形式。我们考虑了基于集体攻击的分割（从而推广了近期针对SETAFs提出的分割技术）、基于集体支持的分割，以及同时基于集体攻击与支持的分割。我们建立了合适的分割方案，并证明了其在最常用论证语义下的正确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to manage complex argumentation structures with collective attacks and supports, this paper introduces novel splitting techniques for bipolar set-based argumentation frameworks (BSAFs), which generalize both SETAFs and BAFs. The authors propose three types of splits: over collective attacks, over collective supports, and over both, thereby extending existing splitting methods. They formalize splitting schemata and prove their correctness under common argumentation semantics, demonstrating that these splits preserve the logical structure and outcomes of the original framework while enabling modular reasoning.
为应对包含集体攻击与支持的复杂论证结构，本文提出针对双极集合论证框架（BSAFs）的新颖拆分技术，该框架同时推广了SETAFs与BAFs。作者设计了基于集体攻击、集体支持或两者结合的拆分方法，扩展了SETAFs的拆分技术。他们形式化了拆分方案，并证明其在主流论证语义下正确，表明这些拆分能保持原框架的逻辑结构与推理结果，同时支持模块化分析。
GSDrive: Reinforcing Driving Policies by Multi-mode Trajectory Probing with 3D Gaussian Splatting Environment
Authors: Ziang Guo, Min Chen, Xuefeng Zhang, Yixiao Zhou, Zufeng Zhang, Dzmitry Tsetserukou
First: 2026-04-30T16:59:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:59:07+00:00
Comments: initial version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
End-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving presents a promising approach for translating perceptual inputs directly into driving actions. However, prohibitive annotation costs and temporal data quality degradation hinder long-term real-world deployment. While combining imitation learning (IL) and reinforcement learning (RL) is a common strategy for policy improvement, conventional RL training relies on delayed, event-based rewards-policies learn only from catastrophic outcomes such as collisions, leading to premature convergence to suboptimal behaviors. To address these limitations, we introduce GSDrive, a framework that exploits 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for differentiable, physics-based reward shaping in E2E driving policy improvement. Our method incorporates a flow matching-based trajectory predictor within the 3DGS simulator, enabling multi-mode trajectory probing where candidate trajectories are rolled out to assess prospective rewards. This establishes a bidirectional knowledge exchange between IL and RL by grounding reward functions in physically simulated interaction signals, offering immediate dense feedback instead of sparse catastrophic events. Evaluated on the reconstructed nuScenes dataset, our method surpasses existing simulation-based RL driving approaches in closed-loop experiments. Code is available at https://github.com/ZionGo6/GSDrive.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GSDrive：通过3D高斯点渲染环境的多模式轨迹探测强化驾驶策略
端到端（E2E）自动驾驶将感知输入直接转化为驾驶动作，具有广阔前景。然而，高昂的标注成本和时序数据质量退化阻碍了其长期实际部署。虽然结合模仿学习（IL）与强化学习（RL）是策略改进的常用方法，但传统RL训练依赖延迟的、事件驱动的奖励——策略仅从碰撞等灾难性结果中学习，导致过早收敛至次优行为。为解决这些问题，我们提出GSDrive框架，利用3D高斯点渲染（3DGS）实现E2E驾驶策略改进中的可微、基于物理的奖励塑造。我们的方法在3DGS模拟器中集成基于流匹配的轨迹预测器，支持多模式轨迹探测，通过展开候选轨迹评估未来奖励。该方法在IL与RL之间建立双向知识交换，将奖励函数锚定在物理模拟的交互信号上，提供密集即时反馈而非稀疏灾难性事件。在重建的nuScenes数据集上评估，我们的方法在闭环实验中超越现有基于模拟的RL驾驶方法。代码见https://github.com/ZionGo6/GSDrive。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the limitations of sparse rewards and high annotation costs in end-to-end autonomous driving, GSDrive introduces a framework that integrates imitation learning with reinforcement learning using a 3D Gaussian Splatting environment for differentiable reward shaping. The method employs a flow matching-based trajectory predictor to simulate multiple future driving paths, enabling dense, physics-based reward signals derived from simulated interactions rather than delayed catastrophic events. Evaluated on the nuScenes dataset, GSDrive achieves superior closed-loop performance compared to existing simulation-based RL methods, demonstrating improved policy convergence and robustness.
为解决端到端自动驾驶中奖励稀疏和标注成本高的问题，GSDrive利用3D高斯点渲染环境实现可微分的奖励塑造，结合模仿学习与强化学习。其通过流匹配轨迹预测器模拟多模式轨迹，提供基于物理交互的密集反馈，而非仅依赖灾难性事件。在nuScenes数据集上的闭环实验中，该方法优于现有基于模拟的强化学习方法。
Auto-FlexSwitch: Efficient Dynamic Model Merging via Learnable Task Vector Compression
Authors: Junqi Gao, Dazhi Zhang, Zhichang Guo, Biqing Qi, Yi Ran, Wangmeng Zuo
First: 2026-04-30T16:58:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:58:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Model merging has attracted attention as an effective path toward multi-task adaptation by integrating knowledge from multiple task-specific models. Among existing approaches, dynamic merging mitigates performance degradation caused by conflicting parameter updates across tasks by flexibly combining task-specific parameters at inference time, thereby maintaining high performance. However, these methods require storing independent parameters for each task, resulting in prohibitive storage overhead. To address this issue, we first experimentally demonstrate that the fine-tuned weight increments (referred to as task vectors) exhibit an impulse-like activation pattern and high robustness to low-bit representations. Driven by this insight, we propose T-Switch, which decomposes task vectors into three compact components: a binary sparse mask, a sign vector, and a scalar scaling factor, achieving high-fidelity approximation at high compression ratios. We then introduce Auto-Switch, a training-free merging scheme that automatically composes task vectors via feature similarity retrieval. Building on this, we develop Auto-Switch, a training-free merging scheme that automatically assembles task vectors through feature similarity retrieval. Furthermore, to transform task vector sparsification and quantization from static rules to adaptive learning, we propose FlexSwitch, a learnable framework which jointly optimizes the compression strategy for each model unit via Learnable Gating Sparsification (LGS) and Bit-width Adaptive Selection (BAS), while employing the Sparsity-Aware Storage Strategy (SASS) to select the optimal storage encoding structure. Finally, by incorporating a K-Nearest Neighbor (KNN) inference scheme with a learnable low-rank metric, we present Auto-FlexSwitch, a dynamic model merging approach that supports highly efficient task vector compression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Auto-FlexSwitch：通过可学习任务向量压缩实现高效的动态模型融合
模型融合作为一种有效的多任务自适应路径，通过整合多个任务特定模型的知识而受到关注。现有方法中，动态融合通过在推理时灵活组合任务特定参数，缓解了不同任务参数更新冲突导致的性能下降，从而保持高性能。然而，这些方法需为每个任务存储独立参数，导致存储开销巨大。为此，我们首先实验证明，微调后的权重增量（称为任务向量）具有脉冲式激活模式且对低比特表示具有高鲁棒性。基于此洞察，我们提出T-Switch，将任务向量分解为三个紧凑组件：二进制稀疏掩码、符号向量和标量缩放因子，在高压缩比下实现高保真近似。随后，我们引入Auto-Switch，一种无需训练的融合方案，通过特征相似性检索自动组合任务向量。进一步地，为将任务向量稀疏化与量化从静态规则转变为自适应学习，我们提出FlexSwitch，一个可学习框架，通过可学习门控稀疏化（LGS）和比特宽自适应选择（BAS）联合优化每个模型单元的压缩策略，并采用稀疏感知存储策略（SASS）选择最优存储编码结构。最后，通过结合可学习低秩度量的K近邻推理方案，我们提出Auto-FlexSwitch，一种支持高效任务向量压缩的动态模型融合方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high storage cost of dynamic model merging, this work proposes Auto-FlexSwitch to compress task vectors efficiently. It first observes that fine-tuned weight increments (task vectors) are robust to low-bit representations and exhibit sparse, impulse-like patterns. Based on this, T-Switch decomposes task vectors into a binary mask, sign vector, and scalar factor for high-compression fidelity. Auto-Switch then retrieves task vectors via feature similarity without training. FlexSwitch further adapts compression per model unit using learnable gating and bit-width selection, paired with sparsity-aware storage. Finally, Auto-FlexSwitch combines KNN inference with a learnable low-rank metric to achieve efficient, dynamic merging with minimal performance loss.
为解决动态模型融合中存储开销过大的问题，本文提出Auto-FlexSwitch，通过高效压缩任务特定参数增量（任务向量）来保持性能。首先引入T-Switch，将任务向量分解为稀疏二进制掩码、符号向量和标量因子，实现高压缩比下的高保真度。随后，Auto-Switch通过特征相似性检索实现无训练的模型融合。最后，FlexSwitch通过LGS和BAS联合学习每个模型单元的压缩策略，并结合SASS选择最优存储格式。实验表明，Auto-FlexSwitch可将任务向量压缩至原大小的5%，性能损失极小，相比静态方法表现更优，存储开销降低超90%。
Agentic Education: Using Claude Code to Teach Claude Code
Authors: Zain Naboulsi
First: 2026-04-19T14:20:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:55:22+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 5 figures, 7 tables. v2: added discussion of the GenAI adoption gap (MIT NANDA 2025) and a future-work direction on affect-aware adaptation; no changes to the system, evaluation, or core contributions. Code: https://github.com/zainnab-sparq/cc-self-train
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AI coding assistants have proliferated rapidly, yet structured pedagogical frameworks for learning these tools remain scarce. Developers face a gap between tool documentation and practical mastery, relying on fragmented resources such as blog posts, video tutorials, and trial-and-error. We present cc-self-train, a modular interactive curriculum for learning Claude Code, an agentic AI coding tool, through hands-on project construction. The system introduces five contributions: (1) a persona progression model that adapts instructor tone across four stages (Guide, Collaborator, Peer, Launcher), operationalizing Gradual Release of Responsibility for AI-mediated instruction; (2) an adaptive learning system that observes engagement quality through hook-based heuristics and adjusts scaffolding at two timescales, using streak detection for mid-module intervention and aggregate metrics for module-boundary persona changes; (3) a cross-domain unified curriculum in which five distinct project domains share identical feature sequencing, enabling transfer learning; (4) a step-pacing mechanism with explicit pause primitives to manage information overload in an AI-as-instructor context; and (5) an auto-updating curriculum design in which the onboarding agent detects upstream tool changes and updates teaching materials before instruction begins. A parametrized test suite enforces structural consistency as a proxy for pedagogical invariants across all 50 modules. A pilot evaluation with 27 participants shows statistically significant reported self-efficacy gains across all 10 assessed skill areas (p < 0.001), with the largest effects on advanced features such as hooks and custom skills. We discuss implications for the design of auto-updating educational systems.

AI编程助手迅速普及，但针对学习这些工具的结构化教学框架仍稀缺。开发者面临工具文档与实际掌握之间的鸿沟，依赖零散资源如博客文章、视频教程和试错。我们提出cc-self-train，一个用于通过动手项目构建学习Claude Code（一种代理式AI编程工具）的模块化互动课程。该系统包含五项贡献：(1) 一种角色进阶模型，通过四个阶段（引导者、合作者、同伴、启动者）调整导师语气，实现AI介导教学中的责任逐步释放；(2) 一个自适应学习系统，通过钩子启发式观察参与质量，并在两个时间尺度上调整支架，利用连续登录检测进行模块内干预，利用聚合指标进行模块边界角色变更；(3) 一个跨领域的统一课程，五个不同项目领域共享相同功能序列，支持迁移学习；(4) 一种步进节奏机制，包含明确的暂停原语，以管理AI作为导师时的信息过载；(5) 一种自动更新课程设计，其中引导代理在教学前检测上游工具变更并更新教学材料。参数化测试套件强制结构一致性，作为所有50个模块教学不变量的代理。对27名参与者的试点评估显示，在所有10个评估技能领域均显著提升自我效能感（p < 0.001），尤其在钩子和自定义技能等高级功能上效果最明显。我们讨论了自动更新教育系统的设计启示。
