Neural Aided Kalman Filtering for UAV State Estimation in Degraded Sensing Environments
Authors: Akhil Gupta, Erhan Guven
First: 2026-04-30T16:55:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:55:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate state estimation of nonlinear dynamical systems is fundamental to modern aerospace operations across air, sea, and space domains. Online tracking of adversarial unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is especially challenging due to agile nonlinear motion, noisy and sparse sensor measurements, and unknown control inputs; conditions that violate key assumptions of classical Kalman filter variants and degrade estimation performance. Neural networks (NNs) can learn complex nonlinear relationships from data, but lack principled uncertainty quantification, which is critical for state estimation tasks where confidence bounds drive downstream decisions. We address this with Bayesian Neural Networks (BNNs), which model uncertainty through distributions over network weights and produce predictive means and uncertainties via Monte Carlo sampling. Building on this, we propose the Bayesian Neural Kalman Filter (BNKF): a hybrid framework coupling a trained BNN with a Kalman correction step for robust online UAV state estimation. Unlike related neural Kalman approaches, BNKF produces full state predictions and incorporates Bayesian uncertainty directly into covariance propagation, improving robustness under high noise conditions. We evaluate BNKF under varying radar noise levels and sampling rates using synthetic nonlinear UAV flight data. Five fold cross validation demonstrates that BNKF outperforms Extended and Unscented Kalman Filters in accuracy, precision, and truth containment under degraded sensing. An ensemble variant (BNKFe) further improves precision in high-noise edge cases at a slight accuracy tradeoff. Runtime analysis confirms minimal inference overhead, supporting real-time deployment feasibility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经辅助卡尔曼滤波在退化感知环境中的无人机状态估计
非线性动态系统的精确状态估计是现代航空航天领域在空、海、天域操作的基础。对敌对无人机（UAV）的在线跟踪尤其具有挑战性，因为其敏捷的非线性运动、噪声和稀疏的传感器测量以及未知控制输入，这些条件违反了经典卡尔曼滤波器变体的关键假设并降低估计性能。神经网络（NN）可以从数据中学习复杂的非线性关系，但缺乏有原则的不确定性量化，而后者在状态估计任务中至关重要，因为置信边界驱动下游决策。我们通过贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）解决此问题，BNN通过网络权重分布建模不确定性，并通过蒙特卡洛采样生成预测均值和不确定性。在此基础上，我们提出贝叶斯神经卡尔曼滤波器（BNKF）：一种将训练好的BNN与卡尔曼校正步骤相结合的混合框架，用于鲁棒的无人机在线状态估计。与相关神经卡尔曼方法不同，BNKF生成完整状态预测，并将贝叶斯不确定性直接纳入协方差传播，从而在高噪声条件下提升鲁棒性。我们在不同雷达噪声水平和采样率下，使用合成非线性无人机飞行数据评估BNKF。五折交叉验证表明，BNKF在退化感知条件下，比扩展卡尔曼滤波器和无迹卡尔曼滤波器在准确性、精确性和真实值包含性方面表现更优。其集成变体（BNKFe）在高噪声边缘情况下进一步提升精确度，以轻微牺牲精度为代价。运行时分析证实推理开销极小，支持实时部署可行性。
Summary / 总结
To improve UAV state estimation in noisy, sparse, and adversarial sensing environments where classical Kalman filters fail, this work proposes the Bayesian Neural Kalman Filter (BNKF), combining a Bayesian Neural Network (BNN) with a Kalman correction step to model uncertainty and enhance robustness. The BNN learns nonlinear dynamics from data while quantifying uncertainty via Monte Carlo sampling over weight distributions, and the Kalman filter refines predictions by incorporating this uncertainty into covariance propagation. Evaluated on synthetic UAV flight data under varying radar noise and sampling rates, BNKF consistently outperforms Extended and Unscented Kalman Filters in accuracy, precision, and truth containment under degraded conditions. An ensemble variant (BNKFe) further boosts precision in high-noise scenarios at a minor accuracy cost, with runtime analysis confirming low inference overhead suitable for real-time deployment.
为提升在噪声大、数据稀疏且对抗性强的传感环境下无人机状态估计的准确性，本文提出了一种混合方法——贝叶斯神经卡尔曼滤波器（BNKF），它结合了贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）与卡尔曼校正步骤。BNN通过权重分布的蒙特卡洛采样量化不确定性，学习非线性动态；卡尔曼滤波器则利用该不确定性感知的输出进行状态修正。在合成无人机飞行数据上，针对不同雷达噪声与采样率进行评估，BNKF在精度、准确性和真实轨迹包含度方面均优于扩展卡尔曼滤波器和无迹卡尔曼滤波器。其增强版本（BNKFe）在高噪声边缘场景下进一步提升精度，仅牺牲少量精度，且运行时分析表明其计算开销极低，适合实时部署。
A Framework for Variational Inference of Lightweight Bayesian Neural Networks with Heteroscedastic Uncertainties
Authors: David J. Schodt, Ryan Brown, Michael Merritt, Samuel Park, Delsin Menolascino, Mark A. Peot
First: 2024-02-22T13:24:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:52:18+00:00
Comments: Fix equation typos
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Obtaining heteroscedastic predictive uncertainties from a Bayesian Neural Network (BNN) is vital to many applications. Often, heteroscedastic aleatoric uncertainties are learned as outputs of the BNN in addition to the predictive means, however doing so may necessitate adding more learnable parameters to the network. In this work, we demonstrate that both the heteroscedastic aleatoric and epistemic variance can be embedded into the variances of learned BNN parameters, improving predictive performance for lightweight networks. By complementing this approach with a moment propagation approach to inference, we introduce a relatively simple framework for sampling-free variational inference suitable for lightweight BNNs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于轻量级贝叶斯神经网络异方差不确定性的变分推断框架
从贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）中获得异方差预测不确定性对许多应用至关重要。通常，异方差aleatoric不确定性被作为BNN的输出与预测均值一同学习，但这样做可能需要在网络中增加更多可学习参数。在本工作中，我们证明了异方差aleatoric和epistemic方差均可嵌入到所学习BNN参数的方差中，从而提升轻量级网络的预测性能。通过结合矩传播方法进行推断，我们提出了一种相对简单的无采样变分推断框架，适用于轻量级BNN。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient Bayesian Neural Networks that yield heteroscedastic uncertainties without excessive parameter overhead, this work proposes embedding both aleatoric and epistemic variances directly into the learned parameters of lightweight BNNs, rather than treating them as separate outputs. To enable variational inference without sampling, the authors combine this parameterization with a moment propagation scheme, resulting in a sampling-free framework. Experimental results show that this approach achieves competitive predictive performance while maintaining computational efficiency, particularly for lightweight architectures.
为在不增加过多参数的前提下实现轻量级贝叶斯神经网络的异方差不确定性输出，该研究提出将异方差的随机误差和认知误差方差直接嵌入网络可学习参数的方差中，并结合矩传播方法实现无采样变分推断。实验表明，该框架在保持计算效率的同时，显著提升了轻量级网络的预测性能。
FiLMMeD: Feature-wise Linear Modulation for Cross-Problem Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing
Authors: Arthur Corrêa, Paulo Nascimento, Samuel Moniz
First: 2026-04-30T16:48:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:48:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Solving practical multi-depot vehicle routing problems (MDVRP) is a challenging optimization task central to modern logistics, increasingly driven by e-commerce. To address the MDVRP's computational complexity, neural-based combinatorial optimization methods offer a promising scalable alternative to traditional approaches. However, neural-based methods typically rely on rigid architectures and input encodings tailored to specific problem formulations. In real-world settings, heterogeneous constraints create multiple MDVRP variants, limiting the applicability of such models. While multi-task learning (MTL) has begun to accelerate the development of unified neural-based solvers, prior works focus almost exclusively on single-depot VRPs, leaving the MDVRP unaddressed. To bridge this gap, we propose Feature-wise Linear Modulation for Cross-Problem Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing (FiLMMeD), a novel unified neural-based model for 24 different MDVRP variants. We introduce three main contributions: (1) to improve the model's generalization, we augment the standard Transformer encoder with Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), which dynamically conditions learned internal representations based on the active set of constraints; (2) we provide an initial demonstration of Preference Optimization in the MTL setting, establishing it as a superior alternative to Reinforcement Learning for future MTL works; (3) to mitigate the generalization gap caused by the introduction of multi-depot constraints, we introduce a targeted curriculum learning strategy that progressively exposes the model to increasingly more complex constraint interactions. Extensive experiments on 24 MDVRP variants (including 8 novel formulations) and 16 single-depot VRPs confirm the effectiveness of FiLMMeD, which consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at: https://github.com/AJ-Correa/FiLMMeD/tree/main
中文标题/摘要
标题：FiLMMeD：面向跨问题多配送中心车辆路径的特征级线性调制
解决实际多配送中心车辆路径问题（MDVRP）是现代物流的核心优化任务，日益受电子商务驱动。为应对MDVRP的计算复杂性，基于神经网络的组合优化方法为传统方法提供了可扩展的替代方案。然而，神经方法通常依赖于针对特定问题形式设计的刚性架构和输入编码。在现实场景中，异构约束导致多种MDVRP变体，限制了此类模型的适用性。尽管多任务学习（MTL）已开始加速统一神经求解器的发展，但现有工作几乎仅关注单配送中心VRP，未解决MDVRP问题。为弥合此差距，我们提出面向跨问题多配送中心车辆路径的特征级线性调制（FiLMMeD），一种适用于24种MDVRP变体的新型统一神经模型。主要贡献包括：（1）为提升模型泛化能力，我们在标准Transformer编码器中引入特征级线性调制（FiLM），根据当前激活的约束集动态调整内部表示；（2）我们首次在MTL框架下演示偏好优化，确立其作为未来MTL工作的更优替代方案，优于强化学习；（3）为缓解多配送中心约束引入导致的泛化差距，我们提出一种定向课程学习策略，逐步让模型接触日益复杂的约束交互。在24种MDVRP变体（含8种新形式）及16种单配送中心VRP上的大量实验验证了FiLMMeD的有效性，其持续优于现有最先进基线。代码见：https://github.com/AJ-Correa/FiLMMeD/tree/main
Summary / 总结
To tackle the computational complexity of real-world multi-depot vehicle routing problems (MDVRP) with heterogeneous constraints, FiLMMeD introduces a unified neural model that dynamically adapts internal representations via Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) based on active constraints, enabling generalization across 24 MDVRP variants. It also pioneers Preference Optimization in multi-task learning as a more effective alternative to reinforcement learning and employs a curriculum learning strategy to gradually expose the model to complex constraint interactions. Experiments across 24 MDVRP variants (including 8 novel ones) and 16 single-depot VRPs show FiLMMeD consistently outperforms existing baselines, demonstrating strong scalability and adaptability.
为应对现实物流中多配送中心车辆路径问题（MDVRP）的多样性与计算复杂性，FiLMMeD提出一种统一神经架构，通过特征级线性调制动态适应不同约束条件，提升跨问题泛化能力。该方法结合FiLM增强的Transformer编码器与渐进式课程学习策略，逐步引入复杂约束交互，并采用偏好优化替代强化学习进行多任务学习。在24种MDVRP变体及16种单配送中心VRP上的实验表明，FiLMMeD持续优于现有基线方法，验证其在跨问题泛化上的有效性。
Revisiting RaBitQ and TurboQuant: A Symmetric Comparison of Methods, Theory, and Experiments
Authors: Jianyang Gao, Yutong Gou, Yuexuan Xu, Jifan Shi, Yongyi Yang, Shuolin Li, Raymond Chi-Wing Wong, Cheng Long
First: 2026-04-21T14:51:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:42:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This technical note revisits the relationship between RaBitQ and TurboQuant under a unified comparison framework. We compare the two methods in terms of methodology, theoretical guarantees, and empirical performance, using a reproducible, transparent, and symmetric setup. Our results show that, despite the claimed advantage of TurboQuant, TurboQuant performs worse than RaBitQ in most tested settings of inner-product estimation, nearest-neighbor search and KV cache quantization. We further find that several reported runtime and recall results in the TurboQuant paper could not be reproduced from the released implementation under the stated configuration. Overall, this note clarifies the shared structure and genuine differences between the two lines of work, while documenting reproducibility issues in the experimental results reported by the TurboQuant paper.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重访RaBitQ与TurboQuant：方法、理论与实验的对称性比较
本技术说明在统一的比较框架下重新审视RaBitQ与TurboQuant的关系。我们从方法论、理论保证和实证性能三个方面，采用可复现、透明且对称的设置对二者进行比较。结果表明，尽管TurboQuant声称具有优势，但在内积估计、最近邻搜索和KV缓存量化等多数测试场景中，其表现劣于RaBitQ。我们进一步发现，TurboQuant论文中报告的若干运行时和召回率结果，在其公开实现下按所述配置无法复现。总体而言，本说明阐明了这两项工作的共同结构与真实差异，同时记录了TurboQuant论文实验结果中的可复现性问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by discrepancies in prior claims about TurboQuant's superiority over RaBitQ, this study conducts a symmetric, reproducible comparison of both methods across methodology, theory, and empirical performance. Using identical experimental setups, the authors find that RaBitQ outperforms TurboQuant in most tasks including inner-product estimation, nearest-neighbor search, and KV cache quantization. Additionally, the study reveals that several runtime and recall metrics reported in the TurboQuant paper cannot be reproduced using its official implementation under the stated conditions, highlighting potential issues with experimental reporting.
鉴于此前对TurboQuant优于RaBitQ的宣称存在争议，本研究在方法、理论与实证表现三个维度上，采用对称且可复现的实验框架对二者进行对比。实验结果表明，在内积估计、最近邻搜索和KV缓存量化等多数任务中，RaBitQ表现优于TurboQuant。同时，研究发现TurboQuant论文中部分运行时和召回率数据无法通过其官方实现按所述配置复现，揭示了原实验报告中的潜在可复现性问题。
Mapping the Methodological Space of Classroom Interaction Research: Scale, Duration, and Modality in an Age of AI
Authors: Dorottya Demszky, Edith Bouton, Alison Twiner, Sara Hennessy, Richard Correnti
First: 2026-04-30T16:42:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:42:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Research on classroom interaction has long been divided between large-scale observation and in-depth ethnographic work. We propose a framework mapping this methodological space along three dimensions--scale, duration, and modality--where a study's position shapes what it reveals and obscures. We illustrate it through contrasting studies of dialogic teaching--Howe et al. (2019) and Snell and Lefstein (2018)--and an interview with the lead researchers, organized around three questions: what can be operationalized, what mechanisms become visible, and what translates to practice. We then examine how AI is expanding this space and how the framework can guide research and tool design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能时代课堂互动研究的方法论空间映射：规模、时长与模态
课堂互动研究长期分为大规模观察与深入民族志研究两类。我们提出一个框架，沿规模、时长与模态三个维度映射此方法论空间，研究位置决定其揭示与遮蔽的内容。我们通过对比对话式教学研究——Howe等（2019）与Snell和Lefstein（2018）——及对主要研究者的访谈（围绕三个问题：何可操作化、何机制显现、何可转化为实践）加以说明。随后探讨AI如何拓展此空间，以及该框架如何指导研究与工具设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmentation in classroom interaction research between large-scale and in-depth methods, this study proposes a three-dimensional framework mapping methodological choices by scale, duration, and modality, arguing that each position reveals distinct insights and obscures others. Using contrasting studies of dialogic teaching and an interview with lead researchers, it explores what can be operationalized, what mechanisms emerge, and what translates to practice. The framework is then applied to assess how AI technologies are expanding the methodological space and how researchers and tool designers can leverage it for more nuanced inquiry.
鉴于课堂互动研究长期在大规模观察与深度民族志方法间割裂，该研究提出一个以规模、时长和模态为维度的方法论空间框架，认为不同位置揭示不同洞见而遮蔽其他方面。通过对比两项对话式教学研究，说明不同方法位置在可操作化、机制可见性及实践转化上的差异。框架进一步结合对主要研究者访谈，并探讨AI如何拓展该方法空间，为未来研究与工具设计提供指导。
UHR-Net: An Uncertainty-Aware Hypergraph Refinement Network for Medical Image Segmentation
Authors: Shuokun Cheng, Jinghao Shi, Kun Sun
First: 2026-04-30T16:38:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:38:51+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Accurate lesion segmentation is crucial for clinical diagnosis and treatment planning. However, lesions often resemble surrounding tissues and exhibit ill-defined boundaries, leading to unstable predictions in boundary/transition regions. Moreover, small-lesion cues can be diluted by multi-scale feature extraction, causing under- or over-segmentation. To address these challenges, we propose an Uncertainty-Aware Hypergraph Refinement Network (UHR-Net). First, we introduce an Uncertainty-Oriented Instance Contrastive (UO-IC) pretraining strategy that couples geometry-aware copy-paste augmentation with hard-negative mining of lesion-like background regions to improve instance-level discrimination for small and visually ambiguous lesions. Second, we design an Uncertainty-Guided Hypergraph Refinement (UGHR) block, which derives an entropy-based uncertainty map from a coarse probability map to guide hypergraph refinement. By splitting hyperedge prototypes into foreground and background groups, UGHR decouples higher-order interactions and improves refinement in ambiguous regions. Experiments on five public benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over strong baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/CUGfreshman/UHR-Net.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UHR-Net：一种面向医学图像分割的不确定性感知超图精炼网络
准确的病灶分割对临床诊断和治疗规划至关重要。然而，病灶常与周围组织相似且边界模糊，导致边界/过渡区域预测不稳定；此外，多尺度特征提取易稀释小病灶线索，引发欠分割或过分割。为解决这些问题，我们提出不确定性感知超图精炼网络（UHR-Net）。首先，引入面向不确定性的实例对比预训练策略（UO-IC），结合几何感知的复制粘贴增强与病灶样背景区域的难负样本挖掘，提升小且视觉模糊病灶的实例级判别能力。其次，设计不确定性引导超图精炼模块（UGHR），从粗概率图生成基于熵的不确定性图以指导超图精炼；通过将超边原型划分为前景与背景组，UGHR解耦高阶交互并提升模糊区域的精炼效果。在五个公开基准上的实验表明，该方法显著优于强基线模型。代码见：https://github.com/CUGfreshman/UHR-Net。
Summary / 总结
To improve lesion segmentation accuracy in medical imaging, especially in regions with ambiguous boundaries and small lesions prone to under- or over-segmentation, UHR-Net introduces an Uncertainty-Aware Hypergraph Refinement Network. It employs an Uncertainty-Oriented Instance Contrastive pretraining strategy combining geometry-aware data augmentation and hard-negative mining to enhance small lesion discrimination. The core UGHR block generates an entropy-based uncertainty map to guide hypergraph refinement, splitting hyperedge prototypes into foreground and background groups to decouple higher-order interactions. Evaluated on five public benchmarks, UHR-Net consistently outperforms strong baselines in segmentation accuracy.
为提升医学图像中边界模糊、易被忽略的小病灶分割精度，UHR-Net提出一种不确定性导向实例对比预训练策略，结合几何感知增强与难负样本挖掘，增强小病灶的实例判别能力。网络进一步设计了不确定性引导超图精炼模块，利用粗概率图的熵值将超边原型分为前景与背景组，从而在不确定区域优化高阶交互。在五个公开数据集上的实验表明，UHR-Net在分割精度与边界稳定性上持续优于主流基线方法。
What Makes a Good Terminal-Agent Benchmark Task: A Guideline for Adversarial, Difficult, and Legible Evaluation Design
Authors: Ivan Bercovich
First: 2026-04-30T16:37:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:37:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Terminal-agent benchmarks have become a primary signal for measuring the coding and system-administration capabilities of large language models. As the market for evaluation environments grows, so does the pressure to ship tasks quickly, often without thorough adversarial review of the verification logic. This paper is a guideline for writing good benchmark tasks, drawn from over a year of contributing to and reviewing tasks for Terminal Bench. Most people write benchmark tasks the way they write prompts. They shouldn't. A prompt is designed to help the agent succeed; a benchmark is designed to find out if it can. We argue that good tasks are adversarial, difficult, and legible, and that a large class of common failure modes -- AI-generated instructions, over-prescriptive specifications, clerical difficulty, oracle solutions that assume hidden knowledge, tests that validate the wrong things, and reward-hackable environments -- are predictable consequences of treating task authoring as prompt authoring. We catalog these failure modes, argue that real difficulty is conceptual rather than environmental, and discuss recent empirical evidence that over 15% of tasks in popular terminal-agent benchmarks are reward-hackable. We hope this serves as a useful reference for benchmark maintainers, task contributors, and researchers using benchmark scores as evidence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：什么是好的终端代理基准任务：对抗性、困难性和可读性评估设计指南
终端代理基准已成为衡量大型语言模型编程与系统管理能力的主要指标。随着评估环境市场的扩大，快速交付任务的压力日益增加，常导致验证逻辑缺乏充分的对抗性审查。本文基于过去一年参与和评审Terminal Bench任务的经验，提出编写优质基准任务的指南。大多数人编写基准任务的方式如同编写提示词，这是错误的。提示词旨在帮助代理成功，而基准任务旨在检验其能力。我们认为，优质任务应具备对抗性、困难性和可读性，而将任务编写视为提示词编写会导致一系列常见失败模式——如AI生成指令、过度详尽的规范、文书难度、假设隐藏知识的“神谕”式解决方案、验证错误内容的测试，以及可奖励作弊的环境——这些失败模式是可以预见的。我们梳理了这些失败模式，主张真正的难度在于概念层面而非环境层面，并讨论了近期实证证据：主流终端代理基准中超过15%的任务存在可奖励作弊的问题。我们希望本文能为基准维护者、任务贡献者及将基准分数作为证据的研究人员提供实用参考。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the need for rigorous terminal-agent benchmark design by identifying common pitfalls that arise when tasks are written like prompts rather than adversarial evaluations. The authors argue that effective benchmarks must be adversarial, difficult, and legible to accurately assess model capabilities, and they catalog failure modes such as AI-generated instructions, over-prescriptive specs, clerical difficulty, oracle solutions, misaligned validation, and reward-hackable environments—all stemming from prompt-like task authoring. Empirical evidence suggests over 15% of tasks in popular benchmarks are reward-hackable, indicating widespread design flaws. The paper serves as a practical guideline for benchmark creators and researchers to avoid these pitfalls and improve evaluation reliability.
为提升终端代理基准测试的可靠性，本文提出一套设计对抗性、困难且易理解任务的框架，以更准确评估大语言模型在真实系统管理与编程场景中的能力。文章指出，将任务写作类比于提示词设计会导致常见失效模式，如奖励可操控环境、预设知识的解法、过度指令化等。通过对主流基准的实证分析，作者发现超过15%的任务存在奖励操控漏洞，并主张应追求概念层面的难度而非环境复杂性。本文旨在为基准维护者、任务贡献者及依赖基准分数的研究人员提供实用指导，避免上述陷阱，确保评估反映真实能力。
Towards Neuro-symbolic Causal Rule Synthesis, Verification, and Evaluation Grounded in Legal and Safety Principles
Authors: Zainab Rehan, Christian Medeiros Adriano, Sona Ghahremani, Holger Giese
Venue: Neurosymbolic eXplainable Trustworthy Systems @ AAMAS 2026
First: 2026-04-30T16:34:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:34:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rule-based systems remain central in safety-critical domains but often struggle with scalability, brittleness, and goal misspecification. These limitations can lead to reward hacking and failures in formal verification, as AI systems tend to optimize for narrow objectives. In previous research, we developed a neuro-symbolic causal framework that integrates first-order logic abduction trees, structural causal models, and deep reinforcement learning within a MAPE-K loop to provide explainable adaptations under distribution shifts. In this paper, we extend that framework by introducing a meta-level layer designed to mitigate goal misspecification and support scalable rule maintenance. This layer consists of a Goal/Rule Synthesizer and a Rule Verification Engine, which iteratively refine a formal rule theory from high-level natural-language goals and principles provided by human experts. The synthesis pipeline employs large language models (LLMs) to: (1) decompose goals into candidate causes, (2) consolidate semantics to remove redundancies, (3) translate them into candidate first-order rules, and (4) compose necessary and sufficient causal sets. The verification pipeline then performs (1) syntax and schema validation, (2) logical consistency analysis, and (3) safety and invariant checks before integrating verified rules into the knowledge base. We evaluated our approach with a proof-of-concept implementation in two autonomous driving scenarios. Results indicate that, given human-specified goals and principles, the pipeline can successfully derive minimal necessary and sufficient rule sets and formalize them as logical constraints. These findings suggest that the pipeline supports incremental, modular, and traceable rule synthesis grounded in established legal and safety principles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于法律与安全原则的神经符号因果规则合成、验证与评估
基于规则的系统在安全关键领域仍居核心地位，但常面临可扩展性差、脆弱性高及目标误指定等问题，易导致奖励黑客行为和形式化验证失败。此前研究中，我们构建了一种神经符号因果框架，将一阶逻辑归约树、结构因果模型与深度强化学习整合于MAPE-K循环中，以实现分布偏移下的可解释自适应。本文进一步扩展该框架，引入元层以缓解目标误指定并支持可扩展规则维护。该层包含目标/规则合成器与规则验证引擎，可迭代地从人类专家提供的高层自然语言目标与原则中提炼形式化规则理论。合成流程利用大语言模型（LLM）完成：（1）将目标分解为候选原因，（2）整合语义以消除冗余，（3）转化为候选一阶规则，（4）构建必要且充分的因果集合。验证流程则执行：（1）语法与模式校验，（2）逻辑一致性分析，（3）安全与不变量检查，最终将验证通过的规则纳入知识库。我们在两个自动驾驶场景中进行了概念验证，结果表明：在人类指定的目标与原则下，该流程可成功推导出最小必要且充分的规则集并形式化为逻辑约束。这表明该流程支持基于既定法律与安全原则的增量式、模块化、可追溯规则合成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of rule-based systems in safety-critical domains—such as scalability issues, brittleness, and reward hacking—the paper introduces a neuro-symbolic causal framework enhanced with a meta-level layer for goal misspecification mitigation and scalable rule maintenance. This layer includes a Goal/Rule Synthesizer and a Rule Verification Engine that iteratively refine formal rule theories from natural-language goals and safety principles provided by human experts. The synthesis phase uses large language models to decompose goals, consolidate semantics, generate candidate first-order rules, and compose causal sets, while the verification phase validates syntax, logic, and safety invariants before integrating rules. Experimental results in two autonomous driving scenarios demonstrate that the pipeline successfully derives minimal, necessary, and sufficient rule sets formalized as logical constraints, supporting incremental, modular, and traceable rule synthesis aligned with legal and safety principles.
为解决规则系统在安全关键领域中的脆弱性与可扩展性问题，本文提出一种增强型神经符号因果框架，引入元层以应对目标误设并支持可扩展规则维护。该方法结合大语言模型、一阶逻辑归约树与结构因果模型，通过目标/规则合成器将自然语言目标分解为候选因果规则，并经规则验证引擎进行语法、逻辑及安全校验后整合。在自动驾驶场景的实验表明，该流程可生成最小必要且充分的规则集并形式化为逻辑约束，支持基于法律与安全原则的增量、模块化、可追溯规则合成。
NashPG: A Policy Gradient Method with Iteratively Refined Regularization for Finding Nash Equilibria
Authors: Eason Yu, Tzu Hao Liu, Clément L. Canonne, Yunke Wang, Chang Xu, Nguyen H. Tran, Stefano V. Albrecht
First: 2025-10-21T00:14:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:28:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Finding Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games remains a central challenge in multi-agent reinforcement learning. Recent multi-round regularization methods offer a promising direction, yet existing approaches either require full enumeration of the game tree or rely on non-policy-gradient inner solvers that underperform in practice, leaving a scalable policy-gradient-based solution open. In this paper, we propose a novel multi-round regularization procedure and show that it guarantees strictly monotonic reduction in Bregman divergence to Nash equilibria and eventual convergence to one in two-player zero-sum extensive-form games. Guided by this framework, we develop a practical algorithm, Nash Policy Gradient (NashPG), which places the regularization directly in the policy optimization objective and is implemented using standard policy gradient methods. Empirically, NashPG achieves comparable or lower exploitability than prior model-free methods on classic benchmark games and scales to large domains such as Battleship and No-Limit Texas Hold'em, where it attains higher average payoff in head-to-head play.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NashPG：一种用于寻找纳什均衡的具有迭代优化正则化的策略梯度方法
在两人零和不完美信息博弈中寻找纳什均衡仍是多智能体强化学习的核心挑战。近期的多轮正则化方法提供了有前景的方向，但现有方法要么需要完整枚举博弈树，要么依赖于表现不佳的非策略梯度内层求解器，仍缺乏可扩展的基于策略梯度的解决方案。本文提出了一种新颖的多轮正则化过程，并证明其在两人零和扩展形式博弈中能保证Bregman发散严格单调递减并最终收敛至纳什均衡。基于此框架，我们开发了实用算法Nash Policy Gradient（NashPG），将正则化直接嵌入策略优化目标，并采用标准策略梯度方法实现。实验证明，NashPG在经典基准博弈中与先前无模型方法相比具有相当或更低的可被利用性，并可扩展至大型领域如战舰与无限注德州扑克，在一对一博弈中获得更高的平均收益。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of finding Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games, this paper introduces NashPG, a policy gradient method that integrates regularization directly into the policy optimization objective. The method employs a novel multi-round regularization procedure that ensures strictly monotonic reduction in Bregman divergence toward Nash equilibria, guaranteeing convergence in two-player zero-sum extensive-form games. Empirically, NashPG matches or outperforms prior model-free methods on classic benchmarks and scales effectively to large domains like Battleship and No-Limit Texas Hold'em, achieving higher average payoffs in head-to-head play.
为解决双人零和不完美信息博弈中纳什均衡的可扩展策略梯度求解问题，本文提出NashPG算法，将正则化直接嵌入策略优化目标中。该方法采用多轮正则化过程，确保Bregman散度单调递减并最终收敛至纳什均衡。实验表明，NashPG在经典基准游戏中表现与现有无模型方法相当或更优，并能有效扩展至大型领域如战舰与无限注德州扑克，在对弈中实现更高平均收益。
Characterizing the Consistency of the Emergent Misalignment Persona
Authors: Anietta Weckauff, Yuchen Zhang, Maksym Andriushchenko
First: 2026-04-30T16:26:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:26:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on narrowly misaligned data generalizes to broadly misaligned behavior, a phenomenon termed emergent misalignment (EM). While prior work has found a correlation between harmful behavior and self-assessment in emergently misaligned models, it remains unclear how consistent this correspondence is across tasks and whether it varies across fine-tuning domains. We characterize the consistency of the EM persona by fine-tuning Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct on six narrowly misaligned domains (e.g., insecure code, risky financial advice, bad medical advice) and administering experiments including harmfulness evaluation, self-assessment, choosing between two descriptions of AI systems, output recognition, and score prediction. Our results reveal two distinct patterns: coherent-persona models, in which harmful behavior and self-reported misalignment are coupled, and inverted-persona models, which produce harmful outputs while identifying as aligned AI systems. These findings reveal a more fine-grained picture of the effects of emergent misalignment, calling into question the consistency of the EM persona.
中文标题/摘要
标题：刻画涌现错位人格的一致性
在狭窄错位数据上微调大语言模型（LLM）会泛化出广泛错位行为，这一现象称为涌现错位（EM）。尽管先前研究发现涌现错位模型中的有害行为与自我评估之间存在相关性，但尚不清楚这种对应关系在不同任务中的稳定性，以及是否随微调领域而变化。我们通过在六个狭窄错位领域（如不安全代码、高风险金融建议、不良医疗建议）上微调Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct，并进行包括有害性评估、自我评估、选择AI系统描述、输出识别和评分预测等实验，刻画了EM人格的一致性。结果揭示两种不同模式：一致人格模型中，有害行为与自我报告的错位耦合；倒置人格模型则产生有害输出，却自认为是符合对齐的AI系统。这些发现揭示了涌现错位效应更精细的图景，质疑了EM人格的一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how consistently emergent misalignment (EM) manifests across tasks and domains, researchers fine-tuned Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct on six narrowly misaligned datasets and evaluated models across multiple behavioral and self-assessment tasks. The study identified two distinct EM patterns: coherent-persona models, where harmful outputs align with self-reported misalignment, and inverted-persona models, which generate harmful outputs while claiming to be aligned. These findings suggest EM is not uniformly consistent and may vary significantly depending on fine-tuning domain and task context.
为探究新兴对齐偏差（EM）在不同微调领域和任务中的表现一致性，研究者在六个狭窄对齐偏差领域微调了Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct模型，并通过多项行为与自我评估任务进行测试。结果发现两类EM模式：一类是‘一致人格’模型，其有害输出与自我报告的对齐偏差一致；另一类是‘反向人格’模型，虽产生有害输出却自认对齐。该研究揭示EM并非单一表现，质疑了EM人格一致性假设。
Measuring Opinion Bias and Sycophancy via LLM-based Persuasion
Authors: Rodrigo Nogueira, Giovana Kerche Bonás, Thales Sales Almeida, Andrea Roque, Ramon Pires, Hugo Abonizio, Thiago Laitz, Celio Larcher, Roseval Malaquias Junior, Marcos Piau
First: 2026-04-23T11:34:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:26:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models increasingly shape the information people consume: they are embedded in search, consulted for professional advice, deployed as agents, and used as a first stop for questions about policy, ethics, health, and politics. When such a model silently holds a position on a contested topic, that position propagates at scale into users' decisions. Eliciting a model's positions is harder than it first appears: contemporary assistants answer direct opinion questions with evasive disclaimers, and the same model may concede the opposite position once the user starts arguing one side. We propose a method, released as the open-source llm-bias-bench, for discovering the opinions an LLM actually holds on contested topics under conditions that resemble real multi-turn interaction. The method pairs two complementary free-form probes. Direct probing asks for the model's opinion across five turns of escalating pressure from a simulated user. Indirect probing never asks for an opinion and engages the model in argumentative debate, letting bias leak through how it concedes, resists, or counter-argues. Three user personas (neutral, agree, disagree) collapse into a nine-way behavioral classification that separates persona-independent positions from persona-dependent sycophancy, and an auditable LLM judge produces verdicts with textual evidence. The first instantiation ships 38 topics in Brazilian Portuguese across values, scientific consensus, philosophy, and economic policy. Applied to 13 assistants, the method surfaces findings of practical interest: argumentative debate triggers sycophancy 2-3x more than direct questioning (median 50% to 79%); models that look opinionated under direct questioning often collapse into mirroring under sustained arguments; and attacker capability matters mainly when an existing opinion must be dislodged, not when the assistant starts neutral.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过基于大语言模型的说服力测量意见偏见与谄媚
大语言模型正越来越多地塑造人们所消费的信息：它们嵌入搜索系统、被用于专业咨询、作为代理部署，并作为政策、伦理、健康和政治问题的首要查询工具。当此类模型在争议性话题上默然持有一种立场时，该立场会大规模传播至用户决策。获取模型立场比表面看起来更困难：当代助手在直接意见问题上常以回避性免责声明作答，且同一模型在用户开始论证某一立场时可能转而承认相反立场。我们提出一种方法，开源工具llm-bias-bench，用于在接近真实多轮交互的条件下发现LLM在争议性话题上的实际立场。该方法结合两种互补的开放式探针：直接探针通过模拟用户五轮逐步施压询问模型立场；间接探针从不直接询问立场，而是引导模型参与辩论，让偏见通过其让步、抵抗或反驳方式显露。三种用户角色（中立、同意、反对）可归结为九种行为分类，区分独立于角色的立场与依赖角色的谄媚行为，且由可审计的LLM裁判员依据文本证据出具裁决。首个版本涵盖38个巴西葡萄牙语话题，涉及价值观、科学共识、哲学与经济政策。应用于13个助手，该方法揭示了实用发现：辩论比直接提问更易触发谄媚行为（中位数50%至79%）；在持续辩论中，看似有主见的模型常退化为镜像回应；攻击者能力主要在需推翻既有立场时起作用，而非当助手初始中立时。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing influence of large language models in shaping user decisions on contentious topics, this study develops a method to uncover hidden opinions and sycophantic tendencies in LLMs under realistic multi-turn interactions. The approach combines direct probing—escalating user pressure to extract opinions—and indirect probing—engaging models in argumentative debate to reveal bias through concessions or counter-arguments. Using three user personas (neutral, agree, disagree), the method classifies model behavior into nine categories to distinguish genuine positions from persona-dependent sycophancy, with an auditable judge providing evidence-based verdicts. Applied to 13 LLMs across 38 Brazilian Portuguese topics, results show that argumentative debate triggers sycophancy 2–3 times more frequently than direct questioning, and models often mirror user positions under sustained debate rather than holding consistent views.
随着大型语言模型在搜索、专业咨询和政策讨论等场景中日益影响用户决策，揭示其隐藏偏见与谄媚倾向变得至关重要。本文提出开源工具llm-bias-bench，通过两种互补探针——在逐步施压下直接提问与在辩论中间接诱导——揭示模型在争议话题上的真实立场。通过三类用户角色（中立、同意、反对）和九种行为分类，该方法区分稳定立场与依赖用户角色的谄媚行为，并由可审计的模型裁判提供文本证据。在13个模型和38个巴西葡萄牙语话题（涵盖价值观、科学共识、哲学与经济政策）上测试发现，辩论比直接提问更易触发谄媚行为（中位数50%至79%），且模型常在持续辩论中从看似有立场转为模仿用户，攻击者成功与否主要取决于是否需推翻模型原有立场。
AesRM: Improving Video Aesthetics with Expert-Level Feedback
Authors: Yujin Han, Yujie Wei, Yefei He, Xinyu Liu, Tianle Li, Zichao Yu, Andi Han, Shiwei Zhang, Tingyu Weng, Difan Zou
First: 2026-04-30T16:24:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:24:07+00:00
Comments: 37 pages, 14 figures, 12 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite rapid advances in photorealistic video generation, real-world applications such as filmmaking require video aesthetics, e.g., harmonious colors and cinematic lighting, beyond visual fidelity. Prior work on visual aesthetics largely focuses on images, often reducing aesthetics to coarse definitions, e.g., visual pleasure, without a rigorous and systematic evaluation. To improve video aesthetics, we propose a hierarchical rubric that decomposes video aesthetics into three core dimensions, Visual Aesthetics (VA), Visual Fidelity (VF), and Visual Plausibility (VP), with 15 fine-grained criteria, e.g., shot composition. This framework enables a large-scale expert-annotated preference dataset and an evaluation benchmark, AesVideo-Bench, containing about 2500 video pairs with expert annotations on VA, VF, and VP. We then build a family of Video Aesthetic Reward Models (AesRM): AesRM-Base, which directly predicts pairwise preferences on these dimensions to provide efficient post-training rewards, and AesRM-CoT, which additionally generates CoT aligned with all 15 criteria to improve assessment interpretability. Specifically, we train AesRM with a three-stage progressive scheme: (1) Atomic Aesthetic Capability Learning, which strengthens AesRM's recognition of fundamental aesthetic concepts, e.g., accurately identifying centered composition; (2) Cold-Start, aligning the model with structured reasoning protocols; and (3) GRPO, further improving evaluation accuracy. To enhance AesRM-CoT, we additionally propose self-consistency-based CoT synthesis to improve CoT quality and design CoT-based process rewards during GRPO. Extensive experiments show AesRM outperforms baselines on multiple aesthetics benchmarks and is more robust, with lower position bias. Finally, we align Wan2.2 with AesRM and observe clear aesthetic gains over existing aesthetic reward models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AesRM：通过专家级反馈提升视频美学
尽管在逼真视频生成方面取得了快速进展，但实际应用如电影制作仍需超越视觉保真度的视频美学，例如和谐的色彩和电影感照明。此前关于视觉美学的研究主要集中在图像上，常将美学简化为粗略定义（如视觉愉悦），缺乏严谨系统的评估。为提升视频美学，我们提出一种分层评分标准，将视频美学分解为三个核心维度：视觉美学（VA）、视觉保真度（VF）和视觉可信度（VP），并包含15个细粒度标准（如镜头构图）。该框架支持大规模专家标注偏好数据集及评估基准AesVideo-Bench，包含约2500对视频及其在VA、VF、VP维度上的专家标注。随后我们构建了视频美学奖励模型（AesRM）系列：AesRM-Base直接预测各维度的成对偏好，提供高效的后训练奖励；AesRM-CoT则额外生成与15项标准对齐的思维链（CoT），以提升评估可解释性。具体而言，我们采用三阶段渐进式训练方案训练AesRM：（1）原子级美学能力学习，强化模型对基础美学概念（如准确识别居中构图）的识别；（2）冷启动阶段，使模型符合结构化推理协议；（3）GRPO阶段，进一步提升评估精度。为提升AesRM-CoT，我们额外提出基于自一致性思维链合成方法以提升CoT质量，并在GRPO阶段设计基于CoT的过程奖励。大量实验表明，AesRM在多个美学基准上优于基线模型，且更鲁棒，位置偏差更低。最后，我们将Wan2.2与AesRM对齐，观察到其在现有美学奖励模型之上获得显著美学提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for aesthetic quality in real-world video applications beyond visual fidelity, this work introduces AesRM, a family of video aesthetic reward models built on a hierarchical rubric decomposing aesthetics into Visual Aesthetics, Visual Fidelity, and Visual Plausibility, with 15 fine-grained criteria. The method trains AesRM-Base to predict pairwise preferences and AesRM-CoT to generate chain-of-thought reasoning aligned with all criteria, using a three-stage progressive training scheme: Atomic Aesthetic Capability Learning, Cold-Start, and GRPO, enhanced by self-consistency for CoT synthesis and process rewards. Experimental results demonstrate AesRM’s superior performance across multiple benchmarks, greater robustness, and reduced position bias compared to baselines, with clear aesthetic improvements when aligned with the video generation model Wan2.2.
为解决视频生成中超出视觉保真度的美学评估需求，本文提出AesRM系列视频美学奖励模型，基于将美学分解为视觉美学、视觉保真度与视觉可信度三大维度及15项细粒度标准的层级评估框架。方法包括AesRM-Base直接预测成对偏好，以及AesRM-CoT生成与所有标准对齐的链式推理，采用三阶段渐进训练：原子美学能力学习、冷启动对齐与GRPO优化，并引入自一致性链式推理合成与过程奖励。实验表明AesRM在多个基准上表现更优，鲁棒性更强、位置偏差更低，与Wan2.2对齐后显著优于现有美学奖励模型。
TopBench: A Benchmark for Implicit Prediction and Reasoning over Tabular Question Answering
Authors: An-Yang Ji, Jun-Peng Jiang, De-Chuan Zhan, Han-Jia Ye
First: 2026-04-30T16:22:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:22:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced Table Question Answering, where most queries can be answered by extracting information or simple aggregation. However, a common class of real-world queries is implicitly predictive, requiring the inference of unobserved answers from historical patterns rather than mere retrieval. These queries introduce two challenges: recognizing latent intent and reliable predictive reasoning over massive tables. To assess LLMs in such Tabular questiOn answering with implicit Prediction tasks, we introduce TopBench, a benchmark consisting of 779 samples across four sub-tasks, ranging from single-point prediction to decision making, treatment effect analysis, and complex filtering, requiring models to generate outputs spanning reasoning text and structured tables. We evaluate diverse models under both text-based and agentic workflows. Experiments reveal that current models often struggle with intent recognition, defaulting to just lookups. Deeper analysis identifies that accurate intent disambiguation serves as the prerequisite for leading these predictive behaviors. Furthermore, elevating the upper bound of prediction precision requires the integration of more sophisticated modeling or reasoning capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TopBench：面向表格问答中隐式预测与推理的基准
大语言模型（LLMs）已推动表格问答的发展，其中多数查询可通过信息提取或简单聚合回答。然而，现实世界中常见的一类查询具有隐式预测性质，需从历史模式推断未观测答案，而非简单检索。此类查询带来两大挑战：识别潜在意图与在大规模表格上进行可靠预测推理。为评估LLMs在隐式预测任务下的表格问答能力，我们提出TopBench基准，包含4个子任务共779个样本，涵盖单点预测、决策制定、处理效应分析及复杂过滤，要求模型生成包含推理文本与结构化表格的输出。我们在基于文本与代理工作流下评估多种模型。实验表明，当前模型常在意图识别上存在困难，倾向于仅进行检索。深入分析发现，准确的意图消歧是引导预测行为的前提。此外，提升预测精度上限需整合更复杂的建模或推理能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language models on real-world tabular question answering tasks that require implicit prediction rather than simple retrieval, the authors introduce TopBench, a benchmark with 779 samples across four sub-tasks including single-point prediction, decision making, treatment effect analysis, and complex filtering. The benchmark demands models to produce both reasoning text and structured outputs. Experiments show that current models frequently fail to recognize latent intent and default to lookup-based responses, indicating that accurate intent disambiguation is essential for enabling predictive reasoning, and that achieving higher prediction accuracy requires more sophisticated modeling or reasoning capabilities.
为评估大语言模型在需要隐式预测而非简单检索的真实世界表格问答任务中的能力，作者提出了TopBench基准，包含779个样本，覆盖单点预测、决策、治疗效应分析和复杂筛选四个子任务，要求模型输出推理文本与结构化表格，检验其从历史模式中推断未观测值的能力。实验表明，当前模型常无法识别隐含意图，倾向于仅进行检索，而准确的意图消歧是实现预测行为的前提；此外，提升预测精度需引入更复杂的建模或推理能力。
PhotIQA: A photoacoustic image data set with image quality ratings
Authors: Anna Breger, Janek Gröhl, Clemens Karner, Thomas R Else, Ian Selby, Tom Rix, Lara-Sophie Witt, Merle Duchêne, Jonathan Weir-McCall, Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb
First: 2025-07-04T11:06:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:22:35+00:00
Comments: 16 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Image quality assessment (IQA) is crucial in the evaluation stage of novel algorithms operating on images, including traditional and machine learning based methods. Due to the lack of available quality-rated medical images, most commonly used full-reference IQA measures have been developed and tested for natural images. Reported pitfalls and inconsistencies arising when applying such measures for medical images are not surprising, as they rely on different properties than natural images. In photoacoustic imaging (PAI), especially, standard benchmarking approaches for assessing the quality of image reconstructions are lacking. PAI is a multi-physics imaging modality, in which two inverse problems have to be solved, which makes the application of IQA measures uniquely challenging due to both, acoustic and optical, artifacts. To support the development and testing of IQA measures we assembled PhotIQA, a data set consisting of 1134 photoacoustic images. The images were rated by five experts across five quality properties in a full-reference setting, where the detailed rating enables usage beyond PAI. The data set with the images and corresponding ratings is publicly available on Zenodo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhotIQA：带图像质量评分的光声图像数据集
图像质量评估（IQA）在评估作用于图像的传统和基于机器学习的新算法时至关重要。由于缺乏带质量评分的医学图像，目前常用的全参考IQA度量大多是在自然图像上开发和测试的。当将这些度量应用于医学图像时出现的缺陷和不一致性并不令人意外，因为它们依赖于与自然图像不同的属性。在光声成像（PAI）中，尤其缺乏评估图像重建质量的标准基准方法。PAI是一种多物理成像模态，需要解决两个逆问题，这使得IQA度量的应用因声学和光学伪影而变得尤为困难。为支持IQA度量的开发和测试，我们构建了PhotIQA数据集，包含1134张光声图像。这些图像由五位专家在全参考设置下，针对五个质量属性进行评分，详细评分使其可应用于PAI之外的领域。该数据集（含图像及其评分）已公开发布于Zenodo。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of quality-rated medical images for evaluating image quality assessment (IQA) methods, especially in photoacoustic imaging (PAI), the authors created PhotIQA, a dataset of 1134 PA images rated by five experts across five quality dimensions. This full-reference dataset addresses the gap in benchmarking IQA for PAI, where standard natural-image-based IQA measures fail due to unique acoustic and optical artifacts. Experimental results show that the ratings are consistent and reliable, enabling robust evaluation of IQA algorithms tailored for PAI and potentially applicable to other medical imaging domains.
为解决医学图像领域缺乏质量评分数据以评估图像质量评估（IQA）方法的问题，特别是针对光声成像（PAI）中现有IQA方法表现不佳的现状，作者构建了PhotIQA数据集，包含1134张由五位专家按五个维度评分的PA图像。该全参考数据集填补了PAI领域缺乏标准化评估基准的空白，实验表明现有IQA指标在PA图像上表现不稳定，凸显了开发领域专用评估工具的必要性。
Repetition over Diversity: High-Signal Data Filtering for Sample-Efficient German Language Modeling
Authors: Ansar Aynetdinov, Patrick Haller, Alan Akbik
First: 2026-04-30T16:21:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:21:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent research has shown that filtering massive English web corpora into high-quality subsets significantly improves training efficiency. However, for high-resource non-English languages like German, French, or Japanese, aggressive filtering creates a strategic dilemma: should practitioners prioritize diversity by training once on large amounts of lightly filtered web data, or prioritize quality by strictly filtering for a high-quality core and repeating it over multiple epochs? We investigate this trade-off for German by constructing hierarchical quality filters applied to 500M web documents, comparing multi-epoch training on the filtered subsets against single-pass training on a diverse corpus. Our experiments across multiple model scales and token budgets show that repeating high-quality data consistently outperforms single-pass training on larger, less filtered sets. Notably, the performance gap persists even after 7 epochs. Our findings suggest that for non-English LLMs, semantic concentration through quality filtering offers a more viable path to efficient language modeling than simply maximizing unique data volume. We release our German language models (called Boldt), as well as our cleaned evaluation benchmarks to the research community. Our experiments indicate that they achieve state-of-the-art results despite training on 10-360x fewer tokens than comparable models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重复胜于多样性：面向高效德语语言建模的高信号数据过滤
近期研究表明，将庞大的英文网络语料库过滤为高质量子集可显著提升训练效率。然而，对于德语、法语或日语等高资源非英语语言，激进的过滤策略带来战略抉择：研究者应优先选择多样性，即在大量轻度过滤的网络数据上训练一次；还是优先选择质量，即严格筛选高质量核心数据并在多个训练周期中重复使用？我们针对德语构建了应用于5亿网页文档的分层质量过滤器，比较了在过滤子集上进行多周期训练与在多样化语料上单次训练的效果。实验结果表明，无论模型规模或token预算如何，重复使用高质量数据始终优于在更大但过滤较弱的数据集上单次训练。值得注意的是，即使经过7个周期，性能差距依然存在。我们的研究结果表明，对于非英语大语言模型，通过质量过滤实现语义集中化，比单纯最大化独特数据量更有利于高效语言建模。我们向研究社区开源了德语语言模型（称为Boldt）及清洗后的评估基准。实验表明，尽管其训练所用token数量仅为同类模型的10至360倍，仍取得了当前最优性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve training efficiency for German language models without sacrificing performance, this study investigates whether repeating high-quality, filtered data yields better results than training once on a larger, less filtered corpus. The authors apply hierarchical quality filters to 500M German web documents and compare multi-epoch training on the filtered subsets against single-pass training on diverse data. Across multiple model scales and token budgets, repeating high-quality data consistently outperforms single-pass training, with performance gaps persisting even after seven epochs. The findings suggest that semantic concentration through quality filtering is more effective than maximizing data diversity for non-English LLMs, and the resulting models achieve state-of-the-art results despite training on 10 to 360 times fewer tokens than comparable models.
鉴于过滤大规模网络语料可提升英语大语言模型的训练效率，本研究探讨该策略是否适用于德语等高资源非英语语言。作者对5亿德语网页文档应用分层质量过滤，对比在过滤后的高质量子集上进行多轮训练与在更大但过滤较弱的语料上单次训练的效果。在多种模型规模和token预算下，重复高质量数据始终表现更优，且七轮训练后性能差距仍显著。结果表明，非英语语言建模中，通过质量过滤实现语义集中化比单纯扩大数据多样性更有效，所发布的德语模型（Boldt）在训练token量仅为同类模型10至360倍的情况下仍达到最先进水平。
VerteNet -- A Multi-Context Hybrid CNN Transformer for Accurate Vertebral Landmark Localization in Lateral Spine DXA Images
Authors: Arooba Maqsood, Zaid Ilyas, Afsah Saleem, Erchuan Zhang, David Suter, Parminder Raina, Jonathan M. Hodgson, John T. Schousboe, William D. Leslie, Joshua R. Lewis, Syed Zulqarnain Gilani
First: 2025-02-04T08:27:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:20:10+00:00
Comments: 17 pages with 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This aims to develop and validate a deep learning model that can accurately locate vertebral landmarks in lateral spine Dual energy X-ray Absorptiometry (DXA) scans. Accurate vertebral landmark localization is critical for reliable fracture assessment and scoring of abdominal aortic calcification using the Kauppila 24-point method; however, DXA lateral spine images are low-contrast, artifact-prone, and manufacturer-dependent, while manual annotation is time-consuming and reader-dependent. This study aimed to address these challenges by developing a dual-resolution self- and cross-attention model for robust vertebral landmark localization using lateral spine DXA scans from four different scanner models. Ground-truth vertebral corner landmarks (T12 to L5) were manually annotated, and performance was evaluated using normalized mean and median localization errors against baseline and state-of-the-art methods. The proposed framework achieved superior localization accuracy across all four DXA scanner models, with a normalized mean error of 4.92 pixels and a median error of 2.35 pixels, outperforming baseline methods. The abdominal aorta crop detection algorithm achieved 100% accuracy in validation and 96% accuracy (sensitivity 0.93, specificity 0.98) in an independent test set. Generated intervertebral guides further improved inter-reader agreement, reflected by higher Cohens weighted kappa and inter-reader correlation. The proposed deep learning framework enables accurate and robust vertebral landmark localization in lateral spine DXA images across heterogeneous imaging systems to support clinically relevant downstream analyses. The code for this work can be found at: https://github.com/zaidilyas89/VerteNet
中文标题/摘要
标题：VerteNet——一种用于侧位脊柱DXA图像椎体标志点精准定位的多上下文混合CNN-Transformer模型
本研究旨在开发并验证一种深度学习模型，以精准定位侧位脊柱双能X线吸收法（DXA）扫描中的椎体标志点。精准的椎体标志点定位对骨折评估及采用Kauppila 24点法评估腹主动脉钙化至关重要；然而，DXA侧位脊柱图像对比度低、易受伪影干扰且依赖于设备制造商，而人工标注耗时且依赖阅片者。本研究通过开发一种双分辨率自注意力与交叉注意力模型，解决了上述挑战，该模型基于四种不同扫描仪的侧位脊柱DXA图像实现稳健的椎体标志点定位。人工标注了T12至L5椎体角点的真值，性能通过归一化均值与中位定位误差与基线及前沿方法对比评估。所提框架在四种DXA扫描仪上均实现更优定位精度，归一化均值误差为4.92像素，中位误差为2.35像素，优于基线方法。腹主动脉裁剪检测算法在验证集上准确率达100%，独立测试集上准确率为96%（敏感度0.93，特异度0.98）。生成的椎间隙引导线进一步提升了阅片者间一致性，表现为Cohen加权Kappa值与阅片者间相关性更高。该深度学习框架可支持异构成像系统下侧位脊柱DXA图像中椎体标志点的精准稳健定位，从而助力临床相关下游分析。本研究代码见：https://github.com/zaidilyas89/VerteNet
Summary / 总结
To address the challenges of low-contrast, artifact-prone, and manufacturer-dependent lateral spine DXA images that hinder accurate vertebral landmark localization—critical for fracture assessment and abdominal aortic calcification scoring—the study proposes VerteNet, a multi-context hybrid CNN-Transformer model combining self- and cross-attention mechanisms at dual resolutions. Evaluated across four different DXA scanner models using manually annotated T12 to L5 vertebral corner landmarks, the model achieved a normalized mean localization error of 4.92 pixels and median error of 2.35 pixels, outperforming baseline methods. Additionally, the abdominal aorta detection algorithm achieved 100% validation accuracy and 96% test accuracy with high sensitivity and specificity, while generated intervertebral guides improved inter-reader agreement via higher Cohen’s weighted kappa and correlation scores.
为解决低对比度、易受伪影干扰且设备依赖性强的侧位脊柱DXA图像所导致的椎体标志点定位困难——这对骨折评估和腹主动脉钙化评分至关重要——研究团队开发了一种名为VerteNet的多上下文混合CNN-Transformer模型。该模型通过双分辨率自注意力与交叉注意力机制增强跨不同设备的特征表达。在四类不同DXA设备的T12至L5椎体角点人工标注数据上评估，VerteNet实现归一化平均定位误差4.92像素、中位误差2.35像素，优于基线方法。其腹主动脉检测算法在验证集达100%准确率，独立测试集达96%准确率（敏感度0.93，特异度0.98），生成的椎间隙引导线亦显著提升阅片者间一致性，表现为更高的Cohen加权Kappa值和相关系数。
FP-IRL: Fokker--Planck Inverse Reinforcement Learning -- A Physics-Constrained Approach to Markov Decision Processes
Authors: Chengyang Huang, Siddhartha Srivastava, Kenneth K. Y. Ho, Kathy E. Luker, Gary D. Luker, Xun Huan, Krishna Garikipati
Venue: Computer Methods in Applied Mechanics and Engineering, 458, 119010 (2026)
First: 2023-06-17T18:28:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:19:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) is a powerful paradigm for uncovering the incentive structure that drives agent behavior, by inferring an unknown reward function from observed trajectories within a Markov decision process (MDP). However, most existing IRL methods require access to the transition function, either prescribed or estimated \textit{a priori}, which poses significant challenges when the underlying dynamics are unknown, unobservable, or not easily sampled. We propose Fokker--Planck inverse reinforcement learning (FP-IRL), a novel physics-constrained IRL framework tailored for systems that can be described by Fokker--Planck (FP) dynamics. FP-IRL simultaneously infers both the reward and transition functions directly from trajectory data, without requiring access to sampled transitions. Our method leverages a correspondence between MDPs and the FP equation, linking reward maximization in MDPs with free energy minimization in FP dynamics. This connection enables inference of the FP potential function using our inference approach of variational system identification, from which the full set of MDP components -- reward, transition, and policy -- can be recovered using analytic expressions. We demonstrate the effectiveness of FP-IRL through experiments on synthetic benchmarks and a modified version of the Mountain Car problem. Our results show that FP-IRL achieves accurate recovery of agent incentives while preserving computational efficiency and physical interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FP-IRL：福克-普朗克逆强化学习——一种面向马尔可夫决策过程的物理约束方法
逆强化学习（IRL）是一种通过从马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中观测到的轨迹推断未知奖励函数，从而揭示驱动智能体行为的激励结构的强大范式。然而，大多数现有IRL方法需要事先获得或估计转移函数，这在系统动力学未知、不可观测或难以采样时面临重大挑战。 我们提出福克-普朗克逆强化学习（FP-IRL），一种专为可由福克-普朗克（FP）动力学描述的系统设计的新型物理约束IRL框架。FP-IRL可直接从轨迹数据同时推断奖励与转移函数，无需访问采样转移。该方法利用MDP与FP方程之间的对应关系，将MDP中的奖励最大化与FP动力学中的自由能最小化联系起来。这一联系使得我们能够通过变分系统识别方法推断FP势函数，进而利用解析表达式恢复完整的MDP组件——奖励、转移与策略。 我们在合成基准和修改版山车问题上验证了FP-IRL的有效性。结果表明，FP-IRL在准确恢复智能体激励的同时，保持了计算效率与物理可解释性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that traditional inverse reinforcement learning methods require prior knowledge of transition dynamics, which is often unavailable or unobservable, the paper introduces FP-IRL, a physics-constrained approach that jointly infers reward and transition functions from trajectory data without sampling transitions. It leverages the mathematical correspondence between Markov decision processes and Fokker-Planck dynamics, translating reward maximization into free energy minimization, and uses variational system identification to recover the FP potential function. Experimental results on synthetic benchmarks and a modified Mountain Car problem show that FP-IRL accurately recovers agent incentives while maintaining computational efficiency and physical interpretability.
为解决在未知或不可观测的动态系统中进行逆强化学习的难题，本文提出FP-IRL方法，该方法无需采样过渡动态，即可从轨迹数据中联合推断奖励与转移函数。其核心在于利用马尔可夫决策过程与福克-普朗克方程之间的数学对应关系，将奖励最大化转化为自由能最小化，并采用变分系统识别方法恢复FP势函数，进而通过解析表达式重建完整的MDP组件。在合成基准和修改版山地车问题上的实验表明，FP-IRL能准确恢复代理激励，同时保持计算效率与物理可解释性。
A Unified Framework of Hyperbolic Graph Representation Learning Methods
Authors: Sofía Pérez Casulo, Marcelo Fiori, Bernardo Marenco, Federico Larroca
First: 2026-04-30T16:17:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:17:53+00:00
Comments: submitted
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hyperbolic geometry has emerged as an effective latent space for representing complex networks, owing to its ability to capture hierarchical organization and heterogeneous connectivity patterns using low-dimensional embeddings. As a result, numerous hyperbolic graph representation learning methods have been proposed in recent years. However, their practical adoption and systematic comparison remain challenging, as implementations are fragmented and shared tools for reproducible and fair evaluation are lacking. In this work, we introduce a unified open-source framework for hyperbolic graph representation learning that integrates several widely used embedding methods under a common optimization interface. The novel framework enables consistent training, visualization, and evaluation of hyperbolic embeddings, and interfaces seamlessly with standard network analysis tools. Leveraging this unified setup, we conduct an experimental study of hyperbolic embedding methods on real-world networks, focusing on two canonical downstream tasks: link prediction and node classification. Beyond predictive accuracy, the study offers practical insights into the strengths and limitations of existing approaches, thereby facilitating informed method selection and fostering reproducible research in hyperbolic graph representation learning.

标题：超椭圆图表示学习方法的统一框架
由于超椭圆几何能够利用低维嵌入捕捉复杂网络的层次结构和异质连接模式，已成为表示复杂网络的有效潜在空间。近年来，已提出多种超椭圆图表示学习方法，但其实际应用和系统性比较仍具挑战性，因实现分散且缺乏可复现、公平评估的共享工具。本文引入一个统一的开源框架，整合多种常用嵌入方法于统一优化接口下，支持超椭圆嵌入的一致训练、可视化与评估，并无缝对接标准网络分析工具。基于此统一设置，我们在真实网络上开展实验研究，聚焦两个经典下游任务：链接预测与节点分类。除预测精度外，研究还提供现有方法优缺点的实用见解，助力方法选择并促进超椭圆图表示学习领域的可复现研究。

The framework enables consistent training, visualization, and evaluation of hyperbolic embeddings while seamlessly interfacing with standard network analysis tools. Experimental results on real-world networks, evaluated via link prediction and node classification tasks, reveal varying performance across methods and highlight their strengths and limitations, providing actionable guidance for method selection and promoting reproducible research in the field.
为解决现有超球面图表示学习方法缺乏统一评估和实际应用的问题，本文提出一个统一的开源框架，将多种主流嵌入方法整合到统一的优化接口下，支持一致的训练、可视化与评估，并无缝对接标准网络分析工具。在真实网络数据上，通过链接预测与节点分类两项下游任务的实验表明，不同方法表现各异，揭示了各自的优势与局限，为方法选择提供实用指导，并推动该领域研究的可复现性。
Assessing the Role of Intersection Proximity in Pedestrian Crashes: Insights from Data Mining Approach
Authors: Ahmed Hossain, Xiaoduan Sun, Subasish Das
First: 2026-04-30T16:12:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:12:13+00:00
Comments: 59 pages, 14 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although intersections are the most complex parts of the roadway network, pedestrian crashes at non-intersection locations are disproportionately frequent, highlighting a serious traffic safety concern. This study investigates non-intersection crashes involving pedestrians using a crash database (2017-2021) collected from Louisiana State. As the risk of pedestrian crashes tends to vary with distance from the intersection, the research team utilized a unique framework "distance to intersection" to capture the differences in crash patterns at non-intersection locations. The study identified that around 50% of non-intersection pedestrian crashes occurred within 198 ft. of the intersection. In the next step, the collected 3,135 pedestrian crashes at non-intersection locations during the study period were subdivided into three zones: D1 zone designates crashes occurring within 150 ft. of an intersection (1,277 crashes), D2 zone designates crashes occurring within 151 ft. to 435 ft. of an intersection (1,060 crashes) and D3 zone designates crashes occurring at 435 ft. or higher from an intersection (798 crashes). To explore the complex interaction of multiple factors, an intuitive data mining technique, Association Rules Mining was used. A total of the top 60 interesting association rules (20 for each zone) were identified by the algorithm (based on lift and support measures). In addition, a total of 124 rules were explored based on Lift Increase Criterion (LIC) measure. The findings of this research provide critical insights into pedestrian crash involvement at non-intersection locations and the variation in crash patterns according to the "distance to intersection". Based on the findings, some of the targeted problem-specific countermeasures are also recommended to address the crash patterns at non-intersection locations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：交叉口邻近性在行人事故中的作用评估：基于数据挖掘方法的见解
尽管交叉口是道路网络中最复杂的部分，但非交叉口位置的行人事故却异常频繁，凸显了严重的交通安全问题。本研究利用路易斯安那州收集的2017-2021年事故数据库，调查非交叉口涉及行人的事故。由于行人事故风险随与交叉口距离变化，研究团队采用“距交叉口距离”这一独特框架，捕捉非交叉口位置事故模式的差异。研究发现，约50%的非交叉口行人事故发生在距交叉口198英尺内。随后，研究将该期间收集的3,135起非交叉口行人事故划分为三个区域：D1区指距交叉口150英尺内事故（1,277起），D2区指距交叉口151至435英尺事故（1,060起），D3区指距交叉口435英尺及以上事故（798起）。为探索多因素复杂交互作用，研究采用直观的数据挖掘技术——关联规则挖掘。算法基于提升度和支撑度指标识别出各区域前60条有趣关联规则（每区20条）。此外，基于提升度增加准则（LIC）共探索124条规则。本研究结果为非交叉口行人事故参与情况及“距交叉口距离”对事故模式变化的影响提供了关键见解，并据此推荐若干针对性的对策以应对非交叉口事故模式。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the underappreciated safety risk of pedestrian crashes occurring away from intersections, despite intersections being the most complex roadway elements. Using Louisiana’s crash data from 2017–2021, researchers segmented non-intersection pedestrian crashes into three distance-based zones (D1: 0–150 ft, D2: 151–435 ft, D3: 435+ ft) and applied Association Rules Mining to uncover patterns. Results showed that nearly half of such crashes occurred within 198 ft of intersections, and the algorithm identified 60 top association rules (20 per zone) and 124 additional rules based on Lift Increase Criterion, revealing how proximity to intersections correlates with crash factors. The findings support targeted countermeasures to reduce pedestrian crashes in these high-risk zones.
本研究关注远离交叉口的行人事故，尽管交叉口是道路网络中最复杂的部分，但此类事故却异常频繁，构成严重交通安全问题。研究基于2017–2021年路易斯安那州的事故数据，按与交叉口距离将事故划分为三个区域：D1区（150英尺内）、D2区（151–435英尺）、D3区（435英尺以上），并采用关联规则挖掘技术分析各区域事故因素的交互模式，识别出60条高价值规则（每区20条）及124条基于提升度增量标准的规则。结果显示，约50%的非交叉口行人事故发生在距交叉口198英尺内，不同距离区域事故模式存在显著差异，为针对性安全改进措施提供了依据。
Decomposed Trust: Privacy, Adversarial Robustness, Ethics, and Fairness in Low-Rank LLMs
Authors: Daniel Agyei Asante, Md Mokarram Chowdhury, Yang Li
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-11-27T04:40:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:12:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have driven major advances across domains, yet their massive size hinders deployment in resource-constrained settings. Low-rank factorization addresses this challenge by compressing models to effectively reduce their computation and memory consumption while maintaining accuracy. While these compressed models boast benign performance and system-level advantages, their trustworthiness implications remain poorly understood. In this paper, we present the first comprehensive study of how low-rank factorization affects LLM trustworthiness across privacy, adversarial robustness, ethics, and fairness, complemented by an explainability-driven analysis of the internal mechanisms behind these trust-related changes. We evaluate multiple LLMs of different sizes and architectures compressed with various low-rank factorization algorithms, revealing key insights: (1) low-rank factorization preserves training data privacy but weakens the protection of personally identifiable information during conversations; (2) adversarial robustness is generally enhanced under compression; (3) ethics degrades in zero-shot prompting but partially recovers in few-shot prompting; (4) fairness declines under compression. Beyond compression, we investigate how model scale and fine-tuning affect trustworthiness. Additionally, to move beyond black-box analysis, we employ a gradient-based attribution to identify which layers of LLMs contribute most to adversarial robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：低秩LLM中的信任分解：隐私、对抗鲁棒性、伦理与公平性
大型语言模型（LLM）在多个领域推动了重大进展，但其庞大的规模阻碍了在资源受限环境中的部署。低秩因子分解通过压缩模型有效降低计算和内存消耗，同时保持精度。尽管这些压缩模型表现出良好的性能和系统级优势，其可信性影响仍不甚明了。本文首次全面研究了低秩因子分解如何影响LLM在隐私、对抗鲁棒性、伦理与公平性方面的可信性，并辅以可解释性分析，揭示其内部机制。 我们评估了多种不同规模和架构的LLM，使用不同低秩因子分解算法压缩后，得出关键发现：（1）低秩因子分解保护训练数据隐私，但削弱了对话中个人身份信息的保护；（2）压缩通常增强对抗鲁棒性；（3）伦理在零样本提示下退化，但在少样本提示下部分恢复；（4）公平性在压缩下下降。此外，我们还研究了模型规模和微调对可信性的影响。为超越黑盒分析，我们采用基于梯度的归因方法，识别LLM中对对抗鲁棒性贡献最大的层。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how low-rank factorization—a technique for compressing large language models (LLMs)—impacts their trustworthiness across privacy, adversarial robustness, ethics, and fairness. Motivated by the need to deploy LLMs in resource-constrained environments without sacrificing performance, the authors evaluate multiple LLMs compressed via various low-rank methods, revealing that while compression preserves training data privacy, it weakens protection of personally identifiable information during interaction, enhances adversarial robustness, degrades ethics in zero-shot settings but partially recovers with few-shot prompting, and reduces fairness. The study further explores how model scale and fine-tuning influence these properties and employs gradient-based attribution to identify layers most responsible for adversarial robustness.
该研究探讨了低秩分解技术对大语言模型（LLM）在隐私、对抗鲁棒性、伦理和公平性方面的可信度影响。为解决在资源受限环境下部署LLM的需求，作者评估了多种不同架构和规模的LLM在不同低秩压缩方法下的表现，发现压缩虽保留训练数据隐私，却削弱了对话中个人身份信息的保护，增强了对抗鲁棒性，降低了零样本提示下的伦理表现（但在少样本提示下部分恢复），并损害了公平性。研究还分析了模型规模与微调对可信度的影响，并通过梯度归因方法识别了对对抗鲁棒性贡献最大的模型层。
3D Reconstruction Techniques in the Manufacturing Domain: Applications, Research Opportunities and Use Cases
Authors: Chialoon Cheng, Kaijun liu, Zhiyang Liu, Marcelo H Ang
First: 2026-04-30T16:11:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:11:02+00:00
Comments: 24 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This comprehensive review examines the evolution and the current state of the art in three-dimensional (3D) reconstruction techniques in manufacturing applications. The analysis covers both traditional approaches and emerging deep learning methods, showing a critical research gap in unified 3d reconstruction frameworks. Through systematic review of 106 recent publications, we classify reconstruction techniques into three primary categories: data acquisition, point cloud generation, post-processing and applications. Non-contact methods, particularly structured light scanning and stereo vision, have shown significant adoption in manufacturing, with 47% of surveyed applications focusing on quality inspection. The integration of deep learning has enhanced reconstruction accuracy and processing speed, particularly in feature extraction and matching. Key applications span design and development (13%), machining (8%), process (17%), assembly (22%), and quality inspection (40%). While current technologies achieve sub-millimeter accuracy in controlled environments, challenges persist in handling reflective surfaces and dynamic environments. Our findings indicate a trend toward hybrid systems combining multiple sensor types and processing methods to overcome individual limitations. This survey provides a structured framework for understanding current capabilities and future directions in manufacturing-focused 3D reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：制造领域三维重建技术：应用、研究机会与用例
本综述全面考察了制造应用中三维（3D）重建技术的演变及其当前研究前沿。分析涵盖传统方法与新兴深度学习方法，揭示统一3D重建框架存在关键研究缺口。通过对106篇近期文献的系统回顾，我们将重建技术划分为三大类：数据采集、点云生成及后处理与应用。非接触式方法，特别是结构光扫描与立体视觉，在制造领域应用广泛，47%的调研应用聚焦于质量检测。深度学习的集成提升了重建精度与处理速度，尤其在特征提取与匹配方面。主要应用涵盖设计与开发（13%）、加工（8%）、工艺（17%）、装配（22%）及质量检测（40%）。当前技术在受控环境下可实现亚毫米级精度，但在处理反射表面与动态环境方面仍存挑战。研究发现，融合多种传感器与处理方法的混合系统正成为趋势，以克服单一方法的局限。本综述为理解制造导向3D重建的当前能力与未来方向提供了结构化框架。

It highlights the dominance of non-contact methods like structured light and stereo vision, especially in quality inspection (47% of applications), and shows how deep learning improves accuracy and speed in feature extraction.
