为应对制造业对高精度三维重建的需求，本文综述了106篇近期文献，梳理技术分类与研究缺口。将方法分为数据采集、点云生成与后处理，指出结构光与立体视觉等非接触式方法在质量检测中应用最广（47%）。深度学习提升特征提取与匹配效率，推动精度与速度提升。实验显示质量检测占应用40%，工艺监控17%，装配22%，在可控环境下可实现亚毫米精度，但反射表面与动态环境仍具挑战。研究建议融合多传感器与处理方法的混合系统为未来方向。
Measuring research data reuse in scholarly publications using generative artificial intelligence: Open Science Indicator development and preliminary results
Authors: Lauren Cadwallader, Iain Hrynaszkiewicz, parth sarin, Tim Vines
First: 2026-04-30T16:07:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:07:43+00:00
Comments: 12 pages. Submitted to 30th Annual International Conference on Science and Technology Indicators
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Numerous metascience studies and other initiatives have begun to monitor the prevalence of open science practices when it is more important to understand the 'downstream' effects or impacts of open science. PLOS and DataSeer have developed a new LLM-based indicator to measure an important effect of open science: the reuse of research data. Our results show a data reuse rate of 43%, which is higher than established bibliometric techniques. We show that data reuse can be measured at scale using LLMs and generative artificial intelligence. The positive effects of research data sharing and reuse may currently be underestimated.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用生成式人工智能衡量学术出版物中的研究数据重用：开放科学指标的开发与初步结果
大量元科学研究及其他倡议已开始监测开放科学实践的普及程度，但更关键的是理解开放科学的‘下游’效应或影响。PLOS 和 DataSeer 开发了一种基于大语言模型的新指标，用于衡量开放科学的一个重要效应——研究数据的重用。我们的结果显示，数据重用率为43%，高于传统文献计量技术。我们证明，可利用大语言模型和生成式人工智能大规模衡量数据重用。目前，研究数据共享与重用的积极效应可能被低估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to quantify the downstream impact of open science practices, researchers developed a novel LLM-based indicator to measure the reuse of research data in scholarly publications. Using generative artificial intelligence, the method automatically detects and counts data reuse instances across a large corpus of publications. Experimental results revealed a data reuse rate of 43%, significantly higher than what traditional bibliometric methods capture, suggesting that open data sharing may have a more substantial impact than previously estimated.
为量化开放科学实践的下游影响，研究人员开发了一种基于大语言模型的新指标，用于测量学术出版物中研究数据的再利用情况。该方法利用生成式人工智能自动识别和统计数据再利用实例，突破传统引文计量方法的局限。实验结果显示，数据再利用率为43%，显著高于传统方法的测算结果，表明当前对数据共享积极效应的评估可能被低估。
K2MUSE: A human lower-limb multimodal walking dataset spanning task and acquisition variability for rehabilitation robotics
Authors: Jiwei Li, Bi Zhang, Xiaowei Tan, Wanxin Chen, Zhaoyuan Liu, Juanjuan Zhang, Weiguang Huo, Jian Huang, Lianqing Liu, Xingang Zhao
First: 2025-04-20T13:03:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:06:30+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 30 figures,7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The natural interaction and control performance of lower limb rehabilitation robots are closely linked to biomechanical information from various human locomotion activities. Multidimensional human motion data significantly deepen the understanding of the complex mechanisms governing neuromuscular alterations, thereby facilitating the development and application of rehabilitation robots in multifaceted real-world environments.However, existing lower limb datasets are inadequate for supplying the essential multimodal data and large-scale gait samples necessary for the development of effective data-driven approaches, and the significant effects of acquisition interference in real applications are neglected.To fill this gap, we present the K2MUSE dataset, which includes a comprehensive collection of multimodal data, comprising kinematic, kinetic, amplitude mode ultrasound (AUS), and surface electromyography (sEMG) measurements. The proposed dataset includes lower-limb multimodal data collected from two cohorts, including 30 able-bodied young adults and 12 older adults, across different inclines (0$^\circ$, $\pm$5$^\circ$, and $\pm$10$^\circ$), speeds (0.5 m/s, 1.0 m/s, and 1.5 m/s), and representative non-ideal acquisition conditions (muscle fatigue, electrode shifts, and interday differences). The kinematic and ground reaction force data were collected with a Vicon motion capture system and an instrumented treadmill with embedded force plates, whereas the sEMG and AUS data of thirteen muscles on the bilateral lower limbs were synchronously recorded.K2MUSE is released with the corresponding structured documentation, preprocessing pipelines, and example code, thereby providing a comprehensive resource for rehabilitation robot development, biomechanical analysis, and wearable sensing research. The dataset is available at https://k2muse.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：K2MUSE：一个涵盖任务与采集变异性的下肢多模态步行数据集，用于康复机器人
下肢康复机器人的自然交互与控制性能与人类多种步行活动的生物力学信息密切相关。多维人体运动数据显著加深了对调控神经肌肉变化的复杂机制的理解，从而促进康复机器人在多场景真实环境中的开发与应用。然而，现有下肢数据集难以提供有效数据驱动方法所需的多模态数据和大规模步态样本，且忽视了实际应用中采集干扰的显著影响。为填补这一空白，我们提出了K2MUSE数据集，包含运动学、动力学、振幅模式超声（AUS）和表面肌电图（sEMG）的综合多模态数据。该数据集涵盖两组受试者（30名健康年轻成人和12名老年人）在不同坡度（0°、±5°、±10°）、速度（0.5 m/s、1.0 m/s、1.5 m/s）及代表性非理想采集条件（肌肉疲劳、电极移位、日间差异）下的下肢多模态数据。运动学和地面反作用力数据由Vicon动作捕捉系统与嵌入式力板的仪器化跑步机采集，而双侧下肢十三块肌肉的sEMG和AUS数据同步记录。K2MUSE数据集附带结构化文档、预处理流程及示例代码，为康复机器人开发、生物力学分析及可穿戴传感研究提供全面资源。数据集可访问：https://k2muse.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of comprehensive multimodal gait data for rehabilitation robotics, the K2MUSE dataset was created to capture kinematic, kinetic, amplitude mode ultrasound (AUS), and surface electromyography (sEMG) signals across diverse walking conditions including varying inclines, speeds, and real-world acquisition challenges like muscle fatigue and electrode drift. Data was collected from 30 young and 12 older adults using a Vicon motion capture system and instrumented treadmill, with synchronized sEMG and AUS recordings from 13 bilateral lower limb muscles. The dataset enables robust data-driven modeling by incorporating variability in task and acquisition conditions, and is accompanied by structured documentation and preprocessing tools for research in biomechanics and wearable sensing.
为弥补康复机器人开发中缺乏全面多模态步态数据的不足，作者构建了K2MUSE数据集，涵盖30名健康青年和12名老年参与者在不同坡度、速度及非理想采集条件（如肌肉疲劳、电极移位、日间差异）下的运动学、动力学、幅度模式超声（AUS）和表面肌电图（sEMG）数据。数据通过Vicon动作捕捉系统与嵌入式力板的仪器化跑步机采集，同步记录13块双下肢肌肉的sEMG与AUS信号。该数据集附带结构化文档、预处理流程与示例代码，支持康复机器人开发、生物力学分析及可穿戴传感研究。
Framework for Collaborative Operation of Autonomous Delivery Vehicles Within a Marshaling Yard
Authors: James O'Hara, Karl Wunderlich, Gregory Stevens
First: 2026-04-30T16:02:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:02:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As autonomous vehicles slowly deploy into urban roads for limited use cases with significant edge case issues, closed facilities like marshaling yards provide a ripe case for combining lower-level vehicle autonomy with fixed infrastructure to create full autonomy without similar edge case concerns. Within a delivery marshaling yard, electric fleet vehicles complete a set of sequential tasks (charging, inspection, cleaning, and loading) before exiting the yard with their new load of deliveries. Hybrid automation of the vehicles and infrastructure can allow these vehicles to reach full autonomy and navigate the facility without the need of a driver, allowing for quicker movement between tasks increasing vehicle throughput. However, isolated autonomous operations based on static rules are prone to gridlock causing facility failures that temporarily shut down operations. Our orchestrated autonomy solution uses decentralized, dynamic priority scoring of vehicles based on the current status of the marshaling yard to optimally assign vehicles to tasks to increase vehicle throughput. Using a simulated facility with three marshaling yard sizes (small, medium, and large) and three demand levels (low, medium, high), we demonstrated that our orchestration solution increases vehicle throughput above static, isolated autonomy for all combinations of yard size and demand, while reducing facility failures at high demand levels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：编组站内自动驾驶配送车辆协同作业框架
随着自动驾驶车辆逐步在城市道路有限场景中部署并面临大量边缘案例问题，封闭设施如编组站为结合车辆低级自主性与固定基础设施，实现无类似边缘案例担忧的完全自主性提供了理想场景。在配送编组站内，电动车队车辆需依次完成充电、检查、清洁和装载等任务后，携新一批配送货物驶离站场。车辆与基础设施的混合自动化可使车辆实现完全自主，无需驾驶员即可在站内导航，从而加快任务间移动速度，提升车辆吞吐量。然而，仅依赖静态规则的孤立自动驾驶易导致拥堵，引发设施故障并暂时中断运营。我们的协同自主解决方案采用基于编组站当前状态的分布式动态优先级评分机制，最优分配车辆至任务，以提升车辆吞吐量。通过模拟包含三种站场规模（小、中、大）和三种需求水平（低、中、高）的设施，我们证明该协同方案在所有站场规模与需求组合下均优于静态孤立自主方案，同时在高需求水平下显著降低设施故障率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to achieve full autonomy for delivery vehicles in controlled environments without edge case risks, this study proposes a decentralized orchestration framework that dynamically assigns tasks to autonomous vehicles in a marshaling yard based on real-time facility status. The method uses priority scoring to optimize vehicle-task allocation, avoiding gridlock and improving throughput. Experimental results across three yard sizes and three demand levels show that the proposed system consistently outperforms static, isolated autonomy, increasing throughput and reducing facility failures under high demand.
为在无城市复杂边缘案例干扰的封闭环境中实现配送车辆的全自动驾驶，本文提出一种基于实时场地状态动态分配任务的分布式协同框架。该方法通过优先级评分协调车辆在充电、检测、清洁和装载等任务间的调度，避免交通堵塞并提升吞吐量。仿真实验表明，无论场地规模或需求高低，该方案均优于静态独立自动驾驶，尤其在高需求下显著减少设施故障并提高车辆效率。
RHyVE: Competence-Aware Verification and Phase-Aware Deployment for LLM-Generated Reward Hypotheses
Authors: Feiyu Wu, Xu Zheng, Zhuocheng Wang, Yi ming Dai, Hui Li
First: 2026-04-30T16:01:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:01:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) make reward design in reinforcement learning substantially more scalable, but generated rewards are not automatically reliable training objectives. Existing work has focused primarily on generating, evolving, or selecting reward candidates, while paying less attention to when such candidates can be verified and deployed during policy optimization. We study this deployment-time problem by treating generated rewards as reward hypotheses whose utility depends on the competence of the current policy and the phase of training. We propose \textsc{RHyVE}, a competence-aware verification and phase-aware deployment protocol that compares small sets of reward hypotheses from shared policy checkpoints using short-horizon fork verification. Our experiments show that reward rankings are unreliable at low competence but become informative after task-dependent thresholds. On a sparse manipulation task, phase-aware deployment improves peak and retained performance under a locked protocol. Updated LLM-generated reward-candidate experiments show candidate-family-dependent behavior: generated pools can exhibit phase-dependent winner changes, but no fixed warm-up schedule is universally optimal. Held-out schedule selection, conservative selector baselines, compute-matched controls, and scale controls further show that \textsc{RHyVE} is best understood as a verification-informed deployment protocol rather than a universal scheduler. Dense and all-failure boundary experiments delimit the scope of the method. Together, these results suggest that reward generation and reward deployment should be studied as coupled problems: generated rewards must be verified and deployed under changing policy competence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RHyVE：面向LLM生成奖励假设的胜任力感知验证与阶段感知部署
大语言模型（LLMs）使强化学习中的奖励设计显著更具可扩展性，但生成的奖励并非自动可靠的学习目标。现有工作主要关注生成、演化或选择奖励候选，而较少关注这些候选在策略优化过程中何时可被验证和部署。我们通过将生成奖励视为依赖于当前策略胜任力和训练阶段的奖励假设，研究这一部署时机问题。我们提出RHyVE，一种胜任力感知验证与阶段感知部署协议，通过短时程分叉验证比较共享策略检查点中的少量奖励假设。实验表明，低胜任力时奖励排序不可靠，但达到任务相关阈值后变得有信息量。在稀疏操作任务中，阶段感知部署在锁定协议下提升了峰值与保留性能。更新的LLM生成奖励候选实验显示候选族依赖行为：生成池可呈现阶段依赖的胜者变化，但无普适最优预热方案。保留调度选择、保守选择器基线、计算匹配对照与规模对照进一步表明，RHyVE更宜理解为验证引导的部署协议而非通用调度器。密集与全失败边界实验限定了方法适用范围。综合结果表明，奖励生成与部署应作为耦合问题研究：生成奖励必须在策略胜任力变化时进行验证与部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reliably deploy LLM-generated reward hypotheses during reinforcement learning, the authors propose RHyVE, a protocol that verifies and deploys rewards based on current policy competence and training phase. RHyVE uses short-horizon fork verification to compare small reward sets from shared policy checkpoints, enabling phase-aware deployment. Experiments show reward rankings are unreliable at low policy competence but become informative after task-specific thresholds; phase-aware deployment boosts peak and retained performance on a sparse manipulation task. Results also reveal that reward candidate families behave differently across phases, with no universal warm-up schedule, and that RHyVE’s value lies in its verification-informed deployment rather than as a fixed scheduler, especially under compute and scale constraints.
为解决LLM生成奖励在强化学习中部署不可靠的问题，作者提出RHyVE协议，根据当前策略能力与训练阶段动态验证并部署奖励。通过在策略检查点间进行短时程分叉验证，评估小规模奖励集合以判断其有效性。实验表明，奖励排序在策略能力较低时不可靠，但达到任务特定阈值后趋于稳定；阶段感知部署可提升稀疏操控任务中的峰值与保留性能。结果还显示，奖励候选集具有家族依赖性，无普适预热策略，支持RHyVE作为验证引导的部署工具而非通用调度器，其适用范围受限于密集与全失败边界条件。
PROMISE-AD: Progression-aware Multi-horizon Survival Estimation for Alzheimer's Disease Progression and Dynamic Tracking
Authors: Qing Lyu, Jeremy Hudson, Mohammad Kawas, Yuming Jiang, Chenyu You, Christopher T Whitlow
First: 2026-04-30T16:01:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:01:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Individualized Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression prediction requires models that use irregular visits, account for censoring, avoid diagnostic leakage, and provide calibrated horizon risks. We propose PROgression-aware MultI-horizon Survival Estimation for Alzheimer's Disease (PROMISE-AD), a leakage-safe survival framework for predicting conversion from cognitively normal (CN) to mild cognitive impairment (MCI) and from MCI to AD dementia using ADNI/TADPOLE tabular histories. PROMISE-AD converts pre-index visits into tokens with standardized measurements, missingness masks, longitudinal changes, time-normalized slopes, visit timing, and non-diagnostic categorical attributes. A temporal Transformer fuses global, attention-pooled, and latest-visit representations to estimate a progression score and latent discrete-time mixture hazards. Training combines survival likelihood, horizon-specific focal risk loss, progression ranking, hazard smoothness, and mixture-balance regularization, followed by validation-set isotonic calibration for 1-, 2-, 3-, and 5-year risks. In held-out testing across three seeds, PROMISE-AD achieved an integrated Brier score (IBS) of 0.085 $\pm$ 0.012, C-index of 0.808 $\pm$ 0.015, and mean time-dependent AUC of 0.840 $\pm$ 0.081 for CN-to-MCI conversion, yielding the lowest IBS among compared methods. For MCI-to-AD conversion, PROMISE-AD achieved the highest C-index (0.894 $\pm$ 0.018) and near-ceiling 5-year discrimination (AUROC 0.997 $\pm$ 0.003; AUPRC 0.999 $\pm$ 0.001), although some baselines had lower IBS. Ablations and interpretability supported longitudinal change features, fused temporal representations, mixture hazards, cognitive and functional measures, APOE4 status, and recent conversion-proximal visits. These findings suggest that progression-aware survival modeling can provide interpretable multi-horizon AD conversion risk estimates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PROMISE-AD：面向阿尔茨海默病进展与动态追踪的进展感知多时际生存估计
个体化阿尔茨海默病（AD）进展预测需要能够处理不规则就诊、考虑删失、避免诊断泄漏并提供校准时际风险的模型。我们提出PROMISE-AD（面向阿尔茨海默病的进展感知多时际生存估计），这是一种安全的生存框架，用于基于ADNI/TADPOLE表格历史数据预测从认知正常（CN）到轻度认知障碍（MCI）及从MCI到AD痴呆的转化。PROMISE-AD将预索引就诊转化为包含标准化测量、缺失掩码、纵向变化、时间标准化斜率、就诊时间及非诊断性分类属性的令牌。一个时序Transformer融合全局、注意力池化及最新就诊表示，以估计进展评分和潜在离散时间混合风险。训练结合生存似然、时际特定焦点风险损失、进展排序、风险平滑性及混合平衡正则化，并通过验证集等距校准获得1、2、3、5年风险。在三个种子的独立测试中，PROMISE-AD在CN到MCI转化中取得综合Brier分数（IBS）0.085±0.012、C指数0.808±0.015、平均时变AUC 0.840±0.081，为对比方法中最低IBS。在MCI到AD转化中，PROMISE-AD取得最高C指数（0.894±0.018）及接近天花板的5年鉴别能力（AUROC 0.997±0.003；AUPRC 0.999±0.001），尽管部分基线方法IBS更低。消融实验与可解释性分析支持纵向变化特征、融合时序表示、混合风险、认知与功能指标、APOE4状态及近期转化临近就诊。这些发现表明，进展感知的生存建模可提供可解释的多时际AD转化风险估计。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of predicting individualized Alzheimer’s disease progression using irregular clinical visits and censored data while avoiding diagnostic leakage, PROMISE-AD introduces a leakage-safe survival model that encodes longitudinal patient histories into structured tokens capturing missingness, time-normalized slopes, and visit timing. It employs a temporal Transformer to fuse global, attention-pooled, and latest-visit representations to estimate a progression score and discrete-time mixture hazards. Trained with survival likelihood, focal risk loss, ranking, smoothness, and mixture-balance regularization, and calibrated via isotonic regression, PROMISE-AD outperformed baselines on CN-to-MCI conversion with the lowest integrated Brier score (0.085 ± 0.012) and C-index (0.808 ± 0.015), and achieved the highest C-index (0.894 ± 0.018) and near-ceiling discrimination (AUROC 0.997 ± 0.003) for MCI-to-AD conversion.
为解决阿尔茨海默病进展预测中因不规则随访、删失数据和诊断泄漏导致的挑战，PROMISE-AD提出一种基于时间Transformer的生存模型，将纵向数据编码为包含缺失掩码、时间标准化斜率和就诊时间的结构化令牌。模型融合全局、注意力池化和最新就诊表征，估计进展分数与离散时间混合风险，通过生存似然、焦点风险损失、排序、平滑性及正则化联合训练，并在验证集上进行等度校准。在留出测试中，其在CN到MCI转化预测中IBS最低（0.085），在MCI到AD转化中C-index最高（0.894），5年鉴别能力接近天花板（AUROC 0.997），表明其在多时间点风险预测中具备优越的校准与区分能力。
To Build or Not to Build? Factors that Lead to Non-Development or Abandonment of AI Systems
Authors: Shreya Chappidi, Jatinder Singh
First: 2026-04-30T16:00:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:00:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACM FAccT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Responsible AI research typically focuses on examining the use and impacts of deployed AI systems. Yet, there is currently limited visibility into the pre-deployment decisions to pursue building such systems in the first place. Decisions taken in the earlier stages of development shape which systems are ultimately released, and therefore represent potential, but underexplored, points for intervention. As such, this paper investigates factors influencing AI non-development and abandonment throughout the development lifecycle. Specifically, we first perform a scoping review of academic literature, civil society resources, and grey literature including journalism and industry reports. Through thematic analysis of these sources, we develop a taxonomy of six categories of factors contributing to AI abandonment: ethical concerns, stakeholder feedback, development lifecycle challenges, organizational dynamics, resource constraints, and legal/regulatory concerns. Then, we collect data on real-world case of AI system abandonment via an AI incident database and a practitioner survey to evidence and compare factors that drive abandonment both prior to and following system deployment. While academic responsible AI communities often emphasize ethical risks as reasons to not develop AI, our empirical analysis of these cases demonstrates the diverse, and often non-ethics-related, levers that motivate organizations to abandon AI development. Synthesizing evidence from our taxonomy and related case study analyses, we identify gaps and opportunities in current responsible AI research to (1) engage with the diverse range of levers that influence organizations to abandon AI development, and (2) better support appropriate (dis)engagement with AI system development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：构建还是不构建？导致AI系统未开发或被放弃的因素
负责任的AI研究通常聚焦于已部署AI系统的使用与影响，但目前对决定是否最初启动此类系统开发的前期决策缺乏可见性。早期开发阶段的决策塑造了最终发布系统的形态，因此代表了潜在但尚未充分探索的干预点。本文研究了影响AI系统在开发周期内未开发或被放弃的因素。首先，我们对学术文献、民间社会资源及灰色文献（包括新闻报道和行业报告）进行范围综述，通过主题分析构建了六类导致AI放弃的因素分类：伦理关切、利益相关者反馈、开发周期挑战、组织动态、资源限制及法律/监管顾虑。随后，我们通过AI事件数据库和从业者调查收集真实案例数据，以实证比较驱动AI放弃的前部署与后部署因素。尽管学术界负责任AI社区常强调伦理风险是不开发AI的原因，但我们的实证分析表明，促使组织放弃AI开发的动因多样，且常与伦理无关。综合分类证据及相关案例研究，我们识别出当前负责任AI研究中的差距与机遇：（1）应关注影响组织放弃AI开发的多元动因；（2）更好地支持组织对AI系统开发的适当参与或退出。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the underexplored pre-deployment phase of AI development by investigating why organizations choose not to build or abandon AI systems. Through a scoping review and thematic analysis of academic, civil society, and industry sources, the authors construct a taxonomy of six key factors: ethical concerns, stakeholder feedback, development lifecycle challenges, organizational dynamics, resource constraints, and legal/regulatory issues. They then validate these categories using real-world case data from an AI incident database and practitioner surveys, revealing that while ethics are often highlighted in responsible AI discourse, actual abandonment is driven by a wide range of non-ethical factors. The study concludes that current responsible AI research overlooks these diverse levers and calls for broader engagement with the decision-making processes that lead to AI non-development or abandonment.
本文聚焦于AI系统开发前的决策阶段，探讨组织为何选择不开发或放弃AI项目。通过文献综述与主题分析，作者构建了六个导致AI项目被放弃的关键因素分类：伦理顾虑、利益相关者反馈、开发周期挑战、组织动态、资源限制及法律监管问题。随后，通过AI事件数据库与从业者调查实证分析，发现尽管学术界常强调伦理风险，但实际放弃项目的主要动因多为非伦理因素，如资源不足、利益相关者反对或组织不匹配。研究呼吁负责任AI研究应拓展视野，纳入这些多元驱动因素，以更有效地支持组织对AI开发的合理参与或退出决策。
Agent-Agnostic Evaluation of SQL Accuracy in Production Text-to-SQL Systems
Authors: Taslim Jamal Arif, Kuldeep Singh
First: 2026-04-30T15:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:59:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-SQL (T2SQL) evaluation in production environments poses fundamental challenges that existing benchmarks do not address. Current evaluation methodologies whether rule-based SQL matching or schema-dependent semantic parsers assume access to ground-truth queries and structured database schema, constraints that are rarely satisfied in real-world deployments. This disconnect leaves production T2SQL agents largely unevaluated beyond developer-time testing, creating silent quality degradation with no feedback mechanism for continuous improvement. We present STEF (Schema-agnostic Text-to-SQL Evaluation Framework), a production-native evaluation system that operates exclusively on natural language inputs the user question, an enriched reformulation, and the generated SQL without requiring database schema or reference queries. STEF extracts semantic specifications from both natural language and SQL representations, performs normalized feature alignment, and produces an interpretable 0 to 100 accuracy score via a composite metric that encompasses filter alignment, semantic verdict, and confidence of the evaluator. Key contributions include: enriched question quality validation as a first-class evaluation signal, configurable application-specific rule injection via prompt templating, and production-robust normalization handling GROUP BY tolerance, ORDER BY defaults, and LIMIT heuristics. Empirical results demonstrate that STEF enables continuous production monitoring and agent improvement feedback loops without schema dependency, making structured query evaluation viable at scale for the first time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生产环境中SQL准确性的代理无关评估
生产环境中文本到SQL（T2SQL）的评估面临现有基准未解决的基本挑战。当前评估方法，无论是基于规则的SQL匹配还是依赖于数据库结构的语义解析器，均假设可访问真实查询和结构化数据库模式，而这些条件在实际部署中很少满足。这种脱节导致生产T2SQL代理在开发阶段测试之外几乎未被评估，从而造成质量无声退化且缺乏持续改进的反馈机制。我们提出STEF（无模式依赖的文本到SQL评估框架），这是一个原生适用于生产环境的评估系统，仅依赖自然语言输入——用户问题、增强重述和生成的SQL，无需数据库模式或参考查询。STEF从自然语言和SQL表示中提取语义规范，执行归一化特征对齐，并通过综合指标（涵盖过滤器对齐、语义判断和评估者置信度）生成可解释的0至100分准确率。主要贡献包括：将增强问题质量验证作为首要评估信号、通过提示模板实现可配置的应用特定规则注入、以及生产环境鲁棒的归一化处理（支持GROUP BY容忍、ORDER BY默认值和LIMIT启发式）。实证结果表明，STEF无需依赖模式即可实现持续生产监控和代理改进反馈循环，首次使结构化查询评估在大规模场景下成为可能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of realistic evaluation methods for production text-to-SQL systems that do not require ground-truth SQL or database schemas, the authors introduce STEF, a schema-agnostic evaluation framework that assesses SQL accuracy using only natural language questions, enriched reformulations, and generated SQL. STEF extracts semantic specifications from both inputs, aligns features through normalization, and computes a composite 0–100 score based on filter alignment, semantic verdict, and evaluator confidence. Experimental results show STEF enables continuous monitoring and feedback for model improvement in production without schema access, validating its scalability and robustness to SQL variations like GROUP BY, ORDER BY, and LIMIT.
为解决现有评估方法依赖数据库结构和参考SQL的问题，作者提出STEF框架，仅通过自然语言问题、改写版本和生成SQL即可评估SQL准确性。该框架从语言和SQL中提取语义规范，进行特征对齐与标准化处理，生成0–100分的综合评分，涵盖过滤器匹配、语义判断与评估置信度。实验表明，STEF可在无数据库结构依赖下实现生产环境持续监控与模型改进反馈，首次实现大规模可扩展的结构化查询评估。
Stable Behavior, Limited Variation: Persona Validity in LLM Agents for Urban Sentiment Perception
Authors: Neemias B da Silva, Rodrigo Minetto, Daniel Silver, Thiago H Silva
First: 2026-04-30T15:59:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:59:11+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 8 figures. IEEE DCOSS - UrbCom
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as proxies for human perception in urban analysis, yet it remains unclear whether persona prompting produces meaningful and reproducible behavioral diversity. We investigate whether distinct personas influence urban sentiment judgments generated by multimodal LLMs. Using a factorial set of personas spanning gender, economic status, political orientation, and personality, we instantiate multiple agents per persona to evaluate urban scene images from the PerceptSent dataset and assess both within-persona consistency and cross-persona variation. Results show strong convergence among agents sharing a persona, indicating stable and reproducible behavior. However, cross-persona differentiation is limited: economic status and personality induce statistically detectable but practically modest variation, while gender shows no measurable effect and political orientation only negligible impact. Agents also exhibit an extremity bias, collapsing intermediate sentiment categories common in human annotations. As a result, performance remains strong on coarse-grained polarity tasks but degrades as sentiment resolution increases, suggesting that simple label-based persona prompting does not capture fine-grained perceptual judgments. To isolate the contribution of persona conditioning, we additionally evaluate the same model without personas. Surprisingly, the no-persona model sometimes matches or exceeds persona-conditioned agreement with human labels across all task variants, suggesting that simple label-based persona prompting may add limited annotation value in this setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稳定行为、有限变异：大语言模型代理在城市情感感知中的角色有效性
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地被用作城市分析中人类感知的代理，但尚不清楚角色提示是否能产生有意义且可重复的行为多样性。我们研究不同角色是否会影响多模态LLM生成的城市情感判断。通过涵盖性别、经济地位、政治倾向和人格的因子化角色集合，我们为每个角色实例化多个代理，评估PerceptSent数据集中的城市场景图像，并评估角色内一致性与角色间变异。结果显示，共享同一角色的代理间高度收敛，表明行为稳定且可重复。然而，角色间差异有限：经济地位和人格可检测到统计学上的但实际意义较小的变异，而性别无显著影响，政治倾向仅产生微弱影响。代理还表现出极端偏倚，将人类标注中常见的中间情感类别压缩。因此，模型在粗粒度极性任务上表现强劲，但随着情感分辨率提高而性能下降，表明简单的标签式角色提示无法捕捉精细感知判断。为分离角色条件化的影响，我们还评估了无角色模型。令人惊讶的是，无角色模型在所有任务变体中有时与角色条件化模型在与人类标签的一致性上相当甚至更优，表明在此设定下，简单的标签式角色提示可能仅提供有限的标注价值。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether persona prompting in multimodal LLMs produces meaningful behavioral diversity for urban sentiment perception. Using a factorial design with personas defined by gender, economic status, political orientation, and personality, the researchers instantiated multiple agents per persona to evaluate urban scene images and measure both within-persona consistency and cross-persona variation. Results show high behavioral stability within each persona, but minimal cross-persona differentiation—economic status and personality induced small, statistically detectable differences, while gender and political orientation had negligible effects. Agents also exhibited an extremity bias, collapsing intermediate sentiment categories, leading to strong performance on coarse-grained polarity tasks but degradation at finer resolution. Notably, a no-persona baseline sometimes matched or exceeded persona-conditioned performance, suggesting label-based persona prompting adds little perceptual value.
本研究探讨在多模态大语言模型中，通过人格提示是否能产生有意义且可重复的行为多样性，用于城市情感感知。研究采用因子设计，以性别、经济地位、政治倾向和性格为维度构建人格，为每个角色实例化多个代理，评估城市场景图像并测量角色内一致性与角色间差异。结果表明，同一人格下的代理行为高度稳定，但角色间差异有限——经济地位和性格能引发微小但统计显著的差异，而性别和政治倾向影响可忽略。代理还表现出极端倾向，压缩了人类标注中常见的中间情感类别，导致在粗粒度极性任务上表现良好，但在细粒度情感任务中性能下降。值得注意的是，无人格基线模型在某些任务中表现甚至优于人格引导模型，表明基于标签的人格提示在此场景下贡献有限。
TAFA-GSGC: Group-wise Scalable Point Cloud Geometry Compression with Progressive Residual Refinement
Authors: Xiumei Li, Alexander Kopte, André Kaup
First: 2026-04-30T15:57:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:57:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE International Conference on Image Processing (ICIP) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scalable compression is essential for bandwidth-adaptive transmission, yet most learned codecs are optimized for a fixed rate-distortion point, making rate adaptation costly due to re-encoding or maintaining multiple bitstreams. In this work, we propose TAFA-GSGC, a scalable learned point cloud geometry codec that enables multi-quality decoding from a single bitstream and a single trained model. TAFA-GSGC combines layered residual refinement with channel-group entropy coding, and introduces Target-Aligned Feature Aggregation module to reduce cross-layer redundancy in enhancement residuals. Our framework supports up to 9 decodable quality levels with monotonic quality improvement as more subbitstreams are received, while maintaining strong compression efficiency. Compared with the baseline PCGCv2, TAFA-GSGC attains comparable and slightly better RD performance, achieving average BD-Rate savings of -4.99% in D1 and -5.92% in D2.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TAFA-GSGC：基于渐进残差细化的组级可扩展点云几何压缩
可扩展压缩对带宽自适应传输至关重要，但大多数学习型编码器针对固定码率-失真点进行优化，导致速率自适应成本高昂，需重新编码或维护多个码流。本文提出TAFA-GSGC，一种可扩展的学习型点云几何编码器，支持从单一码流和单一训练模型中解码多质量级别。TAFA-GSGC结合分层残差细化与通道组熵编码，并引入目标对齐特征聚合模块以降低增强残差层间冗余。该框架支持最高9级可解码质量，且随子码流接收逐步单调提升质量，同时保持高效压缩性能。相比基线PCGCv2，TAFA-GSGC实现相当甚至略优的RD性能，在D1和D2数据集上平均BD-Rate分别降低4.99%和5.92%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for bandwidth-adaptive point cloud compression without costly re-encoding, this work introduces TAFA-GSGC, a scalable learned codec that enables multi-quality decoding from a single bitstream and trained model. It combines layered residual refinement with channel-group entropy coding and employs a Target-Aligned Feature Aggregation module to reduce cross-layer redundancy. Experimental results show the codec supports up to nine decodable quality levels with monotonic quality improvement, while achieving average BD-Rate savings of -4.99% in D1 and -5.92% in D2 compared to PCGCv2, demonstrating comparable or slightly better rate-distortion efficiency.
为实现无需重编码的带宽自适应点云传输，本文提出TAFA-GSGC，一种支持单比特流多质量解码的可扩展几何编码器。该方法结合分层残差细化与通道组熵编码，并引入目标对齐特征聚合模块以降低跨层冗余。实验表明，该编码器支持最高9级可解码质量，且随子比特流接收逐步提升质量，相较PCGCv2平均BD-Rate节省4.99%（D1）和5.92%（D2），压缩效率相当或更优。
Collaborative Agent Reasoning Engineering (CARE): A Three-Party Design Methodology for Systematically Engineering AI Agents with Subject Matter Experts, Developers, and Helper Agents
Authors: Rahul Ramachandran, Nidhi Jha, Muthukumaran Ramasubramanian
First: 2026-04-30T15:54:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:54:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Collaborative Agent Reasoning Engineering (CARE), a disciplined methodology for engineering Large Language Model (LLM) agents in scientific domains. Unlike ad-hoc trial-and-error approaches, CARE specifies behavior, grounding, tool orchestration, and verification through reusable artifacts and systematic, stage-gated phases. The methodology employs a three-party workflow involving Subject-Matter Experts (SMEs), developers, and LLM-based helper agents. These helper agents function as facilitation infrastructure, transforming informal domain intent into structured, reviewable specifications for human approval at defined gates. CARE addresses the "jagged technological frontier", characterized by uneven LLM performance, by bridging the gap between novice and expert analysts regarding domain constraints and verification practices. By generating concrete artifacts, including interaction requirements, reasoning policies, and evaluation criteria, CARE ensures agent behavior is specifiable, testable, and maintainable. Evaluation results from a scientific use case demonstrate that this stage-gated, artifact-driven methodology yields measurable improvements in development efficiency and complex-query performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协作代理推理工程（CARE）：一种结合领域专家、开发者与辅助代理的三方设计方法论，用于系统化构建人工智能代理
我们提出协作代理推理工程（CARE），这是一种用于科学领域大型语言模型（LLM）代理工程的规范方法。与随意试错的方法不同，CARE通过可复用的成果和分阶段、关卡控制的流程，明确指定行为、锚定、工具编排和验证。该方法采用三方工作流，涉及领域专家（SMEs）、开发者和基于LLM的辅助代理。这些辅助代理作为促进基础设施，将非正式的领域意图转化为结构化、可审查的规范，供人类在指定关卡批准。CARE应对“参差不齐的技术前沿”——即LLM性能不均衡的问题，弥合新手与专家分析员在领域约束和验证实践之间的差距。通过生成具体成果，包括交互需求、推理策略和评估标准，CARE确保代理行为可指定、可测试、可维护。科学用例的评估结果表明，这种分阶段、成果驱动的方法显著提升了开发效率和复杂查询性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiencies and inconsistency of ad-hoc LLM agent development, CARE introduces a structured, three-party methodology involving Subject-Matter Experts, developers, and LLM helper agents to systematically engineer AI agents for scientific domains. The method enforces behavior specification, grounding, tool orchestration, and verification through reusable artifacts and stage-gated phases, with helper agents translating informal domain intent into formal, reviewable specifications. Experimental results in a scientific use case show that this artifact-driven, gated approach significantly improves development efficiency and performance on complex queries compared to unstructured methods.
为解决科学领域中LLM代理开发缺乏系统性与可靠性的问题，CARE提出一种三角色协作方法，由领域专家、开发者和基于LLM的辅助代理共同参与。辅助代理将非结构化领域意图转化为可审查的规范文档，支持阶段性人工审核与可追溯的开发流程。实验表明，该方法显著提升了开发效率与复杂查询性能。
SpecVQA: A Benchmark for Spectral Understanding and Visual Question Answering in Scientific Images
Authors: Jialu Shen, Han Lyu, Suyang Zhong, Hanzheng Li, Haoyi Tao, Nan Wang, Changhong Chen, Xi Fang
First: 2026-04-30T15:51:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:51:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spectra are a prevalent yet highly information-dense form of scientific imagery, presenting substantial challenges to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) due to their unstructured and domain-specific characteristics. Here we introduce SpecVQA, a professional scientific-image benchmark for evaluating multimodal models on scientific spectral understanding, covering 7 representative spectrum types with expert-annotated question-answer pairs. The aim comprises two aspects: spectra scientific QA evaluation and corresponding underlying task evaluation. SpecVQA contains 620 figures and 3100 QA pairs curated from peer-reviewed literature, targeting both direct information extraction and domain-specific reasoning. To effectively reduce token length while preserving essential curve characteristics, we propose a spectral data sampling and interpolation reconstruction approach. Ablation studies further confirm that the approach achieves substantial performance improvements on the proposed benchmark. We test the capability of prominent MLLMs in scientific spectral understanding on our benchmark and present a leaderboard. This work represents an essential step toward enhancing spectral understanding in multimodal large models and suggests promising directions for extending visual-language models to broader scientific research and data analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpecVQA：科学图像中光谱理解与视觉问答的基准
光谱是广泛存在且信息密度极高的科学图像形式，因其无结构化和领域特异性特征，对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）构成重大挑战。本文引入SpecVQA，一个专业科学图像基准，用于评估多模态模型在科学光谱理解方面的表现，涵盖7种代表性光谱类型，并配有专家标注的问答对。目标包含两个方面：光谱科学问答评估及对应底层任务评估。SpecVQA包含620幅图像和3100组问答对，源自同行评审文献，旨在支持直接信息提取与领域特定推理。为有效缩短token长度同时保留关键曲线特征，我们提出光谱数据采样与插值重建方法。消融实验进一步证实该方法在本基准上显著提升性能。我们在本基准上测试主流MLLMs在科学光谱理解中的能力，并发布排行榜。本工作是提升多模态大模型光谱理解能力的重要一步，也为扩展视觉-语言模型至更广泛的科学研究与数据分析指明了方向。

为评估多模态大语言模型在科学光谱图像上的理解能力，本研究构建了SpecVQA基准，包含620幅光谱图像与3100组专家标注的问答对，覆盖七类典型光谱。为在压缩模型输入的同时保留曲线关键特征，作者提出光谱采样与插值重建方法，消融实验表明该方法显著提升模型性能。在SpecVQA上对主流多模态模型的测试显示其在光谱理解上存在明显不足，作者发布排行榜以推动模型改进。
Early Detection of Water Stress by Plant Electrophysiology: Machine Learning for Irrigation Management
Authors: Eduard Buss, Till Aust, Heiko Hamann
First: 2026-04-30T15:49:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:49:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Purpose: Fast detection of plant stress is key to plant phenotyping, precision agriculture, and automated crop management. In particular, efficient irrigation management requires early identification of water stress to optimize resource use while maintaining crop performance. Direct physiological sensing offers the potential to detect stress responses before visible symptoms appear. Methods: In this study, we recorded electrophysiological signals from greenhouse-grown tomato plants subjected to water stress and developed a framework based on machine learning for online stress detection. The recorded time-series data were processed using a processing pipeline that includes statistical feature extraction and selection, automated machine learning or alternatively deep learning, and probability calibration. Results: Across multiple input time horizons, we found that a 30-minute look-back window strikes the best balance between rapid decision-making and classification performance. Using automated machine learning, the framework achieved classification accuracies of up to 92%, outperforming deep learning approaches. Sequential backward selection reduced the feature set while maintaining performance. Importantly, the framework detects transitions from healthy to stressed states in recordings that were not included in the training set. Conclusion: Overall, we provide a decision-support tool for farmers and establish a foundation for biofeedback-driven irrigation control to improve resource efficiency in (semi-)autonomous crop production systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过植物电生理学早期检测水分胁迫：用于灌溉管理的机器学习
目的：快速检测植物胁迫对植物表型分析、精准农业和自动化作物管理至关重要。尤其在灌溉管理中，早期识别水分胁迫有助于优化资源利用并维持作物性能。直接生理传感可在可见症状出现前检测胁迫反应。方法：本研究记录了温室种植番茄植株在水分胁迫下的电生理信号，并基于机器学习构建了在线胁迫检测框架。所记录的时间序列数据经处理流程处理，包括统计特征提取与选择、自动化机器学习或深度学习，以及概率校准。结果：在多个输入时间窗口中，30分钟回溯窗口在快速决策与分类性能间取得最佳平衡。使用自动化机器学习，该框架分类准确率最高达92%，优于深度学习方法。顺序后向选择可减少特征集同时保持性能。重要的是，该框架能在未参与训练集的记录中检测健康状态向胁迫状态的转变。结论：总体而言，我们为农民提供了决策支持工具，并为基于生物反馈的灌溉控制奠定了基础，以提高（半）自主作物生产系统的资源效率。
Summary / 总结
To enable timely irrigation decisions and improve resource efficiency in agriculture, this study leverages plant electrophysiology to detect water stress before visible symptoms emerge. Using time-series data from greenhouse-grown tomato plants under controlled water stress, the researchers developed a machine learning framework that combines statistical feature extraction, automated ML, and probability calibration for real-time stress classification. The system achieved up to 92% accuracy using a 30-minute look-back window, outperforming deep learning alternatives, and maintained performance even after feature reduction via sequential backward selection. Crucially, the model successfully identified stress transitions in unseen data, demonstrating generalization capability and potential for deployment in automated crop management systems.
为实现精准农业中的及时灌溉决策，本研究利用植物电生理信号在可见症状出现前检测水分胁迫。研究人员采集温室番茄植株在控水条件下的时序数据，构建了结合统计特征提取、自动化机器学习与概率校准的机器学习框架。30分钟回溯窗口在决策速度与分类精度间取得最佳平衡，自动化机器学习方法最高准确率达92%，优于深度学习方法，且能有效识别训练集外的胁迫状态转变，为半自主农业系统提供实用决策支持工具。
Exponential families from a single KL identity
Authors: Marc Dymetman
First: 2026-04-30T15:48:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:48:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Exponential families encompass the distributions central to modern machine learning -- softmax, Gaussians, and Boltzmann distributions -- and underlie the theory of variational inference, entropy-regularized reinforcement learning, and RLHF. We isolate a simple identity for exponential families that expresses the KL difference $\mathrm{KL}(q \| p_{λ_2}) - \mathrm{KL}(q \| p_{λ_1})$ in terms of the log-partition function $A(λ)$ and the moment $μ_q$. Remarkably, this identity together with the single fact that $\mathrm{KL} \geq 0$ (with equality iff $p = q$) suffices, by direct substitution and rearrangement, to derive a cluster of results that are classically obtained by separate, heavier arguments: a generalized three-point identity for arbitrary reference distributions, Pythagorean theorems for I-projections and reverse I-projections, convexity of the log-partition function, identification of its Legendre dual in KL terms, the Gibbs variational principle, and the explicit optimizer in KL-regularized reward maximization, including the exponential tilting formula underlying entropy-regularized control and RLHF. Beyond these purely algebraic consequences, standard analytic arguments recover the gradient formula for the log-partition function, the Bregman representation of within-family KL divergence, and the surjectivity of the moment map. The note is self-contained.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从单个KL恒等式出发的指数族
指数族涵盖了现代机器学习中至关重要的分布——softmax、高斯分布和玻尔兹曼分布，并构成了变分推断、熵正则化强化学习和RLHF理论的基础。我们推导出指数族的一个简单恒等式，将KL散度差 $\mathrm{KL}(q \| p_{λ_2}) - \mathrm{KL}(q \| p_{λ_1})$ 表示为对数配分函数 $A(λ)$ 和矩 $μ_q$ 的函数。令人惊讶的是，仅凭这一恒等式与KL散度非负性（当且仅当 $p = q$ 时取等）这一基本事实，通过直接代入和重排，即可推导出一系列经典上需独立、复杂论证才能得到的结果：适用于任意参考分布的广义三点恒等式、I投影与逆I投影的毕达哥拉斯定理、对数配分函数的凸性、其Legendre对偶在KL意义下的识别、吉布斯变分原理，以及KL正则化奖励最大化中的显式最优解，包括支撑熵正则化控制和RLHF的指数倾斜公式。除这些纯代数推论外，标准分析论证还可恢复对数配分函数的梯度公式、族内KL散度的Bregman表示，以及矩映射的满射性。本文自洽完整。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the centrality of exponential families in machine learning and variational inference, the paper derives a single KL identity expressing the difference between two KL divergences in terms of the log-partition function and moments. This identity, combined with the basic property that KL divergence is non-negative, enables direct derivation of multiple classical results including the three-point identity, Pythagorean theorems for I-projections, convexity of the log-partition function, its Legendre dual, the Gibbs variational principle, and the exponential tilting formula used in RLHF and entropy-regularized control. The approach is algebraic and self-contained, bypassing traditional proofs and recovering additional analytic results such as the gradient of the log-partition function and the surjectivity of the moment map.
为统一并简化指数族理论中的推导，该论文提出一个单一的KL恒等式，将两个KL散度之差表示为对数分区函数与矩的函数。仅利用KL散度非负性（等号成立当且仅当p=q），该恒等式即可通过代数代入与重排直接推导出多个经典结果，包括三点恒等式、I投影与逆I投影的毕达哥拉斯定理、对数分区函数的凸性、其Legendre对偶、Gibbs变分原理以及指数倾斜公式。该方法依赖代数替换与重排，无需复杂的独立证明。由于该工作为纯理论性质且自洽，无实验结果可报告，重点在于推导恒等式与性质而非实证验证。
Ease of dependency distance minimization in star-like structures
Authors: Emília Garcia-Casademont, Ramon Ferrer-i-Cancho
First: 2026-04-30T15:47:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:47:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The syntactic structure of a sentence can be represented as a tree where edges indicate syntactic dependencies between words. When that structure is a star, it has been demonstrated that the head should be placed in the middle of the linear arrangement according to the principle of syntactic dependency distance minimization. However, hubs of stars tend to be put at one of the ends, against that principle. Here we address two questions: (1) How difficult is it to minimize dependency distance? (2) Why anti dependency distance minimization effects have been found in star structures but not in path structures? The ease of optimization is determined by the shape of the optimization landscape. It was demonstrated that the landscape of star structures is quasiconvex (Ferrer-i-Cancho 2015, Language Dynamics and Change). As for (1), here we show that it is indeed convex (a particular case of quasiconvexity) both for star trees and quasistar trees and thus the distance-based optimization problem is simpler than previously believed. As for (2), we argue that (a) competing principles, rather than the difficulty of optimization, must be the actual reason for anti-dependency distance minimization effects and that (b) dependency distance minimization on star-like structures is less rewarding compared to other structures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：星形结构中依赖距离最小化的易实现性
句子的句法结构可表示为树形结构，其中边表示词间的句法依赖关系。当该结构为星形时，根据句法依赖距离最小化原则，中心词应置于线性排列的中间位置。然而，星形结构的中心词常被置于一端，违背该原则。本文探讨两个问题：(1) 最小化依赖距离有多难？(2) 为何在星形结构中观察到反依赖距离最小化效应，而在路径结构中未见？优化的易实现性取决于优化景观的形状。研究表明，星形结构的景观是拟凸的（Ferrer-i-Cancho 2015，《语言动态与变化》）。针对(1)，本文证明星形树和准星形树的景观均为凸的（拟凸性的一种特例），因此基于距离的优化问题比此前认为的更简单。针对(2)，我们认为：(a) 导致星形结构中反依赖距离最小化效应的原因应是竞争性原则，而非优化难度；(b) 相较于其他结构，星形结构中依赖距离最小化带来的收益较低。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why syntactic dependency distance minimization is often violated in star-like sentence structures, despite theoretical predictions that central placement of the head word should optimize distance. The authors analyze the optimization landscape of star and quasistar trees, demonstrating that it is convex — a stricter condition than previously assumed — making distance minimization easier than thought. They argue that the observed anti-minimization effects are not due to optimization difficulty but rather to competing structural principles, and that star-like structures offer less reward for distance minimization compared to other syntactic forms.
该研究探讨了为何星形句法结构在依赖距离最小化方面比预期更容易实现，以及为何这类结构常违背该原则。作者证明星形与近似星形树的优化景观是凸的（强于此前认为的拟凸性），说明该优化问题比预想中更易解。他们认为，星形结构中出现的反最小化现象并非因优化难度所致，而是由于其他句法原则的竞争效应更强，且星形结构在距离最小化上的收益低于其他结构如路径结构。
Shuffling-Aware Optimization for Private Vector Mean Estimation
Authors: Shun Takagi, Seng Pei Liew
First: 2026-04-30T15:47:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:47:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study $d$-dimensional unbiased mean estimation in the single-message shuffle model, where each user sends a single privatized message and the analyzer only observes the shuffled multiset of reports. While minimax-optimal mechanisms are well understood in the local differential privacy setting, the corresponding notion of optimality after shuffling has remained largely unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce the recently proposed shuffle index and use it to formulate the post-shuffling mechanism design problem as an explicit optimization problem. We then establish a minimax lower bound on the achievable mean squared error in terms of the shuffle index, which implies that mechanisms that are optimal under LDP can become suboptimal once shuffling is applied. Finally, we construct an asymptotically minimax optimal mechanism in the high privacy regime, which as a consequence achieves a privacy-utility trade-off nearly identical to that of the central Gaussian mechanism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向私有向量均值估计的洗牌感知优化
我们研究单消息洗牌模型下的 $d$ 维无偏均值估计，其中每个用户发送一条私有化消息，分析者仅观察到报告的洗牌多重集。虽然在本地差分隐私设置下，最优机制已被充分理解，但洗牌后对应的最优性概念仍鲜有探索。为填补这一空白，我们引入最近提出的洗牌索引，并将其用于将洗牌后机制设计问题形式化为显式优化问题。随后，我们建立了可实现的均方误差的最小最大下界，该下界以洗牌索引为参数，表明在洗牌后原本在LDP下最优的机制可能变得次优。最后，我们在高隐私场景下构造了一个渐近最小最大最优机制，其结果实现了与中心高斯机制几乎相同的隐私-效用权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand optimal mean estimation under shuffling in privacy-preserving systems, this work formulates the problem using the recently proposed shuffle index and casts mechanism design as an explicit optimization task. It derives a minimax lower bound on mean squared error tied to the shuffle index, revealing that LDP-optimal mechanisms can become suboptimal after shuffling. The authors then construct an asymptotically minimax optimal mechanism in the high privacy regime, achieving a privacy-utility trade-off nearly matching that of the central Gaussian mechanism.
为理解在洗牌模型下差分隐私的隐私-效用权衡，本文研究了在 d 维空间中单消息洗牌下的无偏均值估计问题。引入洗牌指数将机制设计建模为明确的优化问题，并推导出均方误差的最小最大下界，揭示了在洗牌后原本 LDP 最优机制可能变得次优。作者进一步在高隐私场景下构造了渐近最小最大最优机制，其隐私-效用权衡接近中心高斯机制。
Models Recall What They Violate: Constraint Adherence in Multi-Turn LLM Ideation
Authors: Garvin Kruthof
First: 2026-04-30T15:46:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:46:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When researchers iteratively refine ideas with large language models, do the models preserve fidelity to the original objective? We introduce DriftBench, a benchmark for evaluating constraint adherence in multi-turn LLM-assisted scientific ideation. Across 2,146 scored benchmark runs spanning seven models from five providers (including two open-weight), four interaction conditions, and 38 research briefs from 24 scientific domains, we find that iterative pressure reliably increases structural complexity and often reduces adherence to original constraints. A restatement probe reveals a dissociation between declarative recall and behavioral adherence, as models accurately restate constraints they simultaneously violate. The knows-but-violates (KBV) rate, measuring constraint non-compliance despite preserved recall, ranges from 8% to 99% across models. Structured checkpointing partially reduces KBV rates but does not close the dissociation, and complexity inflation persists. Human validation against blind raters confirms that the LLM judge under-detects constraint violations, making reported constraint adherence scores conservative. Sensitivity analyses confirm the findings are robust to temperature (0.7 vs.\ 1.0) and pressure type (novelty vs.\ rigor). We release all briefs, prompts, rubrics, transcripts, and scores as an open benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型会回忆起它们违反的内容：多轮LLM辅助科学创意中的约束遵守
当研究人员使用大语言模型迭代优化创意时，模型是否能保持对原始目标的忠实性？我们引入DriftBench，一个用于评估多轮LLM辅助科学创意中约束遵守能力的基准测试。在涵盖五个供应商（包括两个开源模型）的七种模型、四种交互条件和24个科学领域中的38份研究简报的2,146次评分基准运行中，我们发现迭代压力会可靠地增加结构复杂性，并常导致对原始约束的遵守度下降。重述探测揭示了声明性回忆与行为遵守之间的分离，即模型能准确重述约束，同时却违反这些约束。衡量“知道但违反”（KBV）率——即尽管保留了约束回忆却仍不遵守的情况——在不同模型间从8%至99%不等。结构化检查点部分降低了KBV率，但未能消除这种分离，且复杂性膨胀仍持续存在。人类盲评验证确认，LLM裁判对约束违反的检测不足，导致报告的约束遵守分数偏保守。敏感性分析证实，无论温度（0.7 vs. 1.0）或压力类型（新颖性 vs. 严谨性），结果均稳健。我们公开所有简报、提示、评分标准、转录本和评分，作为开放基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether large language models maintain constraint adherence during iterative ideation, the study introduces DriftBench, a benchmark evaluating multi-turn LLM behavior across 2,146 runs involving seven models, 38 research briefs, and four interaction conditions. Results show that iterative refinement increases structural complexity while often reducing constraint compliance, with a significant dissociation between declarative recall and behavioral adherence: models frequently restate constraints they violate. The 'knows-but-violates' (KBV) rate, measuring constraint non-compliance despite correct recall, varies from 8% to 99% across models. Structured checkpointing mitigates KBV slightly but does not eliminate the dissociation or complexity inflation. Human validation confirms LLMs under-detect violations, leading to overly conservative adherence scores, and sensitivity tests show robustness across temperature and pressure types.
为评估大语言模型在多轮创意迭代中是否保持约束一致性，研究构建了DriftBench基准，涵盖2146次运行、七种模型及38个科学领域简报。结果表明，迭代过程显著增加结构复杂性并降低约束遵守率，‘知道却违反’（KBV）比例在8%至99%之间，说明模型常能准确复述约束却同时违反。结构化检查点仅部分缓解KBV，且人工验证显示LLM裁判低估违规行为，导致报告的遵守率偏保守。结果在温度与压力类型上稳健成立。
MIFair: A Mutual-Information Framework for Intersectionality and Multiclass Fairness
Authors: Jeanne Monnier, Thomas George, Frédéric Guyard, Christèle Tarnec, Marios Kountouris
First: 2026-04-30T15:46:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:46:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fairness in machine learning remains challenging due to its ethical complexity, the absence of a universal definition, and the need for context-specific bias metrics. Existing methods still struggle with intersectionality, multiclass settings, and limited flexibility and generality. To address these gaps, we introduce MIFair, a unified framework for bias assessment and mitigation based on mutual information. MIFair provides a flexible metric template and an in-processing mitigation method inspired by the Prejudice Remover, defining group fairness as statistical independence between prediction-derived variables and sensitive attributes. We further strengthen its information-theoretic foundation by establishing equivalences with widely used fairness notions such as independence and separation. MIFair naturally supports intersectionality, complex subgroup structures, and multiclass classification and employs regularization-based training to reduce bias according to the selected metric. Its key advantage is its versatility: it consolidates diverse fairness requirements into a single coherent framework, enabling consistent benchmarking and simplifying practical use. Experiments on real-world tabular and image datasets show that MIFair effectively reduces bias, including previously unaddressed multi-attribute scenarios, while maintaining strong predictive performance across the evaluated settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MIFair：一种用于交叉性和多类公平性的互信息框架
机器学习中的公平性仍因伦理复杂性、缺乏统一定义及需情境特定的偏见度量而具挑战性。现有方法在处理交叉性、多类设置及灵活性与通用性方面仍显不足。为弥补这些不足，我们提出MIFair，一种基于互信息的统一框架，用于偏见评估与缓解。MIFair提供灵活的度量模板及受Prejudice Remover启发的处理中缓解方法，将群体公平性定义为预测衍生变量与敏感属性间的统计独立性。我们进一步通过建立其与常用公平概念（如独立性与分离性）的等价关系，强化其信息论基础。MIFair天然支持交叉性、复杂子群体结构及多类分类，并通过正则化训练依据选定度量减少偏见。其核心优势在于通用性：将多样公平需求整合为单一连贯框架，实现一致基准测试并简化实际应用。在真实世界表格与图像数据集上的实验表明，MIFair有效降低偏见（包括此前未被处理的多属性场景），同时在评估设置中保持强预测性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the ethical complexity and lack of universal definitions in machine learning fairness, MIFair introduces a mutual-information-based framework to unify bias assessment and mitigation, particularly addressing intersectionality and multiclass settings. It defines group fairness as statistical independence between predictions and sensitive attributes, leveraging regularization during training to reduce bias according to user-selected metrics. Experiments on real-world tabular and image datasets demonstrate that MIFair effectively reduces bias across multi-attribute and complex subgroup scenarios while preserving predictive performance.
为应对公平性定义不统一、难以处理交叉性与多分类偏见的问题，MIFair提出基于互信息的统一框架，将群体公平性定义为预测变量与敏感属性之间的统计独立性。该框架采用灵活的度量模板和正则化内处理缓解方法，借鉴Prejudice Remover思路，并形式化关联独立性与分离性等经典公平性概念。实验表明，MIFair在复杂多属性场景下有效降低偏见，同时保持预测性能稳定。
NanoKnow: How to Know What Your Language Model Knows
Authors: Lingwei Gu, Nour Jedidi, Jimmy Lin
Venue: SIGIR 2026
First: 2026-02-23T18:37:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:46:03+00:00
Comments: SIGIR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
How do large language models (LLMs) know what they know? Answering this question has been difficult because pre-training data is often a "black box" - unknown or inaccessible. The recent release of nanochat - a family of small LLMs with fully open pre-training data - addresses this as it provides a transparent view into where a model's parametric knowledge comes from. Towards the goal of understanding how knowledge is encoded by LLMs, we release NanoKnow, a benchmark dataset that partitions questions from Natural Questions and SQuAD into splits based on whether their answers are present in nanochat's pre-training corpus. Using these splits, we can now properly disentangle the sources of knowledge that LLMs rely on when producing an output. To demonstrate NanoKnow's utility, we conduct experiments using eight nanochat checkpoints. Our findings show: (1) closed-book accuracy is strongly influenced by answer frequency in the pre-training data, (2) providing external evidence can mitigate this frequency dependence, (3) even with external evidence, models are more accurate when answers were seen during pre-training, demonstrating that parametric and external knowledge are complementary, and (4) non-relevant information is harmful, with accuracy decreasing based on both the position and the number of non-relevant contexts. We release all NanoKnow artifacts at https://github.com/castorini/NanoKnow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NanoKnow：如何了解你的语言模型知道什么
大型语言模型（LLM）如何知道它们所知道的内容？回答这一问题一直困难，因为预训练数据往往是“黑箱”——未知或不可访问。近期发布的nanochat——一组具有完全开放预训练数据的小型LLM——解决了这一问题，它提供了透明视角，揭示模型参数知识的来源。为理解LLM如何编码知识，我们发布NanoKnow基准数据集，将Natural Questions和SQuAD中的问题按其答案是否存在于nanochat预训练语料库中进行划分。利用这些划分，我们现在可以正确分离LLM生成输出时所依赖的知识来源。为展示NanoKnow的实用性，我们使用八个nanochat检查点进行实验。我们的发现表明：（1）闭卷准确率强烈受预训练数据中答案频率的影响；（2）提供外部证据可缓解这种频率依赖性；（3）即使有外部证据，模型在预训练时见过答案时仍更准确，表明参数化知识与外部知识互补；（4）非相关信息有害，准确率随非相关上下文的位置和数量增加而下降。我们将在https://github.com/castorini/NanoKnow发布所有NanoKnow相关材料。
Summary / 总结
Understanding how large language models acquire and retrieve knowledge has been hindered by the opacity of their pre-training data. To address this, NanoKnow leverages the nanochat family of small LLMs, whose pre-training corpora are fully open, to create a benchmark that splits questions based on whether their answers appear in the training data. By evaluating eight nanochat checkpoints on these splits, the study finds that model accuracy is heavily influenced by answer frequency in pre-training, which can be mitigated by external evidence, yet parametric knowledge still remains more reliable. Additionally, the presence of non-relevant context reduces accuracy, regardless of its position or quantity, highlighting the importance of clean, relevant input for model performance.
由于预训练数据通常不可见，理解大语言模型如何获取和调用知识一直困难重重。NanoKnow 利用预训练数据完全公开的 nanochat 小型语言模型，将自然问题与 SQuAD 中的问题按答案是否出现在预训练语料中进行划分，构建了一个基准测试。在八个 nanochat 检查点上的实验表明，模型准确率高度依赖训练数据中答案的出现频率，但外部证据可缓解这一依赖；即使有外部支持，模型在预训练中见过答案时仍表现更优，说明参数知识与外部知识互补；此外，无关上下文会降低准确率，且其负面影响随位置和数量增加而加剧。
Constrained Policy Optimization with Cantelli-Bounded Value-at-Risk
Authors: Rohan Tangri, Jan-Peter Calliess
First: 2026-01-30T13:57:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:45:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the Value-at-Risk Constrained Policy Optimization algorithm (VaR-CPO), a sample efficient and conservative method designed to optimize Value-at-Risk (VaR) constrained reinforcement learning (RL) problems. Empirically, we demonstrate that VaR-CPO is capable of safe exploration, achieving zero constraint violations during training in feasible environments, a critical property that baseline methods fail to uphold. To overcome the inherent non-differentiability of the VaR constraint, we employ Cantelli's inequality to obtain a tractable approximation based on the first two moments of the cost return. Additionally, by extending the trust-region framework of the Constrained Policy Optimization (CPO) method, we provide worst-case bounds for both policy improvement and constraint violation during the training process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Cantelli界值在风险价值约束下的策略优化
我们提出了价值在险约束策略优化算法（VaR-CPO），这是一种样本效率高且保守的方法，旨在优化价值在险（VaR）约束的强化学习问题。实证表明，VaR-CPO能够实现安全探索，在可行环境中训练期间无约束违反，这是基线方法无法实现的关键特性。为克服VaR约束的固有不可微性，我们采用Cantelli不等式，基于成本回报的一阶和二阶矩获得可处理的近似解。此外，通过扩展约束策略优化（CPO）方法的信任域框架，我们为训练过程中策略改进和约束违反提供了最坏情况的界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe and sample-efficient reinforcement learning under Value-at-Risk constraints, this paper proposes VaR-CPO, a method that leverages Cantelli’s inequality to approximate the non-differentiable VaR constraint using first and second moments of cost returns. By integrating this approximation into a trust-region framework adapted from CPO, the algorithm ensures worst-case bounds on both policy improvement and constraint violation. Experimental results show that VaR-CPO achieves zero constraint violations during training in feasible environments, outperforming baseline methods that often violate constraints.
为实现受VaR约束下的安全且样本高效的强化学习，本文提出VaR-CPO算法，利用Cantelli不等式将不可微的VaR约束转化为基于一阶与二阶矩的可计算近似，并将其嵌入信任域框架以保证策略改进与约束违反的最坏情况界。实验表明，VaR-CPO在可行环境中训练期间实现零约束违反，优于常出现约束违规的基线方法。
